        dist[src] = 0;
        PriorityQueue<int[]> pq = new PriorityQueue<>((a, b) -> Integer.compare(a[1], b[1])); // {node, dist}
        pq.add(new int[]{src, 0});
        while (!pq.isEmpty()) {
            int[] cur = pq.poll();
            int u = cur[0];
            if (cur[1] > dist[u]) continue;
            if (u == dst) break;
            for (int[] e : adj[u]) {
                int v = e[0], nd = dist[u] + e[1];
                if (nd < dist[v]) {
                    dist[v] = nd;
                    prev[v] = u;
                    pq.add(new int[]{v, nd});
                }
            }
        }
        if (dist[dst] == Integer.MAX_VALUE) return Collections.emptyList();
        LinkedList<Integer> path = new LinkedList<>();
        for (int v = dst; v != -1; v = prev[v]) path.addFirst(v);
        return path;
    }

    // ---------- Count shortest paths: Number of Ways to Arrive at Destination (LC 1976) ----------
    // ways[v] = ways[u] on strict improvement, += ways[u] on a tie. Needs strictly positive weights.
    static int countShortestPaths(int n, List<int[]>[] adj, int src, int dst) {
        final int MOD = 1_000_000_007;
        long[] dist = new long[n];
        Arrays.fill(dist, Long.MAX_VALUE);
        long[] ways = new long[n];
        dist[src] = 0;
        ways[src] = 1;
        PriorityQueue<long[]> pq = new PriorityQueue<>((a, b) -> Long.compare(a[1], b[1])); // {node, dist}
        pq.add(new long[]{src, 0});
        while (!pq.isEmpty()) {
            long[] cur = pq.poll();
            int u = (int) cur[0];
            if (cur[1] > dist[u]) continue;
            for (int[] e : adj[u]) {
                int v = e[0];
                long nd = dist[u] + e[1];
                if (nd < dist[v]) {
                    dist[v] = nd;
                    ways[v] = ways[u];
                    pq.add(new long[]{v, nd});
                } else if (nd == dist[v]) {
                    ways[v] = (ways[v] + ways[u]) % MOD;
                }
            }
        }
        return (int) ways[dst];
    }

    // ---------- BFS over (cell, removalsUsed): Shortest Path with Obstacle Elimination (LC 1293) ----------
    static int shortestPathWithObstacleElimination(int[][] grid, int k) {
        int m = grid.length, n = grid[0].length;
        if (k >= m + n - 2) return m + n - 2; // can walk straight through everything
        boolean[][][] seen = new boolean[m][n][k + 1];
        Queue<int[]> q = new ArrayDeque<>(); // {r, c, used}
        q.add(new int[]{0, 0, 0});
        seen[0][0][0] = true;
        int steps = 0;
        while (!q.isEmpty()) {
            for (int sz = q.size(); sz > 0; sz--) {
                int[] cur = q.poll();
                if (cur[0] == m - 1 && cur[1] == n - 1) return steps;
                for (int[] d : DIRS4) {
                    int nr = cur[0] + d[0], nc = cur[1] + d[1];
                    if (nr < 0 || nr >= m || nc < 0 || nc >= n) continue;
                    int used = cur[2] + grid[nr][nc];
                    if (used <= k && !seen[nr][nc][used]) {
                        seen[nr][nc][used] = true;
                        q.add(new int[]{nr, nc, used});
                    }
                }
            }
            steps++;
        }
        return -1;
    }
}
12 — CYCLEANDCOURSEGRAPHS.JAVA

Cycles, Ordering & Cloning

Dependency problems reduce to one question: is there a valid order, and if so what is it? A cycle means there is none. Course Schedule and Alien Dictionary differ only in how the edges get built.

QuestionTool
Is there a cycle in a directed graph?3-color DFS. An edge into a gray node (on the current path) closes a cycle.
Which nodes can never reach a cycle?Same colors. Nodes that finish black are safe (LC 802).
Give me a valid orderKahn's. Fewer than N nodes out means a cycle.
How many rounds if independent items run in parallel?Kahn's, one queue level per round.
Order inferred from sorted data (Alien Dictionary)One edge from the first differing character of each adjacent pair, then Kahn's. Watch the prefix case.
Deep-copy a graphMap old node to new node. The map doubles as the visited set.
CycleAndCourseGraphs.java
3-color cycle detection, safe nodes, course order, parallel courses, alien dictionary, clone graph
O(V+E) 
import java.util.*;

public class CycleAndCourseGraphs {

    // ---------- Directed cycle detection: 3-color DFS ----------
    // white (0) = unvisited, gray (1) = on the current DFS path, black (2) = fully explored.
    // An edge into a gray node is a back edge, i.e. a cycle.
    static boolean hasCycleDirected(int n, List<List<Integer>> adj) {
        int[] color = new int[n];
        for (int i = 0; i < n; i++) {
            if (color[i] == 0 && dfsCycle(i, adj, color)) return true;
        }
        return false;
    }

    private static boolean dfsCycle(int u, List<List<Integer>> adj, int[] color) {
        color[u] = 1;
        for (int v : adj.get(u)) {
            if (color[v] == 1) return true;
            if (color[v] == 0 && dfsCycle(v, adj, color)) return true;
        }
        color[u] = 2;
        return false;
    }

    // ---------- Eventual Safe States (LC 802): nodes that cannot reach a cycle ----------
    static List<Integer> eventualSafeNodes(int[][] graph) {
        int n = graph.length;
        int[] color = new int[n];
        List<Integer> res = new ArrayList<>();
        for (int i = 0; i < n; i++) if (isSafe(i, graph, color)) res.add(i);
        return res;
    }

    private static boolean isSafe(int u, int[][] g, int[] color) {
        if (color[u] != 0) return color[u] == 2; // gray here means we re-entered the path: unsafe
        color[u] = 1;
        for (int v : g[u]) if (!isSafe(v, g, color)) return false;
        color[u] = 2;
        return true;
    }

    // ---------- Course Schedule I/II (LC 207/210): Kahn's. prerequisites[i] = {course, prereq} ----------
    static int[] courseOrder(int numCourses, int[][] prerequisites) {
        List<List<Integer>> adj = new ArrayList<>();
        for (int i = 0; i < numCourses; i++) adj.add(new ArrayList<>());
        int[] indeg = new int[numCourses];
        for (int[] p : prerequisites) {
            adj.get(p[1]).add(p[0]);
            indeg[p[0]]++;
        }
        Queue<Integer> q = new ArrayDeque<>();
        for (int i = 0; i < numCourses; i++) if (indeg[i] == 0) q.add(i);
        int[] order = new int[numCourses];
        int idx = 0;
        while (!q.isEmpty()) {
            int u = q.poll();
            order[idx++] = u;
            for (int v : adj.get(u)) if (--indeg[v] == 0) q.add(v);
        }
        return idx == numCourses ? order : new int[0]; // empty = cycle, impossible
    }

    // ---------- Parallel Courses (LC 1136): Kahn's by level = number of semesters ----------
    // Courses are 1..n; relations[i] = {prev, next}.
    static int minimumSemesters(int n, int[][] relations) {
        List<List<Integer>> adj = new ArrayList<>();
        for (int i = 0; i < n; i++) adj.add(new ArrayList<>());
        int[] indeg = new int[n];
        for (int[] r : relations) {
            adj.get(r[0] - 1).add(r[1] - 1);
            indeg[r[1] - 1]++;
        }
        Queue<Integer> q = new ArrayDeque<>();
        for (int i = 0; i < n; i++) if (indeg[i] == 0) q.add(i);
        int semesters = 0, taken = 0;
        while (!q.isEmpty()) {
            semesters++;
            for (int sz = q.size(); sz > 0; sz--) { // everything available this semester
                int u = q.poll();
                taken++;
                for (int v : adj.get(u)) if (--indeg[v] == 0) q.add(v);
            }
        }
        return taken == n ? semesters : -1;
    }

    // ---------- Alien Dictionary (LC 269): build the graph from adjacent words, then Kahn's ----------
    static String alienOrder(String[] words) {
        Map<Character, Set<Character>> adj = new HashMap<>();
        Map<Character, Integer> indeg = new HashMap<>();
        for (String w : words) {
            for (char c : w.toCharArray()) {
                adj.putIfAbsent(c, new HashSet<>());
                indeg.putIfAbsent(c, 0);
            }
        }
        for (int i = 0; i + 1 < words.length; i++) {
            String a = words[i], b = words[i + 1];
            if (a.length() > b.length() && a.startsWith(b)) return ""; // "abc" before "ab" is invalid
            for (int j = 0; j < Math.min(a.length(), b.length()); j++) {
                if (a.charAt(j) != b.charAt(j)) {
                    if (adj.get(a.charAt(j)).add(b.charAt(j))) indeg.merge(b.charAt(j), 1, Integer::sum);
                    break; // only the first differing char gives information
                }
            }
        }
        Queue<Character> q = new ArrayDeque<>();
        for (Map.Entry<Character, Integer> e : indeg.entrySet()) if (e.getValue() == 0) q.add(e.getKey());
        StringBuilder sb = new StringBuilder();
        while (!q.isEmpty()) {
            char c = q.poll();
            sb.append(c);
            for (char nb : adj.get(c)) if (indeg.merge(nb, -1, Integer::sum) == 0) q.add(nb);
        }
        return sb.length() == indeg.size() ? sb.toString() : ""; // shorter = cycle
    }

    // ---------- Clone Graph (LC 133): old node -> new node map doubles as the visited set ----------
    static class Node {
        int val;
        List<Node> neighbors = new ArrayList<>();
        Node(int val) { this.val = val; }
    }

    static Node cloneGraph(Node start) {
        if (start == null) return null;
        Map<Node, Node> copy = new HashMap<>();
        Queue<Node> q = new ArrayDeque<>();
        copy.put(start, new Node(start.val));
        q.add(start);
        while (!q.isEmpty()) {
            Node cur = q.poll();
            for (Node nb : cur.neighbors) {
                if (!copy.containsKey(nb)) {
                    copy.put(nb, new Node(nb.val));
                    q.add(nb);
                }
                copy.get(cur).neighbors.add(copy.get(nb));
            }
        }
        return copy.get(start);
    }
}
13 — DSUEXTENDED.JAVA

Union-Find Beyond the Basics

The DSU in section 04 answers "are these connected?". These variants carry extra information through the same two operations.

VariantExtra dataProblem
Size trackingsize[] kept on rootslargest component, live component count
Grid cellsid = r * n + cNumber of Islands II (LC 305)
String keysmap each key to an int firstAccounts Merge (LC 721)
Cycle edgeunion returns falseRedundant Connection (LC 684)
Weightedw[x] = ratio to parent, multiplied along the pathEvaluate Division (LC 399)
DSUExtended.java
Size-tracking DSU, online grid islands, accounts merge, redundant connection, weighted DSU
O(α(N)) 
import java.util.*;

public class DSUExtended {

    // ---------- DSU tracking component sizes (iterative find = no recursion) ----------
    static class DSUSize {
        int[] parent, size;
        int components, maxSize = 1;

        DSUSize(int n) {
            parent = new int[n];
            size = new int[n];
            components = n;
            for (int i = 0; i < n; i++) { parent[i] = i; size[i] = 1; }
        }

        int find(int x) { // path halving
            while (parent[x] != x) {
                parent[x] = parent[parent[x]];
                x = parent[x];
            }
            return x;
        }

        boolean union(int a, int b) {
            int ra = find(a), rb = find(b);
            if (ra == rb) return false;
            if (size[ra] < size[rb]) { int t = ra; ra = rb; rb = t; }
            parent[rb] = ra;
            size[ra] += size[rb];
            maxSize = Math.max(maxSize, size[ra]);
            components--;
            return true;
        }

        int sizeOf(int x) { return size[find(x)]; }
    }

    // ---------- DSU on a grid, online: Number of Islands II (LC 305) ----------
    // Cell (r, c) -> id r*n + c. Each new land cell adds 1 island, each successful union removes 1.
    static List<Integer> numIslands2(int m, int n, int[][] positions) {
        DSUSize dsu = new DSUSize(m * n);
        boolean[] land = new boolean[m * n];
        int[][] dirs = {{1, 0}, {-1, 0}, {0, 1}, {0, -1}};
        int islands = 0;
        List<Integer> res = new ArrayList<>();
        for (int[] p : positions) {
            int id = p[0] * n + p[1];
            if (!land[id]) {
                land[id] = true;
                islands++;
                for (int[] d : dirs) {
                    int nr = p[0] + d[0], nc = p[1] + d[1];
                    if (nr < 0 || nr >= m || nc < 0 || nc >= n) continue;
                    int nid = nr * n + nc;
                    if (land[nid] && dsu.union(id, nid)) islands--;
                }
            }
            res.add(islands);
        }
        return res;
    }

    // ---------- String keys: Accounts Merge (LC 721) ----------
    // DSU over account indices; the first account that owns an email becomes its representative.
    static List<List<String>> accountsMerge(List<List<String>> accounts) {
        int n = accounts.size();
        DSUSize dsu = new DSUSize(n);
        Map<String, Integer> emailOwner = new HashMap<>();
        for (int i = 0; i < n; i++) {
            for (int j = 1; j < accounts.get(i).size(); j++) {
                Integer prev = emailOwner.putIfAbsent(accounts.get(i).get(j), i);
                if (prev != null) dsu.union(i, prev);
            }
        }
        Map<Integer, TreeSet<String>> groups = new HashMap<>();
        for (Map.Entry<String, Integer> en : emailOwner.entrySet()) {
            groups.computeIfAbsent(dsu.find(en.getValue()), k -> new TreeSet<>()).add(en.getKey());
        }
        List<List<String>> res = new ArrayList<>();
        for (Map.Entry<Integer, TreeSet<String>> g : groups.entrySet()) {
            List<String> row = new ArrayList<>();
            row.add(accounts.get(g.getKey()).get(0)); // name
            row.addAll(g.getValue());                 // sorted emails
            res.add(row);
        }
        return res;
    }

    // ---------- Redundant Connection (LC 684): the edge whose union fails closes the cycle ----------
    static int[] findRedundantConnection(int[][] edges) {
        DSUSize dsu = new DSUSize(edges.length + 1);
        for (int[] e : edges) if (!dsu.union(e[0], e[1])) return e;
        return new int[0];
    }

    // ---------- Weighted DSU: Evaluate Division (LC 399) ----------
    // w[x] = value(x) / value(parent[x]); after path compression w[x] = value(x) / value(root).
    static class WeightedDSU {
        int[] parent;
        double[] w;

        WeightedDSU(int n) {
            parent = new int[n];
            w = new double[n];
            for (int i = 0; i < n; i++) { parent[i] = i; w[i] = 1.0; }
        }

        int find(int x) {
            if (parent[x] != x) {
                int p = parent[x];
                parent[x] = find(p);
                w[x] *= w[p]; // w[p] is now value(p)/value(root)
            }
            return parent[x];
        }

        // Records value(a) / value(b) = ratio.
        void union(int a, int b, double ratio) {
            int ra = find(a), rb = find(b);
            if (ra == rb) return;
            parent[ra] = rb;
            w[ra] = ratio * w[b] / w[a];
        }

        // value(a) / value(b), or -1 if the two are not connected.
        double query(int a, int b) {
            if (find(a) != find(b)) return -1.0;
            return w[a] / w[b];
        }
    }

    static double[] calcEquation(List<List<String>> equations, double[] values, List<List<String>> queries) {
        Map<String, Integer> id = new HashMap<>();
        for (List<String> eq : equations) for (String s : eq) id.putIfAbsent(s, id.size());
        WeightedDSU dsu = new WeightedDSU(id.size());
        for (int i = 0; i < equations.size(); i++) {
            dsu.union(id.get(equations.get(i).get(0)), id.get(equations.get(i).get(1)), values[i]);
        }
        double[] res = new double[queries.size()];
        for (int i = 0; i < queries.size(); i++) {
            String a = queries.get(i).get(0), b = queries.get(i).get(1);
            res[i] = (id.containsKey(a) && id.containsKey(b)) ? dsu.query(id.get(a), id.get(b)) : -1.0;
        }
        return res;
    }

    // ---------- Path With Minimum Effort (LC 1631), DSU form: sort cells' edges by weight, union until start ~ end ----------
    // Same answer as the Dijkstra variant in DijkstraVariants; use this when the question is "smallest bottleneck".
    static int minimumEffortPathDSU(int[][] h) {
        int m = h.length, n = h[0].length;
        if (m * n == 1) return 0;
        List<int[]> edges = new ArrayList<>(); // {weight, a, b}
        for (int i = 0; i < m; i++)
            for (int j = 0; j < n; j++) {
                if (i + 1 < m) edges.add(new int[]{Math.abs(h[i][j] - h[i + 1][j]), i * n + j, (i + 1) * n + j});
                if (j + 1 < n) edges.add(new int[]{Math.abs(h[i][j] - h[i][j + 1]), i * n + j, i * n + j + 1});
            }
        edges.sort((a, b) -> a[0] - b[0]);
        int[] parent = new int[m * n];
        for (int i = 0; i < parent.length; i++) parent[i] = i;
        for (int[] e : edges) {
            int a = root(parent, e[1]), b = root(parent, e[2]);
            if (a != b) parent[a] = b;
            if (root(parent, 0) == root(parent, m * n - 1)) return e[0];
        }
        return 0;
    }

    private static int root(int[] p, int x) {
        while (p[x] != x) { p[x] = p[p[x]]; x = p[x]; }
        return x;
    }

    // ---------- Evaluate Division (LC 399), DFS form: build a weighted graph, answer each query with a search ----------
    static double[] calcEquationDFS(List<List<String>> eq, double[] vals, List<List<String>> queries) {
        Map<String, Map<String, Double>> g = new HashMap<>();
        for (int i = 0; i < eq.size(); i++) {
            String a = eq.get(i).get(0), b = eq.get(i).get(1);
            g.computeIfAbsent(a, k -> new HashMap<>()).put(b, vals[i]);
            g.computeIfAbsent(b, k -> new HashMap<>()).put(a, 1.0 / vals[i]);
        }
        double[] res = new double[queries.size()];
        for (int i = 0; i < res.length; i++) {
            String a = queries.get(i).get(0), b = queries.get(i).get(1);
            res[i] = !g.containsKey(a) || !g.containsKey(b) ? -1.0 : evalDfs(g, a, b, new HashSet<>());
        }
        return res;
    }

    private static double evalDfs(Map<String, Map<String, Double>> g, String cur, String target, Set<String> seen) {
        if (cur.equals(target)) return 1.0;
        seen.add(cur);
        for (Map.Entry<String, Double> e : g.get(cur).entrySet()) {
            if (seen.contains(e.getKey())) continue;
            double sub = evalDfs(g, e.getKey(), target, seen);
            if (sub >= 0) return e.getValue() * sub;
        }
        return -1.0;
    }
}
14 — TREEGRAPHS.JAVA

Tree Algorithms

A tree is a connected graph with N-1 edges, so everything above applies. These are the tree-specific tools that come up on their own: the farthest pair, ancestors, an answer for every possible root, and centers. All of them build iteratively, so deep trees don't overflow the stack.

NeedToolComplexity
DiameterBFS from any node, then BFS from the farthest oneO(N)
LCA, k-th ancestor, distance between nodesbinary liftingO(N log N) build, O(log N) query
An answer for every node as rootrerooting: bottom-up counts, then a top-down adjustmentO(N)
Root that minimizes heightpeel leaves until 1 or 2 nodes remainO(N)
TreeGraphs.java
Diameter, LCA with binary lifting, k-th ancestor, rerooting, minimum height trees
O(N log N) 
import java.util.*;

public class TreeGraphs {

    // ---------- Tree diameter (LC 1245): BFS from anywhere, then BFS from the farthest node ----------
    static int treeDiameter(List<List<Integer>> adj) {
        int[] d0 = bfsDist(0, adj);
        int far = argMax(d0);
        int[] d1 = bfsDist(far, adj);
        return d1[argMax(d1)]; // in edges
    }

    static int[] bfsDist(int src, List<List<Integer>> adj) {
        int[] dist = new int[adj.size()];
        Arrays.fill(dist, -1);
        Queue<Integer> q = new ArrayDeque<>();
        dist[src] = 0;
        q.add(src);
        while (!q.isEmpty()) {
            int u = q.poll();
            for (int v : adj.get(u)) {
                if (dist[v] == -1) {
                    dist[v] = dist[u] + 1;
                    q.add(v);
                }
            }
        }
        return dist;
    }

    private static int argMax(int[] a) {
        int best = 0;
        for (int i = 1; i < a.length; i++) if (a[i] > a[best]) best = i;
        return best;
    }

    // ---------- LCA + k-th ancestor via binary lifting (LC 236 / 1483) ----------
    // up[j][v] = 2^j-th ancestor of v. Build O(N log N), each query O(log N). BFS-built: no recursion.
    static class LCA {
        final int LOG;
        final int[][] up;
        final int[] depth;

        LCA(int n, List<List<Integer>> adj, int root) {
            int lg = 1;
            while ((1 << lg) < n) lg++;
            LOG = lg;
            up = new int[LOG][n];
            depth = new int[n];
            boolean[] seen = new boolean[n];
            Queue<Integer> q = new ArrayDeque<>();
            q.add(root);
            seen[root] = true;
            up[0][root] = root; // root's ancestor is itself
            while (!q.isEmpty()) {
                int u = q.poll();
                for (int v : adj.get(u)) {
                    if (!seen[v]) {
                        seen[v] = true;
                        depth[v] = depth[u] + 1;
                        up[0][v] = u;
                        q.add(v);
                    }
                }
            }
            for (int j = 1; j < LOG; j++)
                for (int v = 0; v < n; v++)
                    up[j][v] = up[j - 1][up[j - 1][v]];
        }

        int kthAncestor(int v, int k) {
            if (k > depth[v]) return -1;
            for (int j = 0; j < LOG; j++) if (((k >> j) & 1) == 1) v = up[j][v];
            return v;
        }

        int lca(int a, int b) {
            if (depth[a] < depth[b]) { int t = a; a = b; b = t; }
            a = kthAncestor(a, depth[a] - depth[b]); // lift deeper node to the same depth
            if (a == b) return a;
            for (int j = LOG - 1; j >= 0; j--) {
                if (up[j][a] != up[j][b]) { a = up[j][a]; b = up[j][b]; }
            }
            return up[0][a];
        }

        int dist(int a, int b) { return depth[a] + depth[b] - 2 * depth[lca(a, b)]; }
    }

    // ---------- Rerooting: Sum of Distances in Tree (LC 834) ----------
    // Pass 1 (bottom-up): cnt[v] = subtree size, res[v] = sum of distances to nodes in v's subtree.
    // Pass 2 (top-down): moving the root from p to child v brings cnt[v] nodes 1 closer, the other n-cnt[v] 1 farther.
    static int[] sumOfDistancesInTree(int n, int[][] edges) {
        List<List<Integer>> adj = new ArrayList<>();
        for (int i = 0; i < n; i++) adj.add(new ArrayList<>());
        for (int[] e : edges) { adj.get(e[0]).add(e[1]); adj.get(e[1]).add(e[0]); }

        int[] parent = new int[n], order = new int[n];
        Arrays.fill(parent, -1);
        int head = 0, tail = 0;
        boolean[] seen = new boolean[n];
        order[tail++] = 0;
        seen[0] = true;
        while (head < tail) { // BFS order doubles as a traversal order for both passes
            int u = order[head++];
            for (int v : adj.get(u)) {
                if (!seen[v]) { seen[v] = true; parent[v] = u; order[tail++] = v; }
            }
        }

        long[] cnt = new long[n], res = new long[n];
        for (int i = n - 1; i >= 0; i--) { // children before parents
            int v = order[i];
            cnt[v] += 1;
            if (parent[v] != -1) {
                cnt[parent[v]] += cnt[v];
                res[parent[v]] += res[v] + cnt[v];
            }
        }
        for (int i = 1; i < n; i++) { // parents before children
            int v = order[i], p = parent[v];
            res[v] = res[p] - cnt[v] + (n - cnt[v]);
        }
        int[] out = new int[n];
        for (int i = 0; i < n; i++) out[i] = (int) res[i];
        return out;
    }

    // ---------- Minimum Height Trees (LC 310): peel leaves layer by layer; the last 1-2 nodes are the centers ----------
    static List<Integer> minHeightTrees(int n, int[][] edges) {
        if (n == 1) return Collections.singletonList(0);
        List<Set<Integer>> adj = new ArrayList<>();
        for (int i = 0; i < n; i++) adj.add(new HashSet<>());
        for (int[] e : edges) { adj.get(e[0]).add(e[1]); adj.get(e[1]).add(e[0]); }

        List<Integer> leaves = new ArrayList<>();
        for (int i = 0; i < n; i++) if (adj.get(i).size() == 1) leaves.add(i);
        int remaining = n;
        while (remaining > 2) {
            remaining -= leaves.size();
            List<Integer> next = new ArrayList<>();
            for (int leaf : leaves) {
                int nb = adj.get(leaf).iterator().next();
                adj.get(nb).remove(leaf);
                if (adj.get(nb).size() == 1) next.add(nb);
            }
            leaves = next;
        }
        return leaves;
    }
}
15 — MATCHINGCOLORINGFUNCTIONAL.JAVA

Matching, Coloring & Functional Graphs

Three small patterns that don't fit elsewhere. Kuhn's is the matching algorithm you can actually write in 20 minutes. A functional graph is any structure where each node has exactly one outgoing edge, such as a next-pointer array.

PatternIdeaComplexity
Kuhn's matchingaugmenting DFS from each left node; Hopcroft-Karp (section 08) only for large inputsO(V·E)
k-coloringbacktrack node by node, reject a color a neighbor already has (k = 2 is the bipartite check)exponential
Floyd's cycle detectiontortoise and hare on next[]; a second pass from the start finds the cycle entryO(N), O(1) space
Longest cycle, functional graphtimestamp each walk; reaching a stamp from the same walk closes a cycle of length (time - stamp)O(N)
MatchingColoringFunctional.java
Kuhn's matching, k-coloring, Floyd's cycle detection, longest cycle in a functional graph
varies 
import java.util.*;

public class MatchingColoringFunctional {

    // ---------- Kuhn's algorithm: bipartite matching by repeated augmenting DFS ----------
    // O(V*E). Short enough to write live; reach for Hopcroft-Karp (08_NetworkFlow) only on large inputs.
    // adj.get(u) = right-side nodes that left node u can match.
    static int kuhnMatching(int left, int right, List<List<Integer>> adj) {
        int[] matchRight = new int[right];
        Arrays.fill(matchRight, -1);
        int matching = 0;
        for (int u = 0; u < left; u++) {
            if (tryAugment(u, adj, new boolean[right], matchRight)) matching++;
        }
        return matching;
    }

    private static boolean tryAugment(int u, List<List<Integer>> adj, boolean[] seen, int[] matchRight) {
        for (int v : adj.get(u)) {
            if (seen[v]) continue;
            seen[v] = true;
            // v is free, or its current partner can be re-routed to another right node
            if (matchRight[v] == -1 || tryAugment(matchRight[v], adj, seen, matchRight)) {
                matchRight[v] = u;
                return true;
            }
        }
        return false;
    }

    // ---------- k-coloring by backtracking (NP-hard in general; interview versions have small k / small n) ----------
    static boolean kColorable(List<List<Integer>> adj, int k) {
        return colorFrom(0, adj, k, new int[adj.size()]);
    }

    private static boolean colorFrom(int u, List<List<Integer>> adj, int k, int[] color) {
        if (u == adj.size()) return true;
        for (int c = 1; c <= k; c++) {
            boolean ok = true;
            for (int v : adj.get(u)) {
                if (color[v] == c) { ok = false; break; }
            }
            if (!ok) continue;
            color[u] = c;
            if (colorFrom(u + 1, adj, k, color)) return true;
            color[u] = 0; // backtrack
        }
        return false;
    }

    // ---------- Functional graph (every node has exactly one outgoing edge) ----------
    // Floyd's tortoise and hare: Find the Duplicate Number (LC 287). nums[i] is the "next" pointer of i.
    static int findDuplicate(int[] nums) {
        int slow = nums[0], fast = nums[0];
        do {
            slow = nums[slow];
            fast = nums[nums[fast]];
        } while (slow != fast);
        slow = nums[0];
        while (slow != fast) { // phase 2: distance from start to cycle entry equals distance from meeting point to entry
            slow = nums[slow];
            fast = nums[fast];
        }
        return slow;
    }

    // Same idea on a next[] array where -1 means "no next". Returns the first node on the cycle, or -1.
    static int cycleStart(int[] next, int start) {
        int slow = start, fast = start;
        while (true) {
            if (fast == -1 || next[fast] == -1) return -1;
            slow = next[slow];
            fast = next[next[fast]];
            if (fast == -1) return -1;
            if (slow == fast) break;
        }
        slow = start;
        while (slow != fast) {
            slow = next[slow];
            fast = next[fast];
        }
        return slow;
    }

    // ---------- Longest Cycle in a Graph (LC 2360): timestamp walk, O(N) ----------
    // Walk from each unvisited node; hitting a node stamped during THIS walk closes a cycle of length (time - stamp).
    static int longestCycle(int[] edges) {
        int n = edges.length;
        int[] stamp = new int[n]; // 0 = unvisited
        int time = 1, best = -1;
        for (int i = 0; i < n; i++) {
            if (stamp[i] != 0) continue;
            int walkStart = time;
            int u = i;
            while (u != -1 && stamp[u] == 0) {
                stamp[u] = time++;
                u = edges[u];
            }
            if (u != -1 && stamp[u] >= walkStart) best = Math.max(best, time - stamp[u]);
        }
        return best;
    }
}
16 — DPLINEAR.JAVA

Linear DP & State Machines

Run the six-step framework on every problem: state, choice, transition, base, order, shrink. One climbing-stairs problem is written three ways (memo, table, O(1)) so you can move between them on demand.

PatternUse caseComplexity
1D recurrenceCount ways or min cost where step i reads a few earlier stepsO(n)
Take / skipHouse robber familyO(n)
KadaneBest subarray ending here (track max and min for products)O(n)
Stock state machineHolding / not holding, transactions used, cooldown, feeO(nk)
DPLinear.java
Climbing stairs x3, rob, decode ways, Kadane, max product, stock I-IV + cooldown + fee
O(n) 
import java.util.*;

public class DPLinear {

    // DP FRAMEWORK (run it on every problem)
    //   1. State      what is the least I must know to decide the future? (dp[i], dp[i][j], dp[mask])
    //   2. Choice     what can I do from this state?
    //   3. Transition dp[state] = best over choices of (choice cost + dp[next state])
    //   4. Base       states whose answer needs no recursion
    //   5. Order      evaluate so every dependency is ready (memo: automatic; table: loop direction)
    //   6. Shrink     keep only the rows or values later states still read

    // ---------- Climbing Stairs (LC 70): one problem, three forms ----------
    // RECOGNIZE: count ways / min cost where step i depends on a few earlier steps.
    static int climbStairsMemo(int n) { return climb(n, new int[n + 1]); }

    private static int climb(int n, int[] memo) {
        if (n <= 1) return 1;
        if (memo[n] != 0) return memo[n];
        return memo[n] = climb(n - 1, memo) + climb(n - 2, memo);
    }

    static int climbStairsTab(int n) {
        int[] dp = new int[n + 2];
        dp[0] = 1; dp[1] = 1;
        for (int i = 2; i <= n; i++) dp[i] = dp[i - 1] + dp[i - 2];
        return dp[n];
    }

    static int climbStairsO1(int n) {
        int prev = 1, cur = 1;
        for (int i = 2; i <= n; i++) { int next = prev + cur; prev = cur; cur = next; }
        return cur;
    }

    // ---------- House Robber (LC 198): take / skip state ----------
    static int rob(int[] nums) {
        int take = 0, skip = 0; // best total if the previous house was robbed / not robbed
        for (int x : nums) {
            int newTake = skip + x;
            skip = Math.max(skip, take);
            take = newTake;
        }
        return Math.max(take, skip);
    }

    // House Robber II (LC 213): circle = best of "drop first" and "drop last"
    static int robCircular(int[] nums) {
        int n = nums.length;
        if (n == 1) return nums[0];
        return Math.max(robRange(nums, 0, n - 2), robRange(nums, 1, n - 1));
    }

    private static int robRange(int[] a, int lo, int hi) {
        int take = 0, skip = 0;
        for (int i = lo; i <= hi; i++) {
            int newTake = skip + a[i];
            skip = Math.max(skip, take);
            take = newTake;
        }
        return Math.max(take, skip);
    }

    // ---------- Decode Ways (LC 91): dp over prefix length, 1- and 2-digit choices ----------
    static int numDecodings(String s) {
        int n = s.length();
        int prev2 = 1, prev1 = s.charAt(0) == '0' ? 0 : 1; // ways for prefix lengths i-2 and i-1
        for (int i = 2; i <= n; i++) {
            int cur = 0;
            if (s.charAt(i - 1) != '0') cur += prev1;
            int two = Integer.parseInt(s.substring(i - 2, i));
            if (two >= 10 && two <= 26) cur += prev2;
            prev2 = prev1;
            prev1 = cur;
        }
        return prev1;
    }

    // ---------- Kadane (LC 53): best subarray ending here ----------
    static int maxSubArray(int[] nums) {
        int best = nums[0], cur = nums[0];
        for (int i = 1; i < nums.length; i++) {
            cur = Math.max(nums[i], cur + nums[i]);
            best = Math.max(best, cur);
        }
        return best;
    }

    // ---------- Maximum Product Subarray (LC 152): track max AND min (negatives flip them) ----------
    static int maxProduct(int[] nums) {
        int hi = nums[0], lo = nums[0], best = nums[0];
        for (int i = 1; i < nums.length; i++) {
            int x = nums[i];
            if (x < 0) { int t = hi; hi = lo; lo = t; }
            hi = Math.max(x, hi * x);
            lo = Math.min(x, lo * x);
            best = Math.max(best, hi);
        }
        return best;
    }

    // ---------- Stock family: state machine (holding / not holding, transactions used) ----------
    // I (LC 121): one transaction
    static int maxProfitOne(int[] p) {
        int min = Integer.MAX_VALUE, best = 0;
        for (int x : p) { min = Math.min(min, x); best = Math.max(best, x - min); }
        return best;
    }

    // II (LC 122): unlimited transactions = sum of every uphill step
    static int maxProfitUnlimited(int[] p) {
        int sum = 0;
        for (int i = 1; i < p.length; i++) sum += Math.max(0, p[i] - p[i - 1]);
        return sum;
    }

    // IV (LC 188): at most k transactions. hold[t] / sold[t] = best cash while holding / after finishing transaction t
    static int maxProfitK(int k, int[] p) {
        int n = p.length;
        if (n < 2 || k == 0) return 0;
        if (k >= n / 2) return maxProfitUnlimited(p);
        int[] hold = new int[k + 1], sold = new int[k + 1];
        Arrays.fill(hold, Integer.MIN_VALUE / 2);
        for (int x : p) {
            for (int t = 1; t <= k; t++) {
                hold[t] = Math.max(hold[t], sold[t - 1] - x); // buy: open transaction t
                sold[t] = Math.max(sold[t], hold[t] + x);     // sell: close transaction t
            }
        }
        return sold[k];
    }

    // with cooldown (LC 309): hold / sold today / rest (free to buy)
    static int maxProfitCooldown(int[] p) {
        int hold = Integer.MIN_VALUE / 2, sold = 0, rest = 0;
        for (int x : p) {
            int prevSold = sold;
            sold = hold + x;
            hold = Math.max(hold, rest - x);
            rest = Math.max(rest, prevSold);
        }
        return Math.max(sold, rest);
    }

    // with fee (LC 714)
    static int maxProfitFee(int[] p, int fee) {
        int cash = 0, hold = -p[0];
        for (int i = 1; i < p.length; i++) {
            cash = Math.max(cash, hold + p[i] - fee);
            hold = Math.max(hold, cash - p[i]);
        }
        return cash;
    }

    // ---------- Min Cost Climbing Stairs (LC 746) ----------
    static int minCostClimbingStairs(int[] cost) {
        int a = 0, b = 0; // cost to stand on step i-2 and i-1
        for (int i = 2; i <= cost.length; i++) {
            int c = Math.min(b + cost[i - 1], a + cost[i - 2]);
            a = b;
            b = c;
        }
        return b;
    }
}

Pitfalls

  1. Max product needs both max AND min: a negative flips them.
  2. Decode ways: a leading zero kills the one-digit option; 10 and 20 are the only valid two-digit zeros.
  3. Stock with k transactions: if k >= n/2 it degenerates to unlimited.
17 — DPKNAPSACK.JAVA

Knapsack Family

Loop direction over capacity decides reuse: downward means each item once, upward means reusable. For counting, coins outer gives combinations; amount outer gives ordered sequences.

PatternUse caseComplexity
0/1Subset under a capacity, each item onceO(nW)
UnboundedItems reusable (coin change, perfect squares)O(nW)
BoundedSplit count into 1,2,4,... chunks then run 0/1O(nW log c)
Subset-sum reductionsPartition, target sum, last stone IIO(n * sum)
DPKnapsack.java
0/1, unbounded, bounded, subset sum, partition, target sum, coin change min/ways, combination sum IV, perfect squares, ones and zeroes
O(nW) 
import java.util.*;

public class DPKnapsack {

    // KEY RULE: loop direction over capacity decides reuse.
    //   downward = each item at most once (0/1)      upward = items reusable (unbounded)
    // KEY RULE 2 (counting): coins OUTER = combinations (order ignored); amount OUTER = sequences (order matters).

    // ---------- 0/1 knapsack ----------
    // RECOGNIZE: pick a subset under a capacity, maximize value / feasibility / count.
    static int knapsack01(int[] wt, int[] val, int cap) {
        int[] dp = new int[cap + 1];
        for (int i = 0; i < wt.length; i++)
            for (int c = cap; c >= wt[i]; c--) dp[c] = Math.max(dp[c], dp[c - wt[i]] + val[i]);
        return dp[cap];
    }

    // ---------- Unbounded knapsack ----------
    static int knapsackUnbounded(int[] wt, int[] val, int cap) {
        int[] dp = new int[cap + 1];
        for (int i = 0; i < wt.length; i++)
            for (int c = wt[i]; c <= cap; c++) dp[c] = Math.max(dp[c], dp[c - wt[i]] + val[i]);
        return dp[cap];
    }

    // ---------- Bounded knapsack: split count k into chunks 1,2,4,... then run 0/1 ----------
    static int knapsackBounded(int[] wt, int[] val, int[] cnt, int cap) {
        List<int[]> items = new ArrayList<>();
        for (int i = 0; i < wt.length; i++) {
            int k = cnt[i];
            for (int chunk = 1; k > 0; chunk <<= 1) {
                int take = Math.min(chunk, k);
                items.add(new int[]{wt[i] * take, val[i] * take});
                k -= take;
            }
        }
        int[] dp = new int[cap + 1];
        for (int[] it : items)
            for (int c = cap; c >= it[0]; c--) dp[c] = Math.max(dp[c], dp[c - it[0]] + it[1]);
        return dp[cap];
    }

    // ---------- Subset sum: can some subset reach target? ----------
    static boolean subsetSum(int[] nums, int target) {
        if (target < 0) return false;
        boolean[] dp = new boolean[target + 1];
        dp[0] = true;
        for (int x : nums)
            for (int s = target; s >= x; s--) dp[s] |= dp[s - x];
        return dp[target];
    }

    // Partition Equal Subset Sum (LC 416)
    static boolean canPartition(int[] nums) {
        int sum = 0;
        for (int x : nums) sum += x;
        return sum % 2 == 0 && subsetSum(nums, sum / 2);
    }

    // Last Stone Weight II (LC 1049): split into two piles with the smallest difference
    static int lastStoneWeightII(int[] stones) {
        int total = 0;
        for (int x : stones) total += x;
        boolean[] dp = new boolean[total / 2 + 1];
        dp[0] = true;
        for (int x : stones)
            for (int s = total / 2; s >= x; s--) dp[s] |= dp[s - x];
        for (int s = total / 2; s >= 0; s--) if (dp[s]) return total - 2 * s;
        return 0;
    }

    // ---------- Target Sum (LC 494): +/- signs = count subsets summing to (total + target) / 2 ----------
    static int findTargetSumWays(int[] nums, int target) {
        int total = 0;
        for (int x : nums) total += x;
        if (Math.abs(target) > total || (total + target) % 2 != 0) return 0;
        int s = (total + target) / 2;
        int[] dp = new int[s + 1];
        dp[0] = 1;
        for (int x : nums)
            for (int c = s; c >= x; c--) dp[c] += dp[c - x];
        return dp[s];
    }

    // ---------- Coin Change (LC 322): fewest coins, unbounded ----------
    static int coinChangeMin(int[] coins, int amount) {
        int INF = amount + 1;
        int[] dp = new int[amount + 1];
        Arrays.fill(dp, INF);
        dp[0] = 0;
        for (int a = 1; a <= amount; a++)
            for (int c : coins) if (c <= a) dp[a] = Math.min(dp[a], dp[a - c] + 1);
        return dp[amount] >= INF ? -1 : dp[amount];
    }

    // Coin Change II (LC 518): number of COMBINATIONS, coins outer
    static int coinChangeWays(int amount, int[] coins) {
        int[] dp = new int[amount + 1];
        dp[0] = 1;
        for (int c : coins)
            for (int a = c; a <= amount; a++) dp[a] += dp[a - c];
        return dp[amount];
    }

    // Combination Sum IV (LC 377): number of ordered SEQUENCES, amount outer
    static int combinationSum4(int[] nums, int target) {
        long[] dp = new long[target + 1];
        dp[0] = 1;
        for (int t = 1; t <= target; t++)
            for (int x : nums) if (x <= t) dp[t] += dp[t - x];
        return (int) dp[target];
    }

    // Perfect Squares (LC 279): unbounded, min count
    static int numSquares(int n) {
        int[] dp = new int[n + 1];
        Arrays.fill(dp, n);
        dp[0] = 0;
        for (int i = 1; i <= n; i++)
            for (int s = 1; s * s <= i; s++) dp[i] = Math.min(dp[i], dp[i - s * s] + 1);
        return dp[n];
    }

    // ---------- Ones and Zeroes (LC 474): two capacities = 2D 0/1 knapsack ----------
    static int findMaxForm(String[] strs, int m, int n) {
        int[][] dp = new int[m + 1][n + 1];
        for (String s : strs) {
            int zeros = 0, ones = 0;
            for (char ch : s.toCharArray()) if (ch == '0') zeros++; else ones++;
            for (int i = m; i >= zeros; i--)
                for (int j = n; j >= ones; j--) dp[i][j] = Math.max(dp[i][j], dp[i - zeros][j - ones] + 1);
        }
        return dp[m][n];
    }
}

Pitfalls

  1. Iterating capacity upward in a 0/1 problem silently reuses items.
  2. Coin Change II (combinations) vs Combination Sum IV (sequences) differ only in loop order.
  3. Target Sum: the reduction is subsets summing to (total + target) / 2; check parity and range first.
18 — DPSEQUENCES.JAVA

Sequences & Strings

Two sequences give a prefix table dp[i][j]. One sequence with a pairwise condition gives LIS-style dp or the O(n log n) tails array. Palindromes use interval dp or center expansion.

PatternUse caseComplexity
LISLongest chain, each element larger than the previousO(n log n)
LCS / edit distanceTwo strings, match or edit prefixesO(nm)
PalindromesInterval dp or expand around a centerO(n^2)
MatchingWildcard, regex, interleave, distinct subsequencesO(nm)
DPSequences.java
LIS (both forms), count of LIS, envelopes, LCS, edit distance, palindromes, distinct subsequences, word break, wildcard, regex, interleave
O(nm) 
import java.util.*;

public class DPSequences {

    // ---------- LIS in O(n log n) (LC 300) ----------
    // RECOGNIZE: longest chain where each element must exceed the previous (subsequence, order kept).
    // tails[i] = smallest possible tail of an increasing subsequence of length i + 1.
    static int lengthOfLIS(int[] nums) {
        int[] tails = new int[nums.length];
        int size = 0;
        for (int x : nums) {
            int lo = 0, hi = size;
            while (lo < hi) {
                int mid = (lo + hi) >>> 1;
                if (tails[mid] < x) lo = mid + 1; else hi = mid;
            }
            tails[lo] = x;
            if (lo == size) size++;
        }
        return size;
    }

    // O(n^2) reference form: dp[i] = longest increasing subsequence ending at i
    static int lengthOfLISQuadratic(int[] nums) {
        int best = 0;
        int[] dp = new int[nums.length];
        for (int i = 0; i < nums.length; i++) {
            dp[i] = 1;
            for (int j = 0; j < i; j++) if (nums[j] < nums[i]) dp[i] = Math.max(dp[i], dp[j] + 1);
            best = Math.max(best, dp[i]);
        }
        return best;
    }

    // Number of LIS (LC 673): carry a count next to each length
    static int findNumberOfLIS(int[] nums) {
        int n = nums.length, best = 0, res = 0;
        int[] len = new int[n], cnt = new int[n];
        for (int i = 0; i < n; i++) {
            len[i] = 1;
            cnt[i] = 1;
            for (int j = 0; j < i; j++) {
                if (nums[j] >= nums[i]) continue;
                if (len[j] + 1 > len[i]) { len[i] = len[j] + 1; cnt[i] = cnt[j]; }
                else if (len[j] + 1 == len[i]) cnt[i] += cnt[j];
            }
            if (len[i] > best) { best = len[i]; res = cnt[i]; }
            else if (len[i] == best) res += cnt[i];
        }
        return res;
    }

    // Russian Doll Envelopes (LC 354): sort width asc, height DESC, then LIS on heights
    static int maxEnvelopes(int[][] env) {
        Arrays.sort(env, (a, b) -> a[0] != b[0] ? Integer.compare(a[0], b[0]) : Integer.compare(b[1], a[1]));
        int[] h = new int[env.length];
        for (int i = 0; i < env.length; i++) h[i] = env[i][1];
        return lengthOfLIS(h);
    }

    // ---------- LCS (LC 1143) ----------
    // RECOGNIZE: two sequences, keep order, match characters. dp[i][j] over prefixes.
    static int longestCommonSubsequence(String a, String b) {
        int n = a.length(), m = b.length();
        int[][] dp = new int[n + 1][m + 1];
        for (int i = 1; i <= n; i++)
            for (int j = 1; j <= m; j++)
                dp[i][j] = a.charAt(i - 1) == b.charAt(j - 1) ? dp[i - 1][j - 1] + 1 : Math.max(dp[i - 1][j], dp[i][j - 1]);
        return dp[n][m];
    }

    // ---------- Edit Distance (LC 72): insert / delete / replace ----------
    static int minDistance(String a, String b) {
        int n = a.length(), m = b.length();
        int[][] dp = new int[n + 1][m + 1];
        for (int i = 0; i <= n; i++) dp[i][0] = i;
        for (int j = 0; j <= m; j++) dp[0][j] = j;
        for (int i = 1; i <= n; i++)
            for (int j = 1; j <= m; j++)
                dp[i][j] = a.charAt(i - 1) == b.charAt(j - 1)
                        ? dp[i - 1][j - 1]
                        : 1 + Math.min(dp[i - 1][j - 1], Math.min(dp[i - 1][j], dp[i][j - 1]));
        return dp[n][m];
    }

    // ---------- Palindromes ----------
    // Longest Palindromic Subsequence (LC 516): interval dp[i][j]
    static int longestPalindromeSubseq(String s) {
        int n = s.length();
        int[][] dp = new int[n][n];
        for (int i = n - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
            dp[i][i] = 1;
            for (int j = i + 1; j < n; j++)
                dp[i][j] = s.charAt(i) == s.charAt(j) ? dp[i + 1][j - 1] + 2 : Math.max(dp[i + 1][j], dp[i][j - 1]);
        }
        return n == 0 ? 0 : dp[0][n - 1];
    }

    // Longest Palindromic Substring (LC 5): expand around every center, O(n^2) time, O(1) space
    static String longestPalindrome(String s) {
        int bs = 0, be = 0;
        for (int c = 0; c < s.length(); c++) {
            for (int w = 0; w < 2; w++) { // w = 0 odd length, w = 1 even length
                int l = c, r = c + w;
                while (l >= 0 && r < s.length() && s.charAt(l) == s.charAt(r)) { l--; r++; }
                if (r - l - 1 > be - bs) { bs = l + 1; be = r; }
            }
        }
        return s.substring(bs, be);
    }

    // Palindromic Substrings (LC 647)
    static int countSubstrings(String s) {
        int count = 0;
        for (int c = 0; c < s.length(); c++) {
            for (int w = 0; w < 2; w++) {
                int l = c, r = c + w;
                while (l >= 0 && r < s.length() && s.charAt(l) == s.charAt(r)) { count++; l--; r++; }
            }
        }
        return count;
    }

    // ---------- Distinct Subsequences (LC 115): dp[j] = ways to form t[0..j) ----------
    static int numDistinct(String s, String t) {
        int[] dp = new int[t.length() + 1];
        dp[0] = 1;
        for (char c : s.toCharArray())
            for (int j = t.length(); j >= 1; j--)
                if (c == t.charAt(j - 1)) dp[j] += dp[j - 1];
        return dp[t.length()];
    }

    // ---------- Word Break (LC 139): dp[i] = prefix of length i is breakable ----------
    static boolean wordBreak(String s, List<String> dict) {
        Set<String> set = new HashSet<>(dict);
        int maxLen = 0;
        for (String w : dict) maxLen = Math.max(maxLen, w.length());
        boolean[] dp = new boolean[s.length() + 1];
        dp[0] = true;
        for (int i = 1; i <= s.length(); i++) {
            for (int j = Math.max(0, i - maxLen); j < i; j++) {
                if (dp[j] && set.contains(s.substring(j, i))) { dp[i] = true; break; }
            }
        }
        return dp[s.length()];
    }

    // ---------- Wildcard Matching (LC 44): '?' one char, '*' any sequence ----------
    static boolean isMatchWildcard(String s, String p) {
        int n = s.length(), m = p.length();
        boolean[][] dp = new boolean[n + 1][m + 1];
        dp[0][0] = true;
        for (int j = 1; j <= m; j++) if (p.charAt(j - 1) == '*') dp[0][j] = dp[0][j - 1];
        for (int i = 1; i <= n; i++) {
            for (int j = 1; j <= m; j++) {
                char pc = p.charAt(j - 1);
                if (pc == '*') dp[i][j] = dp[i][j - 1] || dp[i - 1][j];
                else if (pc == '?' || pc == s.charAt(i - 1)) dp[i][j] = dp[i - 1][j - 1];
            }
        }
        return dp[n][m];
    }

    // ---------- Regular Expression Matching (LC 10): '.' any char, 'x*' zero or more x ----------
    static boolean isMatchRegex(String s, String p) {
        int n = s.length(), m = p.length();
        boolean[][] dp = new boolean[n + 1][m + 1];
        dp[0][0] = true;
        for (int j = 2; j <= m; j++) if (p.charAt(j - 1) == '*') dp[0][j] = dp[0][j - 2];
        for (int i = 1; i <= n; i++) {
            for (int j = 1; j <= m; j++) {
                char pc = p.charAt(j - 1);
                if (pc == '*') {
                    dp[i][j] = dp[i][j - 2]; // zero copies of the preceding element
                    char prev = p.charAt(j - 2);
                    if (prev == '.' || prev == s.charAt(i - 1)) dp[i][j] |= dp[i - 1][j]; // one more copy
                } else if (pc == '.' || pc == s.charAt(i - 1)) {
                    dp[i][j] = dp[i - 1][j - 1];
                }
            }
        }
        return dp[n][m];
    }

    // ---------- Interleaving String (LC 97) ----------
    static boolean isInterleave(String a, String b, String c) {
        int n = a.length(), m = b.length();
        if (n + m != c.length()) return false;
        boolean[] dp = new boolean[m + 1];
        for (int i = 0; i <= n; i++) {
            for (int j = 0; j <= m; j++) {
                if (i == 0 && j == 0) { dp[j] = true; continue; }
                boolean fromA = i > 0 && dp[j] && a.charAt(i - 1) == c.charAt(i + j - 1);
                boolean fromB = j > 0 && dp[j - 1] && b.charAt(j - 1) == c.charAt(i + j - 1);
                dp[j] = fromA || fromB;
            }
        }
        return dp[m];
    }

    // ---------- Longest Common SUBSTRING-style: Maximum Length of Repeated Subarray (LC 718) ----------
    static int findLength(int[] a, int[] b) {
        int[] dp = new int[b.length + 1];
        int best = 0;
        for (int i = 1; i <= a.length; i++) {
            for (int j = b.length; j >= 1; j--) {
                dp[j] = a[i - 1] == b[j - 1] ? dp[j - 1] + 1 : 0; // contiguous: reset on mismatch
                best = Math.max(best, dp[j]);
            }
        }
        return best;
    }
}

Pitfalls

  1. Russian Doll: sort width ascending and height DESCENDING so equal widths cannot chain.
  2. Subsequence means order kept, substring means contiguous: contiguous resets to 0 on mismatch.
  3. Regex star: zero copies is dp[i][j-2]; one more copy needs the preceding char to match.
19 — DPGRIDINTERVAL.JAVA

Grid & Interval DP

Grids depend on up/left (or backward for dungeon-style). Interval dp answers a range [i, j] by trying every split k; loop by length. In burst balloons, k is the LAST item processed.

PatternUse caseComplexity
Grid pathsRight/down moves, count or min/max path sumO(mn)
Max squareMin of three neighbors plus oneO(mn)
IntervalMatrix chain, burst balloons, cut stick, strange printerO(n^3)
Game dpScore difference the mover can forceO(n^2)
DPGridInterval.java
Unique paths, min path sum, triangle, maximal square, dungeon, cherry pickup II, burst balloons, matrix chain, cut stick, palindrome cuts, strange printer, stone game
O(n^3) 
import java.util.*;

public class DPGridInterval {

    // ---------- Unique Paths (LC 62) ----------
    // RECOGNIZE: grid, move right/down only, count paths or min/max path sum.
    static int uniquePaths(int m, int n) {
        int[] dp = new int[n];
        Arrays.fill(dp, 1);
        for (int i = 1; i < m; i++)
            for (int j = 1; j < n; j++) dp[j] += dp[j - 1];
        return dp[n - 1];
    }

    // Unique Paths II (LC 63): obstacles
    static int uniquePathsObstacles(int[][] g) {
        int n = g[0].length;
        int[] dp = new int[n];
        dp[0] = g[0][0] == 1 ? 0 : 1;
        for (int[] row : g)
            for (int j = 0; j < n; j++) {
                if (row[j] == 1) dp[j] = 0;
                else if (j > 0) dp[j] += dp[j - 1];
            }
        return dp[n - 1];
    }

    // Minimum Path Sum (LC 64)
    static int minPathSum(int[][] g) {
        int m = g.length, n = g[0].length;
        int[] dp = new int[n];
        for (int i = 0; i < m; i++)
            for (int j = 0; j < n; j++) {
                if (i == 0 && j == 0) dp[j] = g[0][0];
                else if (i == 0) dp[j] = dp[j - 1] + g[i][j];
                else if (j == 0) dp[j] = dp[j] + g[i][j];
                else dp[j] = Math.min(dp[j], dp[j - 1]) + g[i][j];
            }
        return dp[n - 1];
    }

    // Triangle (LC 120): bottom-up, one row
    static int minimumTotal(List<List<Integer>> t) {
        int n = t.size();
        int[] dp = new int[n + 1];
        for (int i = n - 1; i >= 0; i--)
            for (int j = 0; j <= i; j++) dp[j] = Math.min(dp[j], dp[j + 1]) + t.get(i).get(j);
        return dp[0];
    }

    // Maximal Square (LC 221): dp = side of largest square with bottom-right corner here
    static int maximalSquare(char[][] g) {
        int m = g.length, n = g[0].length, best = 0;
        int[][] dp = new int[m + 1][n + 1];
        for (int i = 1; i <= m; i++)
            for (int j = 1; j <= n; j++)
                if (g[i - 1][j - 1] == '1') {
                    dp[i][j] = 1 + Math.min(dp[i - 1][j - 1], Math.min(dp[i - 1][j], dp[i][j - 1]));
                    best = Math.max(best, dp[i][j]);
                }
        return best * best;
    }

    // Dungeon Game (LC 174): run BACKWARD, min health needed to stand at each cell
    static int calculateMinimumHP(int[][] d) {
        int m = d.length, n = d[0].length;
        int[][] dp = new int[m + 1][n + 1];
        for (int[] r : dp) Arrays.fill(r, Integer.MAX_VALUE);
        dp[m][n - 1] = dp[m - 1][n] = 1;
        for (int i = m - 1; i >= 0; i--)
            for (int j = n - 1; j >= 0; j--)
                dp[i][j] = Math.max(1, Math.min(dp[i + 1][j], dp[i][j + 1]) - d[i][j]);
        return dp[0][0];
    }

    // Cherry Pickup II (LC 1463): two walkers move row by row together; state = (col1, col2)
    static int cherryPickup2(int[][] g) {
        int m = g.length, n = g[0].length;
        int[][] dp = new int[n][n];
        for (int[] r : dp) Arrays.fill(r, Integer.MIN_VALUE / 2);
        dp[0][n - 1] = g[0][0] + g[0][n - 1];
        for (int i = 1; i < m; i++) {
            int[][] nx = new int[n][n];
            for (int[] r : nx) Arrays.fill(r, Integer.MIN_VALUE / 2);
            for (int a = 0; a < n; a++)
                for (int b = 0; b < n; b++) {
                    int best = Integer.MIN_VALUE / 2;
                    for (int da = -1; da <= 1; da++)
                        for (int db = -1; db <= 1; db++) {
                            int pa = a + da, pb = b + db;
                            if (pa >= 0 && pa < n && pb >= 0 && pb < n) best = Math.max(best, dp[pa][pb]);
                        }
                    nx[a][b] = best + g[i][a] + (a == b ? 0 : g[i][b]);
                }
            dp = nx;
        }
        int ans = 0;
        for (int[] r : dp) for (int v : r) ans = Math.max(ans, v);
        return ans;
    }

    // ===================== INTERVAL DP =====================
    // RECOGNIZE: answer for a range [i, j] built from splitting at k. Loop by LENGTH (or i descending).

    // Burst Balloons (LC 312): k is the LAST balloon burst in (i, j)
    static int maxCoins(int[] nums) {
        int n = nums.length;
        int[] a = new int[n + 2];
        a[0] = a[n + 1] = 1;
        for (int i = 0; i < n; i++) a[i + 1] = nums[i];
        int[][] dp = new int[n + 2][n + 2];
        for (int len = 2; len <= n + 1; len++)
            for (int i = 0; i + len <= n + 1; i++) {
                int j = i + len;
                for (int k = i + 1; k < j; k++)
                    dp[i][j] = Math.max(dp[i][j], dp[i][k] + a[i] * a[k] * a[j] + dp[k][j]);
            }
        return dp[0][n + 1];
    }

    // Matrix Chain Multiplication: dims[i-1] x dims[i] is matrix i
    static int matrixChainOrder(int[] dims) {
        int n = dims.length - 1;
        int[][] dp = new int[n][n];
        for (int len = 2; len <= n; len++)
            for (int i = 0; i + len - 1 < n; i++) {
                int j = i + len - 1;
                dp[i][j] = Integer.MAX_VALUE;
                for (int k = i; k < j; k++)
                    dp[i][j] = Math.min(dp[i][j], dp[i][k] + dp[k + 1][j] + dims[i] * dims[k + 1] * dims[j + 1]);
            }
        return n <= 1 ? 0 : dp[0][n - 1];
    }

    // Minimum Cost to Cut a Stick (LC 1547)
    static int minCostCutStick(int len, int[] cuts) {
        int m = cuts.length;
        int[] c = new int[m + 2];
        int[] sorted = cuts.clone();
        Arrays.sort(sorted);
        System.arraycopy(sorted, 0, c, 1, m);
        c[m + 1] = len;
        int[][] dp = new int[m + 2][m + 2];
        for (int d = 2; d <= m + 1; d++)
            for (int i = 0; i + d <= m + 1; i++) {
                int j = i + d;
                dp[i][j] = Integer.MAX_VALUE;
                for (int k = i + 1; k < j; k++) dp[i][j] = Math.min(dp[i][j], dp[i][k] + dp[k][j] + c[j] - c[i]);
            }
        return dp[0][m + 1];
    }

    // Palindrome Partitioning II (LC 132): min cuts
    static int minCutPalindrome(String s) {
        int n = s.length();
        boolean[][] pal = new boolean[n][n];
        for (int i = n - 1; i >= 0; i--)
            for (int j = i; j < n; j++) pal[i][j] = s.charAt(i) == s.charAt(j) && (j - i < 2 || pal[i + 1][j - 1]);
        int[] cut = new int[n];
        for (int j = 0; j < n; j++) {
            if (pal[0][j]) { cut[j] = 0; continue; }
            cut[j] = j;
            for (int i = 1; i <= j; i++) if (pal[i][j]) cut[j] = Math.min(cut[j], cut[i - 1] + 1);
        }
        return n == 0 ? 0 : cut[n - 1];
    }

    // Strange Printer (LC 664)
    static int strangePrinter(String s) {
        int n = s.length();
        if (n == 0) return 0;
        int[][] dp = new int[n][n];
        for (int i = n - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
            dp[i][i] = 1;
            for (int j = i + 1; j < n; j++) {
                dp[i][j] = dp[i][j - 1] + 1;
                for (int k = i; k < j; k++)
                    if (s.charAt(k) == s.charAt(j)) dp[i][j] = Math.min(dp[i][j], dp[i][k] + (k + 1 <= j - 1 ? dp[k + 1][j - 1] : 0));
            }
        }
        return dp[0][n - 1];
    }

    // Stone Game (LC 877 family): dp[i][j] = best score DIFFERENCE the mover can force on piles i..j
    static int stoneGameDiff(int[] p) {
        int n = p.length;
        int[] dp = new int[n];
        for (int i = n - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
            dp[i] = p[i];
            for (int j = i + 1; j < n; j++) dp[j] = Math.max(p[i] - dp[j], p[j] - dp[j - 1]);
        }
        return dp[n - 1];
    }
}

Pitfalls

  1. Dungeon must run backward: the minimum health needed depends on what comes AFTER.
  2. Burst balloons: think "last to burst" so the two sides become independent.
  3. Pad the array with sentinels (1 at both ends) instead of special-casing borders.
20 — DPBITMASKDIGIT.JAVA

Bitmask & Digit DP

Bitmask dp when n <= 20 and you must use each item once: dp[mask]. Digit dp when counting numbers up to N with a digit property: state is (position, tight, started, property).

PatternUse caseComplexity
AssignmentEach worker takes one task, position = popcount(mask)O(2^n n)
Subset partitionFill buckets, track sum mod targetO(2^n n)
Game on a setMemoize on the set of numbers takenO(2^n n)
Digit dpCount x in [0, N] with a propertyO(digits * states)
DPBitmaskDigit.java
Assignment, k equal subsets, beautiful arrangement, can I win, digit one count, digit set, repeated digits
O(2^n n) 
import java.util.*;

public class DPBitmaskDigit {

    // ---------- Bitmask DP: dp[mask] over a subset of <= ~20 items ----------
    // RECOGNIZE: n <= 20, "assign / partition / visit every item once".

    // Minimum Cost Assignment: cost[i][j] = worker i on task j, one each
    static int minCostAssignment(int[][] cost) {
        int n = cost.length;
        int[] dp = new int[1 << n];
        Arrays.fill(dp, Integer.MAX_VALUE);
        dp[0] = 0;
        for (int mask = 0; mask < (1 << n); mask++) {
            if (dp[mask] == Integer.MAX_VALUE) continue;
            int worker = Integer.bitCount(mask); // next worker to place
            if (worker == n) continue;
            for (int t = 0; t < n; t++)
                if ((mask >> t & 1) == 0)
                    dp[mask | 1 << t] = Math.min(dp[mask | 1 << t], dp[mask] + cost[worker][t]);
        }
        return dp[(1 << n) - 1];
    }

    // Partition to K Equal Sum Subsets (LC 698): dp[mask] = sum mod target of items used, -1 if unreachable
    static boolean canPartitionKSubsets(int[] nums, int k) {
        int sum = 0;
        for (int x : nums) sum += x;
        if (sum % k != 0) return false;
        int target = sum / k, n = nums.length;
        for (int x : nums) if (x > target) return false;
        int[] fill = new int[1 << n];
        Arrays.fill(fill, -1);
        fill[0] = 0;
        for (int mask = 0; mask < (1 << n); mask++) {
            if (fill[mask] < 0) continue;
            for (int i = 0; i < n; i++) {
                if ((mask >> i & 1) != 0 || fill[mask] + nums[i] > target) continue;
                fill[mask | 1 << i] = (fill[mask] + nums[i]) % target;
            }
        }
        return fill[(1 << n) - 1] == 0;
    }

    // Beautiful Arrangement (LC 526): position = popcount(mask) + 1
    static int countArrangement(int n) {
        int[] dp = new int[1 << n];
        dp[0] = 1;
        for (int mask = 0; mask < (1 << n); mask++) {
            if (dp[mask] == 0) continue;
            int pos = Integer.bitCount(mask) + 1;
            for (int v = 1; v <= n; v++)
                if ((mask >> (v - 1) & 1) == 0 && (v % pos == 0 || pos % v == 0)) dp[mask | 1 << (v - 1)] += dp[mask];
        }
        return dp[(1 << n) - 1];
    }

    // Can I Win (LC 464): memo over the set of numbers already taken
    static boolean canIWin(int maxChoosable, int desiredTotal) {
        if (desiredTotal <= 0) return true;
        if (maxChoosable * (maxChoosable + 1) / 2 < desiredTotal) return false;
        return win(maxChoosable, desiredTotal, 0, new Boolean[1 << maxChoosable]);
    }

    private static boolean win(int max, int remain, int used, Boolean[] memo) {
        if (memo[used] != null) return memo[used];
        for (int i = 1; i <= max; i++) {
            if ((used >> (i - 1) & 1) != 0) continue;
            if (i >= remain || !win(max, remain - i, used | 1 << (i - 1), memo)) return memo[used] = true;
        }
        return memo[used] = false;
    }

    // Subset enumeration trick: for (sub = mask; sub > 0; sub = (sub - 1) & mask) -> total 3^n over all masks

    // ---------- Digit DP ----------
    // RECOGNIZE: "count numbers in [0, N] with a digit property". State = (pos, tight, started, property).

    // Number of Digit One (LC 233)
    static int countDigitOne(int n) {
        long count = 0;
        for (long p = 1; p <= n; p *= 10) {
            long high = n / (p * 10), cur = (n / p) % 10, low = n % p;
            if (cur == 0) count += high * p;
            else if (cur == 1) count += high * p + low + 1;
            else count += (high + 1) * p;
        }
        return (int) count;
    }

    // Numbers At Most N Given Digit Set (LC 902)
    static int atMostNGivenDigitSet(String[] digits, int n) {
        String s = String.valueOf(n);
        int L = s.length(), d = digits.length, res = 0;
        for (int len = 1; len < L; len++) res += (int) Math.pow(d, len);
        for (int i = 0; i < L; i++) {
            boolean sameDigit = false;
            for (String dg : digits) {
                if (dg.charAt(0) < s.charAt(i)) res += (int) Math.pow(d, L - i - 1);
                else if (dg.charAt(0) == s.charAt(i)) sameDigit = true;
            }
            if (!sameDigit) return res;
        }
        return res + 1; // n itself is buildable
    }

    // Generic digit DP: count x in [1, n] with no repeated digit (LC 1012)
    static int numDupDigitsAtMostN(int n) {
        return n - countUniqueDigits(n);
    }

    private static int countUniqueDigits(int n) {
        char[] s = String.valueOf(n).toCharArray();
        Map<String, Integer> memo = new HashMap<>();
        return dig(s, 0, 0, true, false, memo);
    }

    private static int dig(char[] s, int pos, int mask, boolean tight, boolean started, Map<String, Integer> memo) {
        if (pos == s.length) return started ? 1 : 0;
        String key = pos + "," + mask + "," + tight + "," + started;
        Integer c = memo.get(key);
        if (c != null) return c;
        int res = 0, lim = tight ? s[pos] - '0' : 9;
        for (int d = 0; d <= lim; d++) {
            boolean nt = tight && d == lim;
            if (!started && d == 0) res += dig(s, pos + 1, mask, nt, false, memo);
            else if ((mask >> d & 1) == 0) res += dig(s, pos + 1, mask | 1 << d, nt, true, memo);
        }
        memo.put(key, res);
        return res;
    }
}

Pitfalls

  1. Digit dp: tight is only true while every prefix digit equals the bound; started handles leading zeros.
  2. Subset enumeration sub = (sub - 1) & mask totals 3^n across all masks.
  3. Use Integer.bitCount(mask) as the implicit "how many placed" index.
21 — DPCOMBINED.JAVA

DP + Other Structures

The hard-round combinations: dp with binary search (job scheduling), dp with a monotonic deque (jump game VI), probability dp, hash-map state, and memoized DFS on an implicit DAG.

PatternUse caseComplexity
dp + binary searchWeighted job scheduling: best profit up to the last compatible jobO(n log n)
dp + dequeWindow max over earlier dp valuesO(n)
Probability dpDistribution over states after k stepsO(k * states)
Memo DFSLongest increasing path in a gridO(mn)
DPCombined.java
Job scheduling, constrained subset sum, jump game VI, knight probability, new 21 game, arithmetic subsequence, longest increasing path
O(n log n) 
import java.util.*;

public class DPCombined {

    // ---------- DP + binary search: Maximum Profit in Job Scheduling (LC 1235) ----------
    // dp[i] = best profit using the first i jobs (sorted by end). Binary-search the last compatible job.
    static int jobScheduling(int[] start, int[] end, int[] profit) {
        int n = start.length;
        Integer[] idx = new Integer[n];
        for (int i = 0; i < n; i++) idx[i] = i;
        Arrays.sort(idx, Comparator.comparingInt(i -> end[i]));
        int[] ends = new int[n], dp = new int[n + 1];
        for (int i = 0; i < n; i++) ends[i] = end[idx[i]];
        for (int i = 1; i <= n; i++) {
            int job = idx[i - 1];
            int lo = 0, hi = i - 1; // count of jobs among first i-1 with end <= start[job]
            while (lo < hi) {
                int mid = (lo + hi) >>> 1;
                if (ends[mid] <= start[job]) lo = mid + 1; else hi = mid;
            }
            dp[i] = Math.max(dp[i - 1], dp[lo] + profit[job]);
        }
        return dp[n];
    }

    // ---------- DP + monotonic deque: sliding-window max over dp ----------
    // Constrained Subset Sum (LC 1425): pick a subsequence, adjacent picks at most k apart
    static int constrainedSubsetSum(int[] nums, int k) {
        Deque<Integer> dq = new ArrayDeque<>(); // indices, dp values decreasing
        int[] dp = new int[nums.length];
        int best = Integer.MIN_VALUE;
        for (int i = 0; i < nums.length; i++) {
            while (!dq.isEmpty() && dq.peekFirst() < i - k) dq.pollFirst();
            dp[i] = nums[i] + (dq.isEmpty() ? 0 : Math.max(0, dp[dq.peekFirst()]));
            while (!dq.isEmpty() && dp[dq.peekLast()] <= dp[i]) dq.pollLast();
            dq.addLast(i);
            best = Math.max(best, dp[i]);
        }
        return best;
    }

    // Jump Game VI (LC 1696): max score path with jumps of 1..k
    static int maxResult(int[] nums, int k) {
        int n = nums.length;
        int[] dp = new int[n];
        dp[0] = nums[0];
        Deque<Integer> dq = new ArrayDeque<>();
        dq.addLast(0);
        for (int i = 1; i < n; i++) {
            while (dq.peekFirst() < i - k) dq.pollFirst();
            dp[i] = nums[i] + dp[dq.peekFirst()];
            while (!dq.isEmpty() && dp[dq.peekLast()] <= dp[i]) dq.pollLast();
            dq.addLast(i);
        }
        return dp[n - 1];
    }

    // ---------- Probability DP ----------
    // Knight Probability in Chessboard (LC 688)
    static double knightProbability(int n, int k, int row, int col) {
        int[][] mv = {{1, 2}, {2, 1}, {-1, 2}, {-2, 1}, {1, -2}, {2, -1}, {-1, -2}, {-2, -1}};
        double[][] cur = new double[n][n];
        cur[row][col] = 1;
        for (int s = 0; s < k; s++) {
            double[][] nx = new double[n][n];
            for (int r = 0; r < n; r++)
                for (int c = 0; c < n; c++) {
                    if (cur[r][c] == 0) continue;
                    for (int[] m : mv) {
                        int nr = r + m[0], nc = c + m[1];
                        if (nr >= 0 && nr < n && nc >= 0 && nc < n) nx[nr][nc] += cur[r][c] / 8.0;
                    }
                }
            cur = nx;
        }
        double sum = 0;
        for (double[] r : cur) for (double v : r) sum += v;
        return sum;
    }

    // New 21 Game (LC 837): dp[i] = P(reach score i), sliding window sum
    static double new21Game(int n, int k, int maxPts) {
        if (k == 0 || n >= k + maxPts) return 1.0;
        double[] dp = new double[n + 1];
        dp[0] = 1.0;
        double window = 1.0, ans = 0;
        for (int i = 1; i <= n; i++) {
            dp[i] = window / maxPts;
            if (i < k) window += dp[i]; else ans += dp[i];
            if (i - maxPts >= 0 && i - maxPts < k) window -= dp[i - maxPts];
        }
        return ans;
    }

    // ---------- Hash-map-state DP ----------
    // Longest Arithmetic Subsequence (LC 1027): dp[i][diff]
    static int longestArithSeqLength(int[] nums) {
        int n = nums.length, best = 2;
        if (n <= 2) return n;
        List<Map<Integer, Integer>> dp = new ArrayList<>();
        for (int i = 0; i < n; i++) {
            Map<Integer, Integer> m = new HashMap<>();
            for (int j = 0; j < i; j++) {
                int diff = nums[i] - nums[j];
                int len = dp.get(j).getOrDefault(diff, 1) + 1;
                m.merge(diff, len, Math::max);
                best = Math.max(best, len);
            }
            dp.add(m);
        }
        return best;
    }

    // ---------- Memoized DFS on a DAG: Longest Increasing Path in a Matrix (LC 329) ----------
    static int longestIncreasingPath(int[][] g) {
        int m = g.length, n = g[0].length, best = 0;
        int[][] memo = new int[m][n];
        for (int i = 0; i < m; i++)
            for (int j = 0; j < n; j++) best = Math.max(best, lip(g, i, j, memo));
        return best;
    }

    private static int lip(int[][] g, int i, int j, int[][] memo) {
        if (memo[i][j] != 0) return memo[i][j];
        int best = 1;
        int[] d = {0, 1, 0, -1, 0};
        for (int k = 0; k < 4; k++) {
            int r = i + d[k], c = j + d[k + 1];
            if (r >= 0 && r < g.length && c >= 0 && c < g[0].length && g[r][c] > g[i][j])
                best = Math.max(best, 1 + lip(g, r, c, memo));
        }
        return memo[i][j] = best;
    }
}

Pitfalls

  1. When transitions look at a sliding window of earlier dp values, reach for a monotonic deque before a segment tree.
  2. Longest increasing path needs no visited set: strictly increasing values make the graph a DAG.
22 — BINARYTREE.JAVA

Binary Tree Patterns

Most tree problems are one post-order that returns a value and optionally updates a global. Traversals, level order, LCA, build from traversals, serialization and tree dp share that skeleton.

PatternUse caseComplexity
TraversalsInorder / preorder / postorder iterative, Morris for O(1) spaceO(n)
Level orderQueue with a level-size snapshotO(n)
Post-order value + globalHeight, diameter, max path sum, balancedO(n)
Tree dpRob III, cameras: return a small state tuple per nodeO(n)
BinaryTree.java
Traversals, level order, diameter, max path sum, LCA, build, serialize, flatten, distance K, vertical order, rob III, cameras
O(n) 
import java.util.*;

public class BinaryTree {

    static class TreeNode {
        int val;
        TreeNode left, right;
        TreeNode(int v) { val = v; }
    }

    // ---------- Traversals ----------
    // RECOGNIZE: any "visit every node" ask. Recursive is fine; know the iterative forms for follow-ups.
    static List<Integer> inorder(TreeNode root) {
        List<Integer> out = new ArrayList<>();
        Deque<TreeNode> st = new ArrayDeque<>();
        TreeNode cur = root;
        while (cur != null || !st.isEmpty()) {
            while (cur != null) { st.push(cur); cur = cur.left; }
            cur = st.pop();
            out.add(cur.val);
            cur = cur.right;
        }
        return out;
    }

    static List<Integer> preorder(TreeNode root) {
        List<Integer> out = new ArrayList<>();
        Deque<TreeNode> st = new ArrayDeque<>();
        if (root != null) st.push(root);
        while (!st.isEmpty()) {
            TreeNode n = st.pop();
            out.add(n.val);
            if (n.right != null) st.push(n.right);
            if (n.left != null) st.push(n.left);
        }
        return out;
    }

    // postorder = reverse of (root, right, left)
    static List<Integer> postorder(TreeNode root) {
        LinkedList<Integer> out = new LinkedList<>();
        Deque<TreeNode> st = new ArrayDeque<>();
        if (root != null) st.push(root);
        while (!st.isEmpty()) {
            TreeNode n = st.pop();
            out.addFirst(n.val);
            if (n.left != null) st.push(n.left);
            if (n.right != null) st.push(n.right);
        }
        return out;
    }

    // Morris inorder: O(1) extra space by threading predecessor.right -> current
    static List<Integer> inorderMorris(TreeNode root) {
        List<Integer> out = new ArrayList<>();
        TreeNode cur = root;
        while (cur != null) {
            if (cur.left == null) { out.add(cur.val); cur = cur.right; continue; }
            TreeNode pred = cur.left;
            while (pred.right != null && pred.right != cur) pred = pred.right;
            if (pred.right == null) { pred.right = cur; cur = cur.left; }
            else { pred.right = null; out.add(cur.val); cur = cur.right; }
        }
        return out;
    }

    // Level order (LC 102): one queue, snapshot the level size
    static List<List<Integer>> levelOrder(TreeNode root) {
        List<List<Integer>> res = new ArrayList<>();
        if (root == null) return res;
        Deque<TreeNode> q = new ArrayDeque<>();
        q.add(root);
        while (!q.isEmpty()) {
            int sz = q.size();
            List<Integer> level = new ArrayList<>();
            while (sz-- > 0) {
                TreeNode n = q.poll();
                level.add(n.val);
                if (n.left != null) q.add(n.left);
                if (n.right != null) q.add(n.right);
            }
            res.add(level);
        }
        return res;
    }

    // Zigzag (LC 103)
    static List<List<Integer>> zigzag(TreeNode root) {
        List<List<Integer>> res = levelOrder(root);
        for (int i = 1; i < res.size(); i += 2) Collections.reverse(res.get(i));
        return res;
    }

    // Right side view (LC 199): last node of each level
    static List<Integer> rightSideView(TreeNode root) {
        List<Integer> out = new ArrayList<>();
        for (List<Integer> lv : levelOrder(root)) out.add(lv.get(lv.size() - 1));
        return out;
    }

    // ---------- Height / balance / diameter: ONE post-order returning a value, updating a global ----------
    static int maxDepth(TreeNode n) { return n == null ? 0 : 1 + Math.max(maxDepth(n.left), maxDepth(n.right)); }

    private static int diam;

    static int diameter(TreeNode root) {
        diam = 0;
        depthForDiameter(root);
        return diam; // in edges
    }

    private static int depthForDiameter(TreeNode n) {
        if (n == null) return 0;
        int l = depthForDiameter(n.left), r = depthForDiameter(n.right);
        diam = Math.max(diam, l + r);
        return 1 + Math.max(l, r);
    }

    // Balanced (LC 110): -1 means "unbalanced" bubbled up
    static boolean isBalanced(TreeNode root) { return height(root) >= 0; }

    private static int height(TreeNode n) {
        if (n == null) return 0;
        int l = height(n.left);
        if (l < 0) return -1;
        int r = height(n.right);
        if (r < 0 || Math.abs(l - r) > 1) return -1;
        return 1 + Math.max(l, r);
    }

    // Max path sum (LC 124): return the best DOWNWARD gain, update answer with the bent path through node
    private static int bestPath;

    static int maxPathSum(TreeNode root) {
        bestPath = Integer.MIN_VALUE;
        gain(root);
        return bestPath;
    }

    private static int gain(TreeNode n) {
        if (n == null) return 0;
        int l = Math.max(0, gain(n.left)), r = Math.max(0, gain(n.right));
        bestPath = Math.max(bestPath, n.val + l + r);
        return n.val + Math.max(l, r);
    }

    // ---------- Structure checks ----------
    static boolean isSameTree(TreeNode a, TreeNode b) {
        if (a == null || b == null) return a == b;
        return a.val == b.val && isSameTree(a.left, b.left) && isSameTree(a.right, b.right);
    }
