    static boolean isSymmetric(TreeNode root) { return root == null || mirror(root.left, root.right); }

    private static boolean mirror(TreeNode a, TreeNode b) {
        if (a == null || b == null) return a == b;
        return a.val == b.val && mirror(a.left, b.right) && mirror(a.right, b.left);
    }

    static TreeNode invertTree(TreeNode n) {
        if (n == null) return null;
        TreeNode t = n.left;
        n.left = invertTree(n.right);
        n.right = invertTree(t);
        return n;
    }

    // Subtree of another tree (LC 572)
    static boolean isSubtree(TreeNode root, TreeNode sub) {
        if (root == null) return sub == null;
        return isSameTree(root, sub) || isSubtree(root.left, sub) || isSubtree(root.right, sub);
    }

    // ---------- Path sums ----------
    static boolean hasPathSum(TreeNode n, int target) {
        if (n == null) return false;
        if (n.left == null && n.right == null) return n.val == target;
        return hasPathSum(n.left, target - n.val) || hasPathSum(n.right, target - n.val);
    }

    // Path Sum III (LC 437): prefix-sum map along the root-to-node path
    static int pathSumIII(TreeNode root, int target) {
        Map<Long, Integer> seen = new HashMap<>();
        seen.put(0L, 1);
        return pathSumDfs(root, 0, target, seen);
    }

    private static int pathSumDfs(TreeNode n, long sum, int target, Map<Long, Integer> seen) {
        if (n == null) return 0;
        sum += n.val;
        int count = seen.getOrDefault(sum - target, 0);
        seen.merge(sum, 1, Integer::sum);
        count += pathSumDfs(n.left, sum, target, seen) + pathSumDfs(n.right, sum, target, seen);
        seen.merge(sum, -1, Integer::sum); // backtrack
        return count;
    }

    // ---------- LCA (binary tree) (LC 236) ----------
    static TreeNode lowestCommonAncestor(TreeNode root, TreeNode p, TreeNode q) {
        if (root == null || root == p || root == q) return root;
        TreeNode l = lowestCommonAncestor(root.left, p, q), r = lowestCommonAncestor(root.right, p, q);
        return l != null && r != null ? root : l != null ? l : r;
    }

    // ---------- Build from traversals ----------
    // Preorder + inorder (LC 105)
    static TreeNode buildTree(int[] pre, int[] in) {
        Map<Integer, Integer> pos = new HashMap<>();
        for (int i = 0; i < in.length; i++) pos.put(in[i], i);
        return build(pre, new int[]{0}, 0, in.length - 1, pos);
    }

    private static TreeNode build(int[] pre, int[] pi, int lo, int hi, Map<Integer, Integer> pos) {
        if (lo > hi) return null;
        TreeNode root = new TreeNode(pre[pi[0]++]);
        int mid = pos.get(root.val);
        root.left = build(pre, pi, lo, mid - 1, pos);
        root.right = build(pre, pi, mid + 1, hi, pos);
        return root;
    }

    // ---------- Serialize / deserialize (LC 297): preorder with null markers ----------
    static String serialize(TreeNode root) {
        StringBuilder sb = new StringBuilder();
        ser(root, sb);
        return sb.toString();
    }

    private static void ser(TreeNode n, StringBuilder sb) {
        if (n == null) { sb.append("#,"); return; }
        sb.append(n.val).append(',');
        ser(n.left, sb);
        ser(n.right, sb);
    }

    static TreeNode deserialize(String data) {
        return des(new ArrayDeque<>(Arrays.asList(data.split(","))));
    }

    private static TreeNode des(Deque<String> q) {
        String s = q.poll();
        if (s == null || s.equals("#")) return null;
        TreeNode n = new TreeNode(Integer.parseInt(s));
        n.left = des(q);
        n.right = des(q);
        return n;
    }

    // ---------- Flatten to linked list (LC 114) ----------
    static void flatten(TreeNode root) {
        TreeNode cur = root;
        while (cur != null) {
            if (cur.left != null) {
                TreeNode p = cur.left;
                while (p.right != null) p = p.right;
                p.right = cur.right;
                cur.right = cur.left;
                cur.left = null;
            }
            cur = cur.right;
        }
    }

    // ---------- Distance K from a node (LC 863): parent map + BFS ----------
    static List<Integer> distanceK(TreeNode root, TreeNode target, int k) {
        Map<TreeNode, TreeNode> parent = new HashMap<>();
        Deque<TreeNode> q = new ArrayDeque<>();
        q.add(root);
        while (!q.isEmpty()) {
            TreeNode n = q.poll();
            for (TreeNode c : new TreeNode[]{n.left, n.right})
                if (c != null) { parent.put(c, n); q.add(c); }
        }
        Set<TreeNode> seen = new HashSet<>(List.of(target));
        q.add(target);
        while (!q.isEmpty() && k-- > 0) {
            for (int s = q.size(); s > 0; s--) {
                TreeNode n = q.poll();
                for (TreeNode nb : new TreeNode[]{n.left, n.right, parent.get(n)})
                    if (nb != null && seen.add(nb)) q.add(nb);
            }
        }
        List<Integer> out = new ArrayList<>();
        for (TreeNode n : q) out.add(n.val);
        return out;
    }

    // ---------- Vertical order (LC 987): sort by column, then row, then value ----------
    static List<List<Integer>> verticalTraversal(TreeNode root) {
        List<int[]> cells = new ArrayList<>(); // col, row, val
        Deque<Object[]> q = new ArrayDeque<>();
        q.add(new Object[]{root, 0, 0});
        while (!q.isEmpty()) {
            Object[] t = q.poll();
            TreeNode n = (TreeNode) t[0];
            if (n == null) continue;
            int col = (int) t[1], row = (int) t[2];
            cells.add(new int[]{col, row, n.val});
            q.add(new Object[]{n.left, col - 1, row + 1});
            q.add(new Object[]{n.right, col + 1, row + 1});
        }
        cells.sort((a, b) -> a[0] != b[0] ? a[0] - b[0] : a[1] != b[1] ? a[1] - b[1] : a[2] - b[2]);
        List<List<Integer>> res = new ArrayList<>();
        int prev = Integer.MIN_VALUE;
        for (int[] c : cells) {
            if (c[0] != prev) { res.add(new ArrayList<>()); prev = c[0]; }
            res.get(res.size() - 1).add(c[2]);
        }
        return res;
    }

    // ---------- Tree DP: House Robber III (LC 337) -> {rob this, skip this} ----------
    static int robTree(TreeNode root) {
        int[] r = robDfs(root);
        return Math.max(r[0], r[1]);
    }

    private static int[] robDfs(TreeNode n) {
        if (n == null) return new int[]{0, 0};
        int[] l = robDfs(n.left), r = robDfs(n.right);
        return new int[]{n.val + l[1] + r[1], Math.max(l[0], l[1]) + Math.max(r[0], r[1])};
    }

    // Binary Tree Cameras (LC 968): states 0 = needs cover, 1 = has camera, 2 = covered
    private static int cams;

    static int minCameraCover(TreeNode root) {
        cams = 0;
        return (cover(root) == 0 ? 1 : 0) + cams;
    }

    private static int cover(TreeNode n) {
        if (n == null) return 2;
        int l = cover(n.left), r = cover(n.right);
        if (l == 0 || r == 0) { cams++; return 1; }
        return l == 1 || r == 1 ? 2 : 0;
    }

    // Helper for tests: build from level-order array, Integer null = absent
    static TreeNode fromLevel(Integer... a) {
        if (a.length == 0 || a[0] == null) return null;
        TreeNode root = new TreeNode(a[0]);
        Deque<TreeNode> q = new ArrayDeque<>();
        q.add(root);
        int i = 1;
        while (!q.isEmpty() && i < a.length) {
            TreeNode n = q.poll();
            if (i < a.length && a[i] != null) { n.left = new TreeNode(a[i]); q.add(n.left); }
            i++;
            if (i < a.length && a[i] != null) { n.right = new TreeNode(a[i]); q.add(n.right); }
            i++;
        }
        return root;
    }
}

Pitfalls

  1. Max path sum: return the best DOWNWARD gain (a path cannot fork upward), but update the answer with the bent path through the node.
  2. Path Sum III: backtrack the prefix-sum map after both children return.
  3. Recursion depth can hit 10^5 on a skewed tree; mention the iterative form.
23 — BST.JAVA

Binary Search Trees

Inorder of a BST is sorted; every BST question is either "use that" or "carry open bounds down". Deletion with two children replaces the node with its inorder successor.

PatternUse caseComplexity
ValidateCarry (lo, hi) bounds, never compare only to the parentO(n)
Kth / iteratorIterative inorder, stop early; stack of the left spineO(h)
Search / LCAWalk down; no recursion neededO(h)
RecoverTwo inversions in the inorder sequenceO(n)
BST.java
Validate, search, insert, delete, kth smallest, LCA, sorted array to BST, recover, iterator, largest BST subtree, floor, Catalan count
O(h) 
import java.util.*;

public class BST {
    // Uses BinaryTree.TreeNode (same package, default package).

    // ---------- Validate (LC 98): carry open bounds down ----------
    static boolean isValidBST(BinaryTree.TreeNode n) { return valid(n, null, null); }

    private static boolean valid(BinaryTree.TreeNode n, Integer lo, Integer hi) {
        if (n == null) return true;
        if ((lo != null && n.val <= lo) || (hi != null && n.val >= hi)) return false;
        return valid(n.left, lo, n.val) && valid(n.right, n.val, hi);
    }

    // ---------- Search / insert / delete ----------
    static BinaryTree.TreeNode search(BinaryTree.TreeNode n, int key) {
        while (n != null && n.val != key) n = key < n.val ? n.left : n.right;
        return n;
    }

    static BinaryTree.TreeNode insert(BinaryTree.TreeNode n, int key) {
        if (n == null) return new BinaryTree.TreeNode(key);
        if (key < n.val) n.left = insert(n.left, key); else n.right = insert(n.right, key);
        return n;
    }

    // Delete (LC 450): 0 or 1 child -> splice; 2 children -> replace with inorder successor
    static BinaryTree.TreeNode delete(BinaryTree.TreeNode n, int key) {
        if (n == null) return null;
        if (key < n.val) n.left = delete(n.left, key);
        else if (key > n.val) n.right = delete(n.right, key);
        else {
            if (n.left == null) return n.right;
            if (n.right == null) return n.left;
            BinaryTree.TreeNode s = n.right;
            while (s.left != null) s = s.left;
            n.val = s.val;
            n.right = delete(n.right, s.val);
        }
        return n;
    }

    // ---------- Kth smallest (LC 230): iterative inorder, stop early ----------
    static int kthSmallest(BinaryTree.TreeNode root, int k) {
        Deque<BinaryTree.TreeNode> st = new ArrayDeque<>();
        BinaryTree.TreeNode cur = root;
        while (cur != null || !st.isEmpty()) {
            while (cur != null) { st.push(cur); cur = cur.left; }
            cur = st.pop();
            if (--k == 0) return cur.val;
            cur = cur.right;
        }
        return -1;
    }

    // ---------- LCA in BST (LC 235): walk down, no recursion needed ----------
    static BinaryTree.TreeNode lcaBST(BinaryTree.TreeNode root, int p, int q) {
        while (root != null) {
            if (p < root.val && q < root.val) root = root.left;
            else if (p > root.val && q > root.val) root = root.right;
            else return root;
        }
        return null;
    }

    // ---------- Sorted array -> height-balanced BST (LC 108) ----------
    static BinaryTree.TreeNode sortedArrayToBST(int[] a) { return arr(a, 0, a.length - 1); }

    private static BinaryTree.TreeNode arr(int[] a, int lo, int hi) {
        if (lo > hi) return null;
        int mid = (lo + hi) >>> 1;
        BinaryTree.TreeNode n = new BinaryTree.TreeNode(a[mid]);
        n.left = arr(a, lo, mid - 1);
        n.right = arr(a, mid + 1, hi);
        return n;
    }

    // ---------- Recover BST (LC 99): two inversions in the inorder sequence ----------
    static void recoverTree(BinaryTree.TreeNode root) {
        BinaryTree.TreeNode first = null, second = null, prev = null, cur = root;
        Deque<BinaryTree.TreeNode> st = new ArrayDeque<>();
        while (cur != null || !st.isEmpty()) {
            while (cur != null) { st.push(cur); cur = cur.left; }
            cur = st.pop();
            if (prev != null && prev.val > cur.val) {
                if (first == null) first = prev;
                second = cur;
            }
            prev = cur;
            cur = cur.right;
        }
        int t = first.val; first.val = second.val; second.val = t;
    }

    // ---------- BST Iterator (LC 173): stack of left spine, O(h) space, amortized O(1) next ----------
    static class BSTIterator {
        private final Deque<BinaryTree.TreeNode> st = new ArrayDeque<>();
        BSTIterator(BinaryTree.TreeNode root) { push(root); }
        private void push(BinaryTree.TreeNode n) { while (n != null) { st.push(n); n = n.left; } }
        int next() { BinaryTree.TreeNode n = st.pop(); push(n.right); return n.val; }
        boolean hasNext() { return !st.isEmpty(); }
    }

    // ---------- Largest BST subtree (LC 333): post-order returns {isBST, size, min, max} ----------
    static int largestBSTSubtree(BinaryTree.TreeNode root) { return lb(root)[3]; }

    // returns {min, max, isBST(1/0), bestSize / size}
    private static int[] lb(BinaryTree.TreeNode n) {
        if (n == null) return new int[]{Integer.MAX_VALUE, Integer.MIN_VALUE, 1, 0};
        int[] l = lb(n.left), r = lb(n.right);
        if (l[2] == 1 && r[2] == 1 && l[1] < n.val && n.val < r[0]) {
            int size = l[3] + r[3] + 1;
            return new int[]{Math.min(l[0], n.val), Math.max(r[1], n.val), 1, size};
        }
        return new int[]{0, 0, 0, Math.max(l[3], r[3])};
    }

    // ---------- Floor / ceiling ----------
    static Integer floor(BinaryTree.TreeNode n, int x) {
        Integer best = null;
        while (n != null) {
            if (n.val == x) return x;
            if (n.val < x) { best = n.val; n = n.right; } else n = n.left;
        }
        return best;
    }

    // ---------- Unique BSTs count (LC 96): Catalan ----------
    static int numTrees(int n) {
        int[] dp = new int[n + 1];
        dp[0] = dp[1] = 1;
        for (int i = 2; i <= n; i++)
            for (int j = 0; j < i; j++) dp[i] += dp[j] * dp[i - 1 - j];
        return dp[n];
    }

    // TreeMap cheat sheet (when an ordered structure is needed in an interview):
    //   floorKey / ceilingKey / lowerKey / higherKey / firstKey / lastKey / pollFirstEntry / headMap / tailMap -- all O(log n)
}

Pitfalls

  1. Comparing a node only against its parent accepts invalid trees; carry bounds.
  2. Recover BST: the first inversion marks first = prev, the last marks second = cur.
  3. TreeMap gives floor/ceiling/first/last in O(log n) when a whole ordered structure is needed.
24 — TRIE.JAVA

Tries

Use a trie when many queries share prefixes: autocomplete, dictionary with wildcards, word search over a grid, and bitwise tries for maximum XOR.

PatternUse caseComplexity
Insert / search / prefix26-way children with end marker and pass countO(L)
WildcardDFS over children on a dotO(26^L) worst
Grid + triePrune words as they are foundO(cells * 4^L)
Binary trieMaximum XOR pair, greedy bit by bitO(32n)
Trie.java
Trie, wildcard search, word search II, maximum XOR, replace words, suggested products
O(L) 
import java.util.*;

public class Trie {

    // ---------- Basic trie (LC 208) ----------
    // RECOGNIZE: many prefix queries, autocomplete, dictionary + wildcard, word search over a grid.
    private final Trie[] next = new Trie[26];
    private boolean end;
    private int passCount; // how many inserted words pass through this node

    void insert(String w) {
        Trie t = this;
        for (char c : w.toCharArray()) {
            if (t.next[c - 'a'] == null) t.next[c - 'a'] = new Trie();
            t = t.next[c - 'a'];
            t.passCount++;
        }
        t.end = true;
    }

    private Trie walk(String s) {
        Trie t = this;
        for (char c : s.toCharArray()) {
            t = t.next[c - 'a'];
            if (t == null) return null;
        }
        return t;
    }

    boolean search(String w) { Trie t = walk(w); return t != null && t.end; }
    boolean startsWith(String p) { return walk(p) != null; }
    int countPrefix(String p) { Trie t = walk(p); return t == null ? 0 : t.passCount; }

    // ---------- Wildcard '.' (LC 211): DFS over children on '.' ----------
    boolean searchWildcard(String w) { return wild(w, 0, this); }

    private boolean wild(String w, int i, Trie t) {
        if (t == null) return false;
        if (i == w.length()) return t.end;
        char c = w.charAt(i);
        if (c != '.') return wild(w, i + 1, t.next[c - 'a']);
        for (Trie ch : t.next) if (ch != null && wild(w, i + 1, ch)) return true;
        return false;
    }

    // ---------- Word Search II (LC 212): trie + grid DFS, prune finished branches ----------
    static List<String> findWords(char[][] board, String[] words) {
        Node root = new Node();
        for (String w : words) {
            Node n = root;
            for (char c : w.toCharArray()) {
                if (n.kids[c - 'a'] == null) n.kids[c - 'a'] = new Node();
                n = n.kids[c - 'a'];
            }
            n.word = w;
        }
        List<String> out = new ArrayList<>();
        for (int i = 0; i < board.length; i++)
            for (int j = 0; j < board[0].length; j++) dfs(board, i, j, root, out);
        return out;
    }

    private static class Node {
        Node[] kids = new Node[26];
        String word;
    }

    private static void dfs(char[][] b, int i, int j, Node p, List<String> out) {
        if (i < 0 || j < 0 || i >= b.length || j >= b[0].length) return;
        char c = b[i][j];
        if (c == '#' || p.kids[c - 'a'] == null) return;
        p = p.kids[c - 'a'];
        if (p.word != null) { out.add(p.word); p.word = null; } // dedupe
        b[i][j] = '#';
        dfs(b, i + 1, j, p, out); dfs(b, i - 1, j, p, out); dfs(b, i, j + 1, p, out); dfs(b, i, j - 1, p, out);
        b[i][j] = c;
    }

    // ---------- Binary trie: Maximum XOR of two numbers (LC 421) ----------
    static int findMaximumXOR(int[] nums) {
        int[][] ch = new int[nums.length * 32 + 2][2];
        int cnt = 1, best = 0;
        for (int x : nums) {
            int node = 0, other = 0, xr = 0;
            // insert x
            for (int b = 30; b >= 0; b--) {
                int bit = x >> b & 1;
                if (ch[node][bit] == 0) ch[node][bit] = cnt++;
                node = ch[node][bit];
            }
            // query best partner
            node = 0;
            for (int b = 30; b >= 0; b--) {
                int bit = x >> b & 1, want = bit ^ 1;
                if (ch[node][want] != 0) { xr |= 1 << b; node = ch[node][want]; } else node = ch[node][bit];
            }
            best = Math.max(best, xr);
        }
        return best;
    }

    // ---------- Replace Words (LC 648): shortest root prefix ----------
    static String replaceWords(List<String> dict, String sentence) {
        Node root = new Node();
        for (String w : dict) {
            Node n = root;
            for (char c : w.toCharArray()) {
                if (n.kids[c - 'a'] == null) n.kids[c - 'a'] = new Node();
                n = n.kids[c - 'a'];
            }
            n.word = w;
        }
        StringBuilder sb = new StringBuilder();
        for (String tok : sentence.split(" ")) {
            Node n = root;
            String rep = tok;
            for (char c : tok.toCharArray()) {
                n = n.kids[c - 'a'];
                if (n == null) break;
                if (n.word != null) { rep = n.word; break; }
            }
            if (sb.length() > 0) sb.append(' ');
            sb.append(rep);
        }
        return sb.toString();
    }

    // ---------- Autocomplete top-3 (LC 1268 style): sorted words + binary search is the simple robust form ----------
    static List<List<String>> suggestedProducts(String[] products, String search) {
        Arrays.sort(products);
        List<List<String>> res = new ArrayList<>();
        String prefix = "";
        for (char c : search.toCharArray()) {
            prefix += c;
            int lo = 0, hi = products.length;
            while (lo < hi) {
                int mid = (lo + hi) >>> 1;
                if (products[mid].compareTo(prefix) < 0) lo = mid + 1; else hi = mid;
            }
            List<String> top = new ArrayList<>();
            for (int i = lo; i < Math.min(lo + 3, products.length) && products[i].startsWith(prefix); i++) top.add(products[i]);
            res.add(top);
        }
        return res;
    }
}

Pitfalls

  1. Word Search II: null out the stored word after finding it so duplicates are not reported.
  2. Binary trie over 31 bits (not 32) for non-negative ints keeps the sign bit out of the way.
25 — RANGESTRUCTURES.JAVA

Range Queries

Pick the lightest tool: prefix sums for static sums, a difference array for range add, Fenwick for point update plus prefix sum, a segment tree for any monoid, lazy propagation for range updates, a sparse table for static range min.

PatternUse caseComplexity
Prefix / differenceStatic range sum, offline range addO(n)
FenwickPoint update, prefix sum, inversion countingO(log n)
Segment treePoint update, range query (any associative op)O(log n)
Lazy segment treeRange add + range sumO(log n)
Sparse tableStatic range min in O(1)O(n log n) build
RangeStructures.java
Prefix, difference, 2D prefix, Fenwick, count smaller, inversions, segment tree, lazy tree, sparse table, skyline
O(log n) 
import java.util.*;

public class RangeStructures {

    // Pick: prefix sums (static), Fenwick (point update + prefix/range sum), segment tree (any monoid, range ops),
    //       lazy segment tree (range update), sparse table (static range min/max in O(1)).

    // ---------- Prefix sum / difference array ----------
    static long[] prefix(int[] a) {
        long[] p = new long[a.length + 1];
        for (int i = 0; i < a.length; i++) p[i + 1] = p[i] + a[i];
        return p; // sum [l, r] = p[r + 1] - p[l]
    }

    // Range add via difference array (LC 370 / 1109)
    static int[] rangeAdd(int n, int[][] updates) { // {l, r, v}
        int[] d = new int[n + 1];
        for (int[] u : updates) { d[u[0]] += u[2]; d[u[1] + 1] -= u[2]; }
        int[] res = new int[n];
        int run = 0;
        for (int i = 0; i < n; i++) { run += d[i]; res[i] = run; }
        return res;
    }

    // 2D prefix sum (LC 304)
    static class NumMatrix {
        private final int[][] p;
        NumMatrix(int[][] m) {
            p = new int[m.length + 1][m[0].length + 1];
            for (int i = 0; i < m.length; i++)
                for (int j = 0; j < m[0].length; j++) p[i + 1][j + 1] = m[i][j] + p[i][j + 1] + p[i + 1][j] - p[i][j];
        }
        int sumRegion(int r1, int c1, int r2, int c2) { return p[r2 + 1][c2 + 1] - p[r1][c2 + 1] - p[r2 + 1][c1] + p[r1][c1]; }
    }

    // ---------- Fenwick tree (BIT), 1-indexed internally ----------
    static class Fenwick {
        private final long[] t;
        Fenwick(int n) { t = new long[n + 1]; }
        void add(int i, long delta) { for (i++; i < t.length; i += i & -i) t[i] += delta; }       // 0-indexed API
        long prefix(int i) { long s = 0; for (i++; i > 0; i -= i & -i) s += t[i]; return s; }     // sum [0, i]
        long range(int l, int r) { return prefix(r) - (l > 0 ? prefix(l - 1) : 0); }
    }

    // Count of Smaller Numbers After Self (LC 315): compress values, Fenwick over ranks, scan right to left
    static List<Integer> countSmaller(int[] nums) {
        int n = nums.length;
        int[] sorted = Arrays.stream(nums).distinct().sorted().toArray();
        Fenwick f = new Fenwick(sorted.length);
        Integer[] res = new Integer[n];
        for (int i = n - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
            int rank = Arrays.binarySearch(sorted, nums[i]);
            res[i] = rank == 0 ? 0 : (int) f.prefix(rank - 1);
            f.add(rank, 1);
        }
        return Arrays.asList(res);
    }

    // Count inversions with Fenwick
    static long countInversions(int[] a) {
        int[] sorted = Arrays.stream(a).distinct().sorted().toArray();
        Fenwick f = new Fenwick(sorted.length);
        long inv = 0;
        for (int i = a.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
            int rank = Arrays.binarySearch(sorted, a[i]);
            if (rank > 0) inv += f.prefix(rank - 1);
            f.add(rank, 1);
        }
        return inv;
    }

    // ---------- Segment tree (iterative, bottom-up): point update, range query (sum; swap combine for min/max) ----------
    static class SegTree {
        private final int n;
        private final long[] t;
        SegTree(int[] a) {
            n = a.length;
            t = new long[2 * n];
            for (int i = 0; i < n; i++) t[n + i] = a[i];
            for (int i = n - 1; i > 0; i--) t[i] = t[2 * i] + t[2 * i + 1];
        }
        void set(int i, long v) {
            for (t[i += n] = v; i > 1; i >>= 1) t[i >> 1] = t[i] + t[i ^ 1];
        }
        long query(int l, int r) { // inclusive
            long res = 0;
            for (l += n, r += n + 1; l < r; l >>= 1, r >>= 1) {
                if ((l & 1) == 1) res += t[l++];
                if ((r & 1) == 1) res += t[--r];
            }
            return res;
        }
    }

    // ---------- Lazy segment tree: range add + range sum (recursive) ----------
    static class LazySeg {
        private final long[] sum, lazy;
        private final int n;
        LazySeg(int[] a) {
            n = a.length;
            sum = new long[4 * n];
            lazy = new long[4 * n];
            build(1, 0, n - 1, a);
        }
        private void build(int o, int l, int r, int[] a) {
            if (l == r) { sum[o] = a[l]; return; }
            int m = (l + r) >>> 1;
            build(2 * o, l, m, a);
            build(2 * o + 1, m + 1, r, a);
            sum[o] = sum[2 * o] + sum[2 * o + 1];
        }
        private void push(int o, int l, int r) {
            if (lazy[o] == 0) return;
            int m = (l + r) >>> 1;
            apply(2 * o, l, m, lazy[o]);
            apply(2 * o + 1, m + 1, r, lazy[o]);
            lazy[o] = 0;
        }
        private void apply(int o, int l, int r, long v) { sum[o] += v * (r - l + 1); lazy[o] += v; }
        void add(int ql, int qr, long v) { add(1, 0, n - 1, ql, qr, v); }
        private void add(int o, int l, int r, int ql, int qr, long v) {
            if (qr < l || r < ql) return;
            if (ql <= l && r <= qr) { apply(o, l, r, v); return; }
            push(o, l, r);
            int m = (l + r) >>> 1;
            add(2 * o, l, m, ql, qr, v);
            add(2 * o + 1, m + 1, r, ql, qr, v);
            sum[o] = sum[2 * o] + sum[2 * o + 1];
        }
        long query(int ql, int qr) { return query(1, 0, n - 1, ql, qr); }
        private long query(int o, int l, int r, int ql, int qr) {
            if (qr < l || r < ql) return 0;
            if (ql <= l && r <= qr) return sum[o];
            push(o, l, r);
            int m = (l + r) >>> 1;
            return query(2 * o, l, m, ql, qr) + query(2 * o + 1, m + 1, r, ql, qr);
        }
    }

    // ---------- Sparse table: static range minimum in O(1) after O(n log n) build ----------
    static class SparseTable {
        private final int[][] st;
        private final int[] log;
        SparseTable(int[] a) {
            int n = a.length, K = 1;
            while ((1 << K) <= n) K++;
            st = new int[K][n];
            log = new int[n + 2];
            for (int i = 2; i <= n + 1; i++) log[i] = log[i / 2] + 1;
            st[0] = a.clone();
            for (int k = 1; k < K; k++)
                for (int i = 0; i + (1 << k) <= n; i++) st[k][i] = Math.min(st[k - 1][i], st[k - 1][i + (1 << (k - 1))]);
        }
        int min(int l, int r) {
            int k = log[r - l + 1];
            return Math.min(st[k][l], st[k][r - (1 << k) + 1]);
        }
    }

    // ---------- Skyline (LC 218): sweep + max-heap with lazy deletion ----------
    static List<List<Integer>> getSkyline(int[][] b) {
        List<int[]> ev = new ArrayList<>();
        for (int[] x : b) { ev.add(new int[]{x[0], -x[2]}); ev.add(new int[]{x[1], x[2]}); }
        ev.sort((p, q) -> p[0] != q[0] ? p[0] - q[0] : p[1] - q[1]); // starts (negative) before ends at equal x, taller start first
        TreeMap<Integer, Integer> heights = new TreeMap<>();
        heights.put(0, 1);
        int prev = 0;
        List<List<Integer>> res = new ArrayList<>();
        for (int[] e : ev) {
            if (e[1] < 0) heights.merge(-e[1], 1, Integer::sum);
            else heights.merge(e[1], -1, (a, c) -> a + c == 0 ? null : a + c);
            int cur = heights.lastKey();
            if (cur != prev) { res.add(List.of(e[0], cur)); prev = cur; }
        }
        return res;
    }
}

Pitfalls

  1. Fenwick is 1-indexed internally; the template exposes a 0-indexed API.
  2. Count-smaller problems: compress values to ranks first, then scan right to left.
  3. Skyline: process starts before ends at equal x and keep a TreeMap multiset of heights.
26 — ARRAYSHASHING.JAVA

Arrays & Hashing

The core move is turning a nested loop into one pass plus a map: store what you have seen (value, prefix sum, balance) and look up what you need. Most "count subarrays" problems are prefix sum + frequency map.

PatternUse caseComplexity
Complement mapTwo sum, pair conditionsO(n)
Prefix sum + mapSubarray sum = k, divisible by k, balanced 0/1O(n)
Index as hashFirst missing positive, find duplicateO(n)
In-place passesDutch flag, rotate, next permutation, matricesO(n)
ArraysHashing.java
Two sum, subarray sums, product except self, anagram groups, longest consecutive, top K, cyclic sort, Boyer-Moore, Dutch flag, next permutation, rotations, matrix ops, trapping water
O(n) 
import java.util.*;

public class ArraysHashing {

    // ---------- Two Sum (LC 1): value -> index map, one pass ----------
    static int[] twoSum(int[] a, int target) {
        Map<Integer, Integer> seen = new HashMap<>();
        for (int i = 0; i < a.length; i++) {
            Integer j = seen.get(target - a[i]);
            if (j != null) return new int[]{j, i};
            seen.put(a[i], i);
        }
        return new int[0];
    }

    // ---------- Subarray Sum Equals K (LC 560): prefix-sum frequency map ----------
    // RECOGNIZE: count/exists contiguous subarray with a sum (or sum mod, or balance) condition. Works with negatives.
    static int subarraySum(int[] a, int k) {
        Map<Integer, Integer> freq = new HashMap<>();
        freq.put(0, 1);
        int sum = 0, count = 0;
        for (int x : a) {
            sum += x;
            count += freq.getOrDefault(sum - k, 0);
            freq.merge(sum, 1, Integer::sum);
        }
        return count;
    }

    // Subarray Sums Divisible by K (LC 974): key = prefix mod k, normalized to non-negative
    static int subarraysDivByK(int[] a, int k) {
        int[] cnt = new int[k];
        cnt[0] = 1;
        int sum = 0, res = 0;
        for (int x : a) {
            sum = ((sum + x) % k + k) % k;
            res += cnt[sum]++;
        }
        return res;
    }

    // Contiguous Array (LC 525): map 0 -> -1, longest subarray with sum 0 via first-seen index
    static int findMaxLength(int[] a) {
        Map<Integer, Integer> first = new HashMap<>();
        first.put(0, -1);
        int bal = 0, best = 0;
        for (int i = 0; i < a.length; i++) {
            bal += a[i] == 1 ? 1 : -1;
            Integer j = first.putIfAbsent(bal, i);
            if (j != null) best = Math.max(best, i - j);
        }
        return best;
    }

    // ---------- Product of Array Except Self (LC 238): left pass, then right pass ----------
    static int[] productExceptSelf(int[] a) {
        int n = a.length;
        int[] res = new int[n];
        res[0] = 1;
        for (int i = 1; i < n; i++) res[i] = res[i - 1] * a[i - 1];
        int right = 1;
        for (int i = n - 1; i >= 0; i--) { res[i] *= right; right *= a[i]; }
        return res;
    }

    // ---------- Group Anagrams (LC 49): canonical key = sorted chars (or 26-count signature) ----------
    static List<List<String>> groupAnagrams(String[] strs) {
        Map<String, List<String>> g = new HashMap<>();
        for (String s : strs) {
            char[] c = s.toCharArray();
            Arrays.sort(c);
            g.computeIfAbsent(new String(c), k -> new ArrayList<>()).add(s);
        }
        return new ArrayList<>(g.values());
    }

    // ---------- Longest Consecutive Sequence (LC 128): only start counting at sequence heads ----------
    static int longestConsecutive(int[] a) {
        Set<Integer> set = new HashSet<>();
        for (int x : a) set.add(x);
        int best = 0;
        for (int x : set) {
            if (set.contains(x - 1)) continue;
            int len = 1;
            while (set.contains(x + len)) len++;
            best = Math.max(best, len);
        }
        return best;
    }

    // ---------- Top K Frequent (LC 347): bucket by frequency, O(n) ----------
    static int[] topKFrequent(int[] a, int k) {
        Map<Integer, Integer> f = new HashMap<>();
        for (int x : a) f.merge(x, 1, Integer::sum);
        List<Integer>[] bucket = new List[a.length + 1];
        for (Map.Entry<Integer, Integer> e : f.entrySet()) {
            if (bucket[e.getValue()] == null) bucket[e.getValue()] = new ArrayList<>();
            bucket[e.getValue()].add(e.getKey());
        }
        int[] res = new int[k];
        int p = 0;
        for (int c = a.length; c >= 1 && p < k; c--)
            if (bucket[c] != null) for (int x : bucket[c]) if (p < k) res[p++] = x;
        return res;
    }

    // ---------- Cyclic sort / index-as-hash: First Missing Positive (LC 41), values in 1..n go to index v-1 ----------
    static int firstMissingPositive(int[] a) {
        int n = a.length;
        for (int i = 0; i < n; i++)
            while (a[i] > 0 && a[i] <= n && a[a[i] - 1] != a[i]) {
                int t = a[a[i] - 1]; a[a[i] - 1] = a[i]; a[i] = t;
            }
        for (int i = 0; i < n; i++) if (a[i] != i + 1) return i + 1;
        return n + 1;
    }

    // Find the Duplicate Number (LC 287): Floyd cycle detection on i -> a[i]
    static int findDuplicate(int[] a) {
        int slow = a[0], fast = a[a[0]];
        while (slow != fast) { slow = a[slow]; fast = a[a[fast]]; }
        slow = 0;
        while (slow != fast) { slow = a[slow]; fast = a[fast]; }
        return slow;
    }

    // ---------- Boyer-Moore majority vote (LC 169) ----------
    static int majorityElement(int[] a) {
        int cand = 0, cnt = 0;
        for (int x : a) {
            if (cnt == 0) cand = x;
            cnt += x == cand ? 1 : -1;
        }
        return cand;
    }

    // ---------- Dutch National Flag (LC 75) ----------
    static void sortColors(int[] a) {
        int lo = 0, mid = 0, hi = a.length - 1;
        while (mid <= hi) {
            if (a[mid] == 0) swap(a, lo++, mid++);
            else if (a[mid] == 2) swap(a, mid, hi--);
            else mid++;
        }
    }

    private static void swap(int[] a, int i, int j) { int t = a[i]; a[i] = a[j]; a[j] = t; }

    // ---------- Next Permutation (LC 31) ----------
    static void nextPermutation(int[] a) {
        int i = a.length - 2;
        while (i >= 0 && a[i] >= a[i + 1]) i--;
        if (i >= 0) {
            int j = a.length - 1;
            while (a[j] <= a[i]) j--;
            swap(a, i, j);
        }
        for (int l = i + 1, r = a.length - 1; l < r; l++, r--) swap(a, l, r);
    }

    // ---------- Rotate Array (LC 189): three reversals ----------
    static void rotate(int[] a, int k) {
        int n = a.length;
        k %= n;
        rev(a, 0, n - 1); rev(a, 0, k - 1); rev(a, k, n - 1);
    }

    private static void rev(int[] a, int l, int r) { while (l < r) swap(a, l++, r--); }

    // ---------- Matrix: rotate 90 clockwise (LC 48), spiral (LC 54), set zeroes O(1) (LC 73) ----------
    static void rotateMatrix(int[][] m) {
        int n = m.length;
        for (int i = 0; i < n; i++) for (int j = i + 1; j < n; j++) { int t = m[i][j]; m[i][j] = m[j][i]; m[j][i] = t; }
        for (int[] row : m) rev(row, 0, n - 1);
    }

    static List<Integer> spiralOrder(int[][] m) {
        List<Integer> out = new ArrayList<>();
        int top = 0, bot = m.length - 1, left = 0, right = m[0].length - 1;
        while (top <= bot && left <= right) {
            for (int j = left; j <= right; j++) out.add(m[top][j]);
            top++;
            for (int i = top; i <= bot; i++) out.add(m[i][right]);
            right--;
            if (top <= bot) { for (int j = right; j >= left; j--) out.add(m[bot][j]); bot--; }
            if (left <= right) { for (int i = bot; i >= top; i--) out.add(m[i][left]); left++; }
        }
        return out;
    }

    static void setZeroes(int[][] m) {
        int R = m.length, C = m[0].length;
        boolean row0 = false, col0 = false;
        for (int j = 0; j < C; j++) if (m[0][j] == 0) row0 = true;
        for (int i = 0; i < R; i++) if (m[i][0] == 0) col0 = true;
        for (int i = 1; i < R; i++) for (int j = 1; j < C; j++) if (m[i][j] == 0) { m[i][0] = 0; m[0][j] = 0; }
        for (int i = 1; i < R; i++) for (int j = 1; j < C; j++) if (m[i][0] == 0 || m[0][j] == 0) m[i][j] = 0;
        if (row0) for (int j = 0; j < C; j++) m[0][j] = 0;
        if (col0) for (int i = 0; i < R; i++) m[i][0] = 0;
    }

    // ---------- Reservoir sampling (uniform pick from a stream of unknown length) ----------
    static int reservoirPick(int[] stream, Random rnd) {
        int pick = -1;
        for (int i = 0; i < stream.length; i++) if (rnd.nextInt(i + 1) == 0) pick = stream[i];
        return pick;
    }

    // Fisher-Yates shuffle
    static void shuffle(int[] a, Random rnd) {
        for (int i = a.length - 1; i > 0; i--) swap(a, i, rnd.nextInt(i + 1));
    }

    // ---------- Trapping Rain Water (LC 42): two pointers on the lower wall ----------
    static int trap(int[] h) {
        int l = 0, r = h.length - 1, lm = 0, rm = 0, water = 0;
        while (l < r) {
            if (h[l] < h[r]) { lm = Math.max(lm, h[l]); water += lm - h[l]; l++; }
            else { rm = Math.max(rm, h[r]); water += rm - h[r]; r--; }
        }
        return water;
    }
}

Pitfalls

  1. Prefix-sum maps must be seeded with {0: 1} (or {0: -1} for earliest index).
  2. Negative mod: normalize with ((x % k) + k) % k.
  3. Longest consecutive: only start counting at sequence heads to stay O(n).
27 — SLIDINGWINDOWPOINTERS.JAVA

Sliding Window & Two Pointers

Variable window: grow right, shrink left while invalid. "Longest valid" updates after the shrink; "shortest valid" updates inside the shrink loop; "count subarrays" uses atMost(k) - atMost(k - 1). Two pointers on sorted data move the pointer that cannot improve.

PatternUse caseComplexity
Variable windowLongest / shortest substring or subarray under a conditionO(n)
Exact-K countingatMost(k) minus atMost(k - 1)O(n)
Fixed windowAnagrams, max averageO(n)
Two pointersSorted pair sums, 3Sum, container, dedupe, mergeO(n) after sort
SlidingWindowPointers.java
Longest unique substring, character replacement, minimum window, min subarray length, K distinct, exactly K, ones III, anagrams, 2Sum/3Sum, container, dedupe, merge
O(n) 
import java.util.*;

public class SlidingWindowPointers {

    // ===== VARIABLE WINDOW SKELETON =====
    //   for (r...) { add a[r]; while (window invalid) { remove a[l]; l++; } update answer }
    // "longest valid"  -> update after the shrink loop.
    // "shortest valid" -> update INSIDE a while (window valid) loop that shrinks.
    // "count subarrays with property" -> atMost(k) - atMost(k - 1).

    // Longest Substring Without Repeating (LC 3)
    static int lengthOfLongestSubstring(String s) {
        int[] last = new int[128];
        Arrays.fill(last, -1);
        int l = 0, best = 0;
        for (int r = 0; r < s.length(); r++) {
            char c = s.charAt(r);
            if (last[c] >= l) l = last[c] + 1;
            last[c] = r;
            best = Math.max(best, r - l + 1);
        }
        return best;
    }

    // Longest Repeating Character Replacement (LC 424): window valid iff len - maxFreq <= k (maxFreq never shrinks)
    static int characterReplacement(String s, int k) {
        int[] cnt = new int[26];
        int l = 0, maxFreq = 0, best = 0;
        for (int r = 0; r < s.length(); r++) {
            maxFreq = Math.max(maxFreq, ++cnt[s.charAt(r) - 'A']);
            if (r - l + 1 - maxFreq > k) cnt[s.charAt(l++) - 'A']--;
            best = Math.max(best, r - l + 1);
        }
        return best;
    }

    // Minimum Window Substring (LC 76): shortest window covering all of t
    static String minWindow(String s, String t) {
        int[] need = new int[128];
        for (char c : t.toCharArray()) need[c]++;
        int missing = t.length(), l = 0, bestL = 0, bestLen = Integer.MAX_VALUE;
        for (int r = 0; r < s.length(); r++) {
            if (need[s.charAt(r)]-- > 0) missing--;
            while (missing == 0) {
                if (r - l + 1 < bestLen) { bestLen = r - l + 1; bestL = l; }
                if (++need[s.charAt(l++)] > 0) missing++;
            }
        }
        return bestLen == Integer.MAX_VALUE ? "" : s.substring(bestL, bestL + bestLen);
    }

    // Minimum Size Subarray Sum (LC 209): positives only
    static int minSubArrayLen(int target, int[] a) {
        int l = 0, sum = 0, best = Integer.MAX_VALUE;
        for (int r = 0; r < a.length; r++) {
            sum += a[r];
            while (sum >= target) { best = Math.min(best, r - l + 1); sum -= a[l++]; }
        }
        return best == Integer.MAX_VALUE ? 0 : best;
    }

    // Longest Substring with At Most K Distinct (LC 340 / 159 / fruit baskets 904)
    static int lengthOfLongestSubstringKDistinct(String s, int k) {
        Map<Character, Integer> cnt = new HashMap<>();
        int l = 0, best = 0;
        for (int r = 0; r < s.length(); r++) {
            cnt.merge(s.charAt(r), 1, Integer::sum);
            while (cnt.size() > k) {
                char c = s.charAt(l++);
                if (cnt.merge(c, -1, Integer::sum) == 0) cnt.remove(c);
            }
            best = Math.max(best, r - l + 1);
        }
        return best;
    }

    // Subarrays with exactly K distinct (LC 992) = atMost(K) - atMost(K-1)
    static int subarraysWithKDistinct(int[] a, int k) { return atMostDistinct(a, k) - atMostDistinct(a, k - 1); }

    private static int atMostDistinct(int[] a, int k) {
        if (k < 0) return 0;
        Map<Integer, Integer> cnt = new HashMap<>();
        int l = 0, res = 0;
        for (int r = 0; r < a.length; r++) {
            cnt.merge(a[r], 1, Integer::sum);
            while (cnt.size() > k) {
                if (cnt.merge(a[l], -1, Integer::sum) == 0) cnt.remove(a[l]);
                l++;
            }
            res += r - l + 1; // every subarray ending at r and starting in [l, r]
        }
        return res;
    }

    // Max Consecutive Ones III (LC 1004): at most k zeros flipped
    static int longestOnes(int[] a, int k) {
        int l = 0, zeros = 0, best = 0;
        for (int r = 0; r < a.length; r++) {
            if (a[r] == 0) zeros++;
            while (zeros > k) if (a[l++] == 0) zeros--;
            best = Math.max(best, r - l + 1);
        }
        return best;
    }

    // Permutation in String (LC 567) / Find All Anagrams (LC 438): fixed window, compare counts
    static List<Integer> findAnagrams(String s, String p) {
        List<Integer> res = new ArrayList<>();
        int m = p.length();
        if (s.length() < m) return res;
        int[] diff = new int[26];
        for (int i = 0; i < m; i++) { diff[p.charAt(i) - 'a']--; diff[s.charAt(i) - 'a']++; }
        int nonZero = 0;
        for (int d : diff) if (d != 0) nonZero++;
        if (nonZero == 0) res.add(0);
        for (int r = m; r < s.length(); r++) {
            nonZero += adjust(diff, s.charAt(r) - 'a', 1);
            nonZero += adjust(diff, s.charAt(r - m) - 'a', -1);
            if (nonZero == 0) res.add(r - m + 1);
        }
        return res;
    }

    private static int adjust(int[] d, int i, int delta) {
        int before = d[i] != 0 ? 1 : 0;
        d[i] += delta;
        return (d[i] != 0 ? 1 : 0) - before;
    }

    // Maximum Average Subarray I (LC 643): fixed window sum
    static double findMaxAverage(int[] a, int k) {
        long sum = 0, best;
        for (int i = 0; i < k; i++) sum += a[i];
        best = sum;
        for (int i = k; i < a.length; i++) { sum += a[i] - a[i - k]; best = Math.max(best, sum); }
        return (double) best / k;
    }

    // ===================== TWO POINTERS =====================

    // Two Sum II sorted (LC 167)
    static int[] twoSumSorted(int[] a, int target) {
        int l = 0, r = a.length - 1;
        while (l < r) {
            int s = a[l] + a[r];
            if (s == target) return new int[]{l + 1, r + 1};
            if (s < target) l++; else r--;
        }
        return new int[0];
    }

    // 3Sum (LC 15): sort, fix i, two pointers, skip duplicates
    static List<List<Integer>> threeSum(int[] a) {
        Arrays.sort(a);
        List<List<Integer>> res = new ArrayList<>();
        for (int i = 0; i < a.length - 2; i++) {
            if (i > 0 && a[i] == a[i - 1]) continue;
            int l = i + 1, r = a.length - 1;
            while (l < r) {
                int s = a[i] + a[l] + a[r];
                if (s < 0) l++;
                else if (s > 0) r--;
                else {
                    res.add(List.of(a[i], a[l], a[r]));
                    l++; r--;
                    while (l < r && a[l] == a[l - 1]) l++;
                    while (l < r && a[r] == a[r + 1]) r--;
                }
            }
        }
        return res;
    }

    // 3Sum Closest (LC 16)
    static int threeSumClosest(int[] a, int target) {
        Arrays.sort(a);
        int best = a[0] + a[1] + a[2];
        for (int i = 0; i < a.length - 2; i++) {
            int l = i + 1, r = a.length - 1;
            while (l < r) {
                int s = a[i] + a[l] + a[r];
                if (Math.abs(s - target) < Math.abs(best - target)) best = s;
                if (s < target) l++; else if (s > target) r--; else return s;
            }
        }
        return best;
    }

    // Container With Most Water (LC 11): move the shorter wall
    static int maxArea(int[] h) {
        int l = 0, r = h.length - 1, best = 0;
        while (l < r) {
            best = Math.max(best, Math.min(h[l], h[r]) * (r - l));
            if (h[l] < h[r]) l++; else r--;
        }
        return best;
    }

    // Remove Duplicates from Sorted Array (LC 26): read/write pointers
    static int removeDuplicates(int[] a) {
        if (a.length == 0) return 0;
        int w = 1;
        for (int r = 1; r < a.length; r++) if (a[r] != a[w - 1]) a[w++] = a[r];
        return w;
    }

    // Move Zeroes (LC 283)
    static void moveZeroes(int[] a) {
        int w = 0;
        for (int x : a) if (x != 0) a[w++] = x;
        while (w < a.length) a[w++] = 0;
    }

    // Valid Palindrome (LC 125)
    static boolean isPalindrome(String s) {
        int l = 0, r = s.length() - 1;
        while (l < r) {
            while (l < r && !Character.isLetterOrDigit(s.charAt(l))) l++;
            while (l < r && !Character.isLetterOrDigit(s.charAt(r))) r--;
            if (Character.toLowerCase(s.charAt(l++)) != Character.toLowerCase(s.charAt(r--))) return false;
        }
        return true;
    }

    // Merge sorted arrays in place from the back (LC 88)
    static void merge(int[] a, int m, int[] b, int n) {
        int i = m - 1, j = n - 1, k = m + n - 1;
        while (j >= 0) a[k--] = i >= 0 && a[i] > b[j] ? a[i--] : b[j--];
    }

    // Subarray Product Less Than K (LC 713)
    static int numSubarrayProductLessThanK(int[] a, int k) {
        if (k <= 1) return 0;
        int l = 0, res = 0;
        long prod = 1;
        for (int r = 0; r < a.length; r++) {
            prod *= a[r];
            while (prod >= k) prod /= a[l++];
            res += r - l + 1;
        }
        return res;
    }
}

Pitfalls

  1. Sliding window with negatives breaks the "shrink" logic; use prefix sums + map or a deque instead.
  2. 3Sum: skip duplicates for the fixed element AND after recording a triple.
  3. Minimum window: track missing as a count so validity is O(1) per step.
28 — MONOTONICSTRUCTURES.JAVA

Monotonic Stack, Deque & Parsers

A monotonic stack answers "nearest bigger/smaller element" for every index: pop while the new element breaks the order and the pop IS the answer. A monotonic deque keeps the window maximum. A plain stack parses brackets, calculators and nested encodings.

PatternUse caseComplexity
Next greater / smallerDaily temperatures, next greater elementO(n)
HistogramLargest rectangle, maximal rectangle, subarray minimumsO(n)
Monotonic dequeSliding window max, shortest subarray with negativesO(n)
Stack parsersParentheses, calculators, decode string, RPNO(n)
MonotonicStructures.java
Next greater (plain + circular), daily temps, histogram, maximal rectangle, subarray minimums, remove K digits, stock span, parsers, min stack, sliding max, shortest subarray, limit window
O(n) 
import java.util.*;

public class MonotonicStructures {

    // ===== MONOTONIC STACK =====
    // Question it answers: "for each i, where is the nearest element to the left/right that is bigger/smaller?"
    // Keep indices on the stack in monotone order; pop while the new element breaks the order -- the pop IS the answer.

    // Next Greater Element (LC 496 / 503 circular via 2n pass)
    static int[] nextGreater(int[] a) {
        int n = a.length;
        int[] res = new int[n];
        Arrays.fill(res, -1);
        Deque<Integer> st = new ArrayDeque<>(); // indices with decreasing values
        for (int i = 0; i < n; i++) {
            while (!st.isEmpty() && a[st.peek()] < a[i]) res[st.pop()] = a[i];
            st.push(i);
        }
        return res;
    }

    static int[] nextGreaterCircular(int[] a) {
        int n = a.length;
        int[] res = new int[n];
        Arrays.fill(res, -1);
        Deque<Integer> st = new ArrayDeque<>();
        for (int i = 0; i < 2 * n; i++) {
            int x = a[i % n];
            while (!st.isEmpty() && a[st.peek()] < x) res[st.pop()] = x;
            if (i < n) st.push(i);
        }
        return res;
    }

    // Daily Temperatures (LC 739): distance to next warmer day
    static int[] dailyTemperatures(int[] t) {
        int[] res = new int[t.length];
        Deque<Integer> st = new ArrayDeque<>();
        for (int i = 0; i < t.length; i++) {
            while (!st.isEmpty() && t[st.peek()] < t[i]) { int j = st.pop(); res[j] = i - j; }
            st.push(i);
        }
        return res;
    }

    // Largest Rectangle in Histogram (LC 84): sentinel 0 at the end flushes the stack
    static int largestRectangleArea(int[] h) {
        Deque<Integer> st = new ArrayDeque<>();
        int best = 0;
        for (int i = 0; i <= h.length; i++) {
            int cur = i == h.length ? 0 : h[i];
            while (!st.isEmpty() && h[st.peek()] >= cur) {
                int height = h[st.pop()];
                int left = st.isEmpty() ? -1 : st.peek();
                best = Math.max(best, height * (i - left - 1));
            }
            st.push(i);
        }
        return best;
    }

    // Maximal Rectangle (LC 85): histogram per row
    static int maximalRectangle(char[][] g) {
        if (g.length == 0) return 0;
        int[] h = new int[g[0].length];
        int best = 0;
        for (char[] row : g) {
            for (int j = 0; j < row.length; j++) h[j] = row[j] == '1' ? h[j] + 1 : 0;
            best = Math.max(best, largestRectangleArea(h));
        }
        return best;
    }

    // Sum of Subarray Minimums (LC 907): contribution = a[i] * (i - prevLess) * (nextLessOrEqual - i)
    static int sumSubarrayMins(int[] a) {
        int n = a.length;
        long MOD = 1_000_000_007L, res = 0;
        int[] left = new int[n], right = new int[n];
        Deque<Integer> st = new ArrayDeque<>();
        for (int i = 0; i < n; i++) {
            while (!st.isEmpty() && a[st.peek()] > a[i]) st.pop();
            left[i] = st.isEmpty() ? -1 : st.peek();
            st.push(i);
        }
        st.clear();
        for (int i = n - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
            while (!st.isEmpty() && a[st.peek()] >= a[i]) st.pop();
            right[i] = st.isEmpty() ? n : st.peek();
            st.push(i);
        }
        for (int i = 0; i < n; i++) res = (res + (long) a[i] * (i - left[i]) % MOD * (right[i] - i)) % MOD;
        return (int) res;
    }

    // Remove K Digits (LC 402) / smallest subsequence: greedy with stack
    static String removeKdigits(String num, int k) {
        StringBuilder st = new StringBuilder();
        for (char c : num.toCharArray()) {
            while (k > 0 && st.length() > 0 && st.charAt(st.length() - 1) > c) { st.setLength(st.length() - 1); k--; }
            st.append(c);
        }
        st.setLength(Math.max(0, st.length() - k));
        int i = 0;
        while (i < st.length() - 1 && st.charAt(i) == '0') i++;
        String res = st.substring(i);
        return res.isEmpty() ? "0" : res;
    }

    // Online Stock Span (LC 901)
    static class StockSpanner {
        private final Deque<int[]> st = new ArrayDeque<>(); // {price, span}
        int next(int price) {
            int span = 1;
            while (!st.isEmpty() && st.peek()[0] <= price) span += st.pop()[1];
            st.push(new int[]{price, span});
            return span;
        }
    }

    // ===== STACK AS PARSER =====
    // Valid Parentheses (LC 20)
    static boolean isValid(String s) {
        Deque<Character> st = new ArrayDeque<>();
        for (char c : s.toCharArray()) {
            if (c == '(') st.push(')'); else if (c == '[') st.push(']'); else if (c == '{') st.push('}');
            else if (st.isEmpty() || st.pop() != c) return false;
        }
        return st.isEmpty();
    }

    // Longest Valid Parentheses (LC 32): stack of indices with a base sentinel
    static int longestValidParentheses(String s) {
        Deque<Integer> st = new ArrayDeque<>();
        st.push(-1);
        int best = 0;
        for (int i = 0; i < s.length(); i++) {
            if (s.charAt(i) == '(') st.push(i);
            else {
                st.pop();
                if (st.isEmpty()) st.push(i); else best = Math.max(best, i - st.peek());
            }
        }
        return best;
    }

    // Basic Calculator I (LC 224): + - ( ) with a sign stack
    static int calculate(String s) {
        Deque<Integer> st = new ArrayDeque<>();
        int res = 0, num = 0, sign = 1;
        for (char c : s.toCharArray()) {
            if (Character.isDigit(c)) num = num * 10 + (c - '0');
            else if (c == '+' || c == '-') { res += sign * num; num = 0; sign = c == '+' ? 1 : -1; }
            else if (c == '(') { st.push(res); st.push(sign); res = 0; sign = 1; }
            else if (c == ')') { res += sign * num; num = 0; res *= st.pop(); res += st.pop(); }
        }
        return res + sign * num;
    }

    // Basic Calculator II (LC 227): + - * / precedence
    static int calculate2(String s) {
        Deque<Integer> st = new ArrayDeque<>();
        int num = 0;
        char op = '+';
        for (int i = 0; i <= s.length(); i++) {
            char c = i < s.length() ? s.charAt(i) : '+';
            if (Character.isDigit(c)) { num = num * 10 + (c - '0'); continue; }
            if (c == ' ') continue;
            if (op == '+') st.push(num);
            else if (op == '-') st.push(-num);
            else if (op == '*') st.push(st.pop() * num);
            else st.push(st.pop() / num);
            op = c;
            num = 0;
        }
        int sum = 0;
        for (int x : st) sum += x;
        return sum;
    }

    // Decode String (LC 394): "3[a2[c]]" -> "accaccacc"
    static String decodeString(String s) {
        Deque<StringBuilder> strs = new ArrayDeque<>();
        Deque<Integer> counts = new ArrayDeque<>();
        StringBuilder cur = new StringBuilder();
        int k = 0;
        for (char c : s.toCharArray()) {
            if (Character.isDigit(c)) k = k * 10 + (c - '0');
            else if (c == '[') { counts.push(k); strs.push(cur); cur = new StringBuilder(); k = 0; }
            else if (c == ']') {
                StringBuilder prev = strs.pop();
                for (int t = counts.pop(); t > 0; t--) prev.append(cur);
                cur = prev;
            } else cur.append(c);
        }
        return cur.toString();
    }

    // Evaluate RPN (LC 150)
    static int evalRPN(String[] tokens) {
        Deque<Integer> st = new ArrayDeque<>();
        for (String t : tokens) {
            switch (t) {
                case "+": st.push(st.pop() + st.pop()); break;
                case "*": st.push(st.pop() * st.pop()); break;
                case "-": { int b = st.pop(), a = st.pop(); st.push(a - b); break; }
                case "/": { int b = st.pop(), a = st.pop(); st.push(a / b); break; }
                default: st.push(Integer.parseInt(t));
            }
        }
        return st.pop();
    }

    // Min Stack (LC 155): store (value, min so far)
    static class MinStack {
        private final Deque<int[]> st = new ArrayDeque<>();
        void push(int x) { st.push(new int[]{x, st.isEmpty() ? x : Math.min(x, st.peek()[1])}); }
        void pop() { st.pop(); }
        int top() { return st.peek()[0]; }
        int getMin() { return st.peek()[1]; }
    }

    // ===== MONOTONIC DEQUE: sliding window maximum (LC 239) =====
    static int[] maxSlidingWindow(int[] a, int k) {
        int n = a.length;
        int[] res = new int[n - k + 1];
        Deque<Integer> dq = new ArrayDeque<>(); // indices, values decreasing
        for (int i = 0; i < n; i++) {
            while (!dq.isEmpty() && dq.peekFirst() <= i - k) dq.pollFirst();
            while (!dq.isEmpty() && a[dq.peekLast()] <= a[i]) dq.pollLast();
            dq.addLast(i);
            if (i >= k - 1) res[i - k + 1] = a[dq.peekFirst()];
        }
        return res;
    }

    // Shortest Subarray with Sum at Least K (LC 862): negatives allowed -> monotonic deque over prefix sums
    static int shortestSubarray(int[] a, int k) {
        int n = a.length;
        long[] p = new long[n + 1];
        for (int i = 0; i < n; i++) p[i + 1] = p[i] + a[i];
        Deque<Integer> dq = new ArrayDeque<>();
        int best = Integer.MAX_VALUE;
        for (int i = 0; i <= n; i++) {
            while (!dq.isEmpty() && p[i] - p[dq.peekFirst()] >= k) best = Math.min(best, i - dq.pollFirst());
            while (!dq.isEmpty() && p[dq.peekLast()] >= p[i]) dq.pollLast();
            dq.addLast(i);
        }
        return best == Integer.MAX_VALUE ? -1 : best;
    }

    // Longest Subarray with |max - min| <= limit (LC 1438): two deques
    static int longestSubarray(int[] a, int limit) {
        Deque<Integer> mx = new ArrayDeque<>(), mn = new ArrayDeque<>();
        int l = 0, best = 0;
        for (int r = 0; r < a.length; r++) {
            while (!mx.isEmpty() && mx.peekLast() < a[r]) mx.pollLast();
            while (!mn.isEmpty() && mn.peekLast() > a[r]) mn.pollLast();
            mx.addLast(a[r]);
            mn.addLast(a[r]);
            while (mx.peekFirst() - mn.peekFirst() > limit) {
                if (mx.peekFirst() == a[l]) mx.pollFirst();
                if (mn.peekFirst() == a[l]) mn.pollFirst();
                l++;
            }
            best = Math.max(best, r - l + 1);
        }
        return best;
    }
}

Pitfalls

  1. Use strict vs non-strict comparison on exactly one side to avoid double-counting equal elements (sum of subarray minimums).
  2. Append a sentinel 0 height to flush the stack in the histogram problem.
  3. Deque stores indices so expired elements can be dropped from the front.
29 — HEAPSTOPK.JAVA

Heaps & Top-K

For K largest keep a MIN-heap of size K; for K smallest keep a MAX-heap. Two heaps track a median. K-way merge pushes the next element of whichever list just emitted. Greedy scheduling problems often pair a sort with a heap.

PatternUse caseComplexity
Top KK largest / closest / most frequentO(n log k)
QuickselectKth element, average linearO(n) avg
Two heapsRunning medianO(log n) per add
K-way mergeMerge sorted lists, kth in sorted matrix, smallest rangeO(N log k)
HeapsTopK.java
Kth largest (heap + quickselect), K closest, top words, median finder, K-way merge, task scheduler, reorganize string, meeting rooms, IPO, furthest building, ugly numbers
O(n log k) 
import java.util.*;

public class HeapsTopK {

    // ---------- Kth Largest: keep a MIN-heap of size k (LC 215) ----------
    // RECOGNIZE: "k largest / smallest / most frequent / closest". Heap of size k -> O(n log k).
    static int findKthLargest(int[] a, int k) {
        PriorityQueue<Integer> pq = new PriorityQueue<>();
        for (int x : a) { pq.add(x); if (pq.size() > k) pq.poll(); }
        return pq.peek();
    }

    // Quickselect: average O(n), worst O(n^2) -- randomize the pivot
    static int quickselectKthLargest(int[] a, int k) {
        int[] b = a.clone();
        int target = b.length - k, lo = 0, hi = b.length - 1;
        Random rnd = new Random(1);
        while (lo < hi) {
            int p = b[lo + rnd.nextInt(hi - lo + 1)], i = lo, j = hi;
            while (i <= j) { // Hoare-style three-way-safe partition
                while (b[i] < p) i++;
                while (b[j] > p) j--;
                if (i <= j) { int t = b[i]; b[i] = b[j]; b[j] = t; i++; j--; }
            }
            if (target <= j) hi = j; else if (target >= i) lo = i; else return b[target];
        }
        return b[target];
    }

    // K Closest Points to Origin (LC 973): MAX-heap of size k on distance
    static int[][] kClosest(int[][] pts, int k) {
        PriorityQueue<int[]> pq = new PriorityQueue<>((a, b) -> Long.compare(d2(b), d2(a)));
        for (int[] p : pts) { pq.add(p); if (pq.size() > k) pq.poll(); }
        return pq.toArray(new int[0][]);
    }

    private static long d2(int[] p) { return (long) p[0] * p[0] + (long) p[1] * p[1]; }

    // Top K Frequent Words (LC 692): tie-break alphabetical
    static List<String> topKFrequentWords(String[] words, int k) {
        Map<String, Integer> f = new HashMap<>();
        for (String w : words) f.merge(w, 1, Integer::sum);
        PriorityQueue<String> pq = new PriorityQueue<>((a, b) -> f.get(a).equals(f.get(b)) ? b.compareTo(a) : f.get(a) - f.get(b));
        for (String w : f.keySet()) { pq.add(w); if (pq.size() > k) pq.poll(); }
        LinkedList<String> res = new LinkedList<>();
        while (!pq.isEmpty()) res.addFirst(pq.poll());
        return res;
    }

    // ---------- Two heaps: Find Median from Data Stream (LC 295) ----------
    static class MedianFinder {
        private final PriorityQueue<Integer> lo = new PriorityQueue<>(Collections.reverseOrder()); // lower half
        private final PriorityQueue<Integer> hi = new PriorityQueue<>();                           // upper half
        void addNum(int x) {
            lo.add(x);
            hi.add(lo.poll());
            if (hi.size() > lo.size()) lo.add(hi.poll());
        }
        double findMedian() { return lo.size() > hi.size() ? lo.peek() : (lo.peek() + (double) hi.peek()) / 2; }
    }

    // Sliding Window Median (LC 480): TreeMap-free version with two multiset heaps + lazy delete is long;
    // this simple O(n k) insertion version is the safe interview baseline.
    static double[] medianSlidingWindow(int[] a, int k) {
        double[] res = new double[a.length - k + 1];
        List<Integer> win = new ArrayList<>();
        for (int i = 0; i < a.length; i++) {
            int pos = Collections.binarySearch(win, a[i]);
            win.add(pos < 0 ? -pos - 1 : pos, a[i]);
            if (i >= k) win.remove(Collections.binarySearch(win, a[i - k]));
            if (i >= k - 1)
                res[i - k + 1] = k % 2 == 1 ? win.get(k / 2) : ((long) win.get(k / 2 - 1) + win.get(k / 2)) / 2.0;
        }
        return res;
    }

    // ---------- Merge K sorted lists / arrays (LC 23): heap of (value, list, index) ----------
    static List<Integer> mergeKSorted(List<int[]> lists) {
        PriorityQueue<int[]> pq = new PriorityQueue<>((x, y) -> x[0] - y[0]); // {value, listIdx, elemIdx}
        for (int i = 0; i < lists.size(); i++) if (lists.get(i).length > 0) pq.add(new int[]{lists.get(i)[0], i, 0});
        List<Integer> out = new ArrayList<>();
        while (!pq.isEmpty()) {
            int[] t = pq.poll();
            out.add(t[0]);
            int[] src = lists.get(t[1]);
            if (t[2] + 1 < src.length) pq.add(new int[]{src[t[2] + 1], t[1], t[2] + 1});
        }
        return out;
    }

    // Kth Smallest in Sorted Matrix (LC 378)
    static int kthSmallest(int[][] m, int k) {
        int n = m.length;
        PriorityQueue<int[]> pq = new PriorityQueue<>((a, b) -> a[0] - b[0]);
        for (int i = 0; i < Math.min(n, k); i++) pq.add(new int[]{m[i][0], i, 0});
        int v = 0;
        while (k-- > 0) {
            int[] t = pq.poll();
            v = t[0];
            if (t[2] + 1 < n) pq.add(new int[]{m[t[1]][t[2] + 1], t[1], t[2] + 1});
        }
        return v;
    }

    // Smallest Range Covering Elements from K Lists (LC 632)
    static int[] smallestRange(List<List<Integer>> nums) {
        PriorityQueue<int[]> pq = new PriorityQueue<>((a, b) -> a[0] - b[0]);
        int max = Integer.MIN_VALUE;
        for (int i = 0; i < nums.size(); i++) { pq.add(new int[]{nums.get(i).get(0), i, 0}); max = Math.max(max, nums.get(i).get(0)); }
        int[] best = {pq.peek()[0], max};
        while (true) {
            int[] t = pq.poll();
            if (max - t[0] < best[1] - best[0]) best = new int[]{t[0], max};
            if (t[2] + 1 == nums.get(t[1]).size()) break;
            int nv = nums.get(t[1]).get(t[2] + 1);
            max = Math.max(max, nv);
            pq.add(new int[]{nv, t[1], t[2] + 1});
        }
        return best;
    }

    // ---------- Greedy with heap ----------
    // Task Scheduler (LC 621): closed form
    static int leastInterval(char[] tasks, int n) {
        int[] c = new int[26];
        int max = 0, maxCount = 0;
        for (char t : tasks) max = Math.max(max, ++c[t - 'A']);
        for (int x : c) if (x == max) maxCount++;
        return Math.max(tasks.length, (max - 1) * (n + 1) + maxCount);
    }

    // Reorganize String (LC 767): always place the most frequent char that is not the previous one
    static String reorganizeString(String s) {
        int[] c = new int[26];
        for (char ch : s.toCharArray()) c[ch - 'a']++;
        PriorityQueue<int[]> pq = new PriorityQueue<>((a, b) -> b[1] - a[1]);
        for (int i = 0; i < 26; i++) if (c[i] > 0) pq.add(new int[]{i, c[i]});
        StringBuilder sb = new StringBuilder();
        int[] prev = null;
        while (!pq.isEmpty()) {
            int[] cur = pq.poll();
            sb.append((char) ('a' + cur[0]));
            if (prev != null && prev[1] > 0) pq.add(prev);
            cur[1]--;
            prev = cur;
        }
        return sb.length() == s.length() ? sb.toString() : "";
    }

    // Meeting Rooms II (LC 253): min-heap of end times
    static int minMeetingRooms(int[][] iv) {
        Arrays.sort(iv, (a, b) -> a[0] - b[0]);
        PriorityQueue<Integer> ends = new PriorityQueue<>();
        for (int[] i : iv) {
            if (!ends.isEmpty() && ends.peek() <= i[0]) ends.poll();
            ends.add(i[1]);
        }
        return ends.size();
    }

    // IPO (LC 502): sort by capital, max-heap of affordable profits
    static int findMaximizedCapital(int k, int w, int[] profits, int[] capital) {
        int n = profits.length;
        Integer[] idx = new Integer[n];
        for (int i = 0; i < n; i++) idx[i] = i;
        Arrays.sort(idx, Comparator.comparingInt(i -> capital[i]));
        PriorityQueue<Integer> pq = new PriorityQueue<>(Collections.reverseOrder());
        int p = 0;
        while (k-- > 0) {
            while (p < n && capital[idx[p]] <= w) pq.add(profits[idx[p++]]);
            if (pq.isEmpty()) break;
            w += pq.poll();
        }
        return w;
    }

    // Furthest Building You Can Reach (LC 1642): spend ladders on the biggest gaps (min-heap of ladder gaps)
    static int furthestBuilding(int[] h, int bricks, int ladders) {
        PriorityQueue<Integer> used = new PriorityQueue<>();
        for (int i = 0; i + 1 < h.length; i++) {
            int d = h[i + 1] - h[i];
            if (d <= 0) continue;
            used.add(d);
            if (used.size() > ladders) bricks -= used.poll();
            if (bricks < 0) return i;
        }
        return h.length - 1;
    }

    // Ugly Number II (LC 264): three pointers (heap-free)
    static int nthUglyNumber(int n) {
        int[] u = new int[n];
        u[0] = 1;
        int i2 = 0, i3 = 0, i5 = 0;
        for (int i = 1; i < n; i++) {
            int next = Math.min(u[i2] * 2, Math.min(u[i3] * 3, u[i5] * 5));
            u[i] = next;
            if (next == u[i2] * 2) i2++;
            if (next == u[i3] * 3) i3++;
            if (next == u[i5] * 5) i5++;
        }
        return u[n - 1];
    }

    // Lazy-deletion heap pattern: remember what is deleted in a map, discard stale tops when peeking.
    // (see Dijkstra "if (d > dist[u]) continue" in the graphs topic -- same idea)
}

Pitfalls

  1. Heap of size K for K largest is a MIN-heap; people reach for a max-heap by reflex.
  2. PriorityQueue has no decrease-key: push duplicates and skip stale entries on pop.
  3. Comparator subtraction can overflow; use Integer.compare for large values.
30 — INTERVALS.JAVA

Intervals & Sweep Line

First decide the sort key: by START for merging, inserting and overlap counting; by END for "keep the most / fewest arrows" greedy. Sweep line turns intervals into +1/-1 events; TreeMap handles online booking.

PatternUse caseComplexity
Merge / insertSort by start, extend the last output intervalO(n log n)
Max non-overlappingSort by END, keep the earliest finisherO(n log n)
Sweep line+1 at start, -1 at end, track the running maxO(n log n)
Online bookingTreeMap floor / ceiling checksO(log n)
Intervals.java
Merge, insert, non-overlapping, arrows, intersections, meeting rooms, sweep, car pooling, calendar I/III, employee free time, covered intervals, summary ranges
O(n log n) 
import java.util.*;

public class Intervals {

    // ALWAYS first decide: sort by START (merge, insert, overlaps) or by END (max non-overlapping, min arrows).

    // Merge Intervals (LC 56)
    static int[][] merge(int[][] iv) {
        Arrays.sort(iv, (a, b) -> Integer.compare(a[0], b[0]));
        List<int[]> out = new ArrayList<>();
        for (int[] cur : iv) {
            if (out.isEmpty() || out.get(out.size() - 1)[1] < cur[0]) out.add(cur.clone());
            else out.get(out.size() - 1)[1] = Math.max(out.get(out.size() - 1)[1], cur[1]);
        }
        return out.toArray(new int[0][]);
    }

    // Insert Interval (LC 57): before / overlapping / after, one pass
    static int[][] insert(int[][] iv, int[] add) {
        List<int[]> out = new ArrayList<>();
        int i = 0, n = iv.length;
        while (i < n && iv[i][1] < add[0]) out.add(iv[i++]);
        int[] cur = add.clone();
        while (i < n && iv[i][0] <= cur[1]) { cur[0] = Math.min(cur[0], iv[i][0]); cur[1] = Math.max(cur[1], iv[i][1]); i++; }
        out.add(cur);
        while (i < n) out.add(iv[i++]);
        return out.toArray(new int[0][]);
    }

    // Non-overlapping Intervals (LC 435): keep the interval with the EARLIEST END; count removals
    static int eraseOverlapIntervals(int[][] iv) {
        Arrays.sort(iv, (a, b) -> Integer.compare(a[1], b[1]));
        int removed = 0, end = Integer.MIN_VALUE;
        for (int[] x : iv) {
            if (x[0] >= end) end = x[1]; else removed++;
        }
        return removed;
    }

    // Minimum Arrows to Burst Balloons (LC 452): sort by end, one arrow per cluster
    static int findMinArrowShots(int[][] pts) {
        Arrays.sort(pts, (a, b) -> Integer.compare(a[1], b[1]));
        int arrows = 0;
        long end = Long.MIN_VALUE;
        for (int[] p : pts) if (p[0] > end) { arrows++; end = p[1]; }
        return arrows;
    }

    // Interval List Intersections (LC 986): two pointers, advance the one that ends first
    static int[][] intervalIntersection(int[][] a, int[][] b) {
        List<int[]> out = new ArrayList<>();
        int i = 0, j = 0;
        while (i < a.length && j < b.length) {
            int lo = Math.max(a[i][0], b[j][0]), hi = Math.min(a[i][1], b[j][1]);
            if (lo <= hi) out.add(new int[]{lo, hi});
            if (a[i][1] < b[j][1]) i++; else j++;
        }
        return out.toArray(new int[0][]);
    }

    // Meeting Rooms (LC 252): any overlap?
    static boolean canAttendMeetings(int[][] iv) {
        Arrays.sort(iv, (a, b) -> a[0] - b[0]);
        for (int i = 1; i < iv.length; i++) if (iv[i][0] < iv[i - 1][1]) return false;
        return true;
    }

    // ---------- Sweep line with events: max concurrent overlap (rooms) ----------
    // Ends sort BEFORE starts at the same time when [s, e) is half-open.
    static int maxOverlap(int[][] iv) {
        int[] starts = new int[iv.length], ends = new int[iv.length];
        for (int i = 0; i < iv.length; i++) { starts[i] = iv[i][0]; ends[i] = iv[i][1]; }
        Arrays.sort(starts);
        Arrays.sort(ends);
        int e = 0, cur = 0, best = 0;
        for (int s : starts) {
            while (ends[e] <= s) { e++; cur--; }
            cur++;
            best = Math.max(best, cur);
        }
        return best;
    }

    // Car Pooling (LC 1094): difference array over positions
    static boolean carPooling(int[][] trips, int capacity) {
        int[] diff = new int[1002];
        for (int[] t : trips) { diff[t[1]] += t[0]; diff[t[2]] -= t[0]; }
        int cur = 0;
        for (int d : diff) { cur += d; if (cur > capacity) return false; }
        return true;
    }

    // My Calendar I (LC 729): TreeMap floor / ceiling check
    static class MyCalendar {
        private final TreeMap<Integer, Integer> m = new TreeMap<>(); // start -> end
        boolean book(int s, int e) {
            Integer prev = m.floorKey(s), next = m.ceilingKey(s);
            if ((prev != null && m.get(prev) > s) || (next != null && next < e)) return false;
            m.put(s, e);
            return true;
        }
    }

    // My Calendar III (LC 732): max k-booking via TreeMap of +1 / -1 deltas
    static class MyCalendarThree {
        private final TreeMap<Integer, Integer> delta = new TreeMap<>();
        int book(int s, int e) {
            delta.merge(s, 1, Integer::sum);
            delta.merge(e, -1, Integer::sum);
            int cur = 0, best = 0;
            for (int d : delta.values()) { cur += d; best = Math.max(best, cur); }
            return best;
        }
    }

    // Employee Free Time (LC 759): merge all busy intervals, gaps are free
    static List<int[]> employeeFreeTime(List<List<int[]>> schedule) {
        List<int[]> all = new ArrayList<>();
        for (List<int[]> s : schedule) all.addAll(s);
        all.sort((a, b) -> a[0] - b[0]);
        List<int[]> free = new ArrayList<>();
        int end = all.get(0)[1];
        for (int[] x : all) {
            if (x[0] > end) free.add(new int[]{end, x[0]});
            end = Math.max(end, x[1]);
        }
        return free;
    }

    // Remove Covered Intervals (LC 1288)
    static int removeCoveredIntervals(int[][] iv) {
        Arrays.sort(iv, (a, b) -> a[0] != b[0] ? a[0] - b[0] : b[1] - a[1]);
        int count = 0, maxEnd = 0;
        for (int[] x : iv) if (x[1] > maxEnd) { count++; maxEnd = x[1]; }
        return count;
    }

    // Data Stream as Disjoint Intervals (LC 352)
    static class SummaryRanges {
        private final TreeMap<Integer, Integer> m = new TreeMap<>(); // start -> end
        void addNum(int v) {
            Integer lo = m.floorKey(v);
            if (lo != null && m.get(lo) >= v) return;
            boolean joinLeft = lo != null && m.get(lo) == v - 1, joinRight = m.containsKey(v + 1);
            if (joinLeft && joinRight) { m.put(lo, m.remove(v + 1)); }
            else if (joinLeft) m.put(lo, v);
            else if (joinRight) { m.put(v, m.remove(v + 1)); }
            else m.put(v, v);
        }
        int[][] getIntervals() {
            int[][] r = new int[m.size()][];
            int i = 0;
            for (Map.Entry<Integer, Integer> e : m.entrySet()) r[i++] = new int[]{e.getKey(), e.getValue()};
            return r;
        }
    }
}

Pitfalls

  1. Half-open [s, e): process ends before starts at the same timestamp.
  2. Touching intervals: decide up front whether [1,2] and [2,3] overlap.
  3. Insert interval needs no sort when the input is already sorted: three phases in one pass.
31 — BINARYSEARCH.JAVA

Binary Search

One template covers everything: find the FIRST index in [lo, hi] where a monotone predicate ok(i) becomes true, with `while (lo < hi)` and `hi = mid` on true. Searching on the answer is the same loop with ok(x) = "is x feasible?".

PatternUse caseComplexity
BoundslowerBound / upperBound, first and last positionO(log n)
Rotated arraysOne half is always sortedO(log n)
Search on answerMin speed / capacity / days such that feasibleO(n log range)
Partition searchMedian of two sorted arraysO(log min(m, n))
BinarySearch.java
Lower/upper bound, range, rotated search and min, peak, matrices, Koko, shipping, split array, bouquets, multiplication table, two-array median, sqrt, time map, K closest
O(log n) 
import java.util.*;

public class BinarySearch {

    // ===== THE ONE TEMPLATE: find the FIRST index in [lo, hi] where ok(i) is true (ok is monotone F..F T..T) =====
    //   lo = 0, hi = n (n means "none true"); while (lo < hi) { mid = lo + (hi - lo) / 2; if (ok(mid)) hi = mid; else lo = mid + 1; }
    //   Every problem = choose the search space + write ok(). "Last true" = (first true of the negation) - 1.

    // lower bound: first index with a[i] >= x
    static int lowerBound(int[] a, int x) {
        int lo = 0, hi = a.length;
        while (lo < hi) {
            int mid = lo + (hi - lo) / 2;
            if (a[mid] >= x) hi = mid; else lo = mid + 1;
        }
        return lo;
    }

    // upper bound: first index with a[i] > x
    static int upperBound(int[] a, int x) {
        int lo = 0, hi = a.length;
        while (lo < hi) {
            int mid = lo + (hi - lo) / 2;
            if (a[mid] > x) hi = mid; else lo = mid + 1;
        }
        return lo;
    }

    // Find First and Last Position (LC 34)
    static int[] searchRange(int[] a, int target) {
        int l = lowerBound(a, target);
        if (l == a.length || a[l] != target) return new int[]{-1, -1};
        return new int[]{l, upperBound(a, target) - 1};
    }

    // Search Insert Position (LC 35)
    static int searchInsert(int[] a, int target) { return lowerBound(a, target); }

    // ---------- Rotated sorted array (LC 33, distinct): one half is always sorted ----------
    static int searchRotated(int[] a, int t) {
        int lo = 0, hi = a.length - 1;
        while (lo <= hi) {
            int mid = lo + (hi - lo) / 2;
            if (a[mid] == t) return mid;
            if (a[lo] <= a[mid]) { // left half sorted
                if (a[lo] <= t && t < a[mid]) hi = mid - 1; else lo = mid + 1;
            } else {
                if (a[mid] < t && t <= a[hi]) lo = mid + 1; else hi = mid - 1;
            }
        }
        return -1;
    }
