    // Find Minimum in Rotated Sorted Array (LC 153); duplicates (LC 154) shrink hi on ties
    static int findMin(int[] a) {
        int lo = 0, hi = a.length - 1;
        while (lo < hi) {
            int mid = lo + (hi - lo) / 2;
            if (a[mid] > a[hi]) lo = mid + 1;
            else if (a[mid] < a[hi]) hi = mid;
            else hi--; // equal: cannot tell, shrink safely
        }
        return a[lo];
    }

    // Peak Element (LC 162): follow the uphill side
    static int findPeakElement(int[] a) {
        int lo = 0, hi = a.length - 1;
        while (lo < hi) {
            int mid = lo + (hi - lo) / 2;
            if (a[mid] < a[mid + 1]) lo = mid + 1; else hi = mid;
        }
        return lo;
    }

    // Search a 2D Matrix (LC 74): treat as a flat sorted array
    static boolean searchMatrix(int[][] m, int t) {
        int R = m.length, C = m[0].length, lo = 0, hi = R * C - 1;
        while (lo <= hi) {
            int mid = lo + (hi - lo) / 2, v = m[mid / C][mid % C];
            if (v == t) return true;
            if (v < t) lo = mid + 1; else hi = mid - 1;
        }
        return false;
    }

    // Search a 2D Matrix II (LC 240): staircase from the top-right, O(m + n)
    static boolean searchMatrixII(int[][] m, int t) {
        int r = 0, c = m[0].length - 1;
        while (r < m.length && c >= 0) {
            if (m[r][c] == t) return true;
            if (m[r][c] > t) c--; else r++;
        }
        return false;
    }

    // ===== BINARY SEARCH ON THE ANSWER: ok(x) = "is x enough / feasible?" =====
    // Koko Eating Bananas (LC 875): min speed finishing in h hours
    static int minEatingSpeed(int[] piles, int h) {
        int lo = 1, hi = Arrays.stream(piles).max().getAsInt();
        while (lo < hi) {
            int mid = lo + (hi - lo) / 2;
            long hours = 0;
            for (int p : piles) hours += (p + mid - 1) / mid;
            if (hours <= h) hi = mid; else lo = mid + 1;
        }
        return lo;
    }

    // Capacity To Ship Packages Within D Days (LC 1011)
    static int shipWithinDays(int[] w, int days) {
        int lo = 0, hi = 0;
        for (int x : w) { lo = Math.max(lo, x); hi += x; }
        while (lo < hi) {
            int mid = lo + (hi - lo) / 2, need = 1, cur = 0;
            for (int x : w) { if (cur + x > mid) { need++; cur = 0; } cur += x; }
            if (need <= days) hi = mid; else lo = mid + 1;
        }
        return lo;
    }

    // Split Array Largest Sum (LC 410): same shape as shipping
    static int splitArray(int[] a, int k) { return shipWithinDays(a, k); }

    // Minimum Days to Make m Bouquets (LC 1482)
    static int minDays(int[] bloom, int m, int k) {
        if ((long) m * k > bloom.length) return -1;
        int lo = 1, hi = Arrays.stream(bloom).max().getAsInt();
        while (lo < hi) {
            int mid = lo + (hi - lo) / 2, bouquets = 0, run = 0;
            for (int b : bloom) {
                if (b <= mid) { if (++run == k) { bouquets++; run = 0; } } else run = 0;
            }
            if (bouquets >= m) hi = mid; else lo = mid + 1;
        }
        return lo;
    }

    // Kth Smallest in Multiplication Table (LC 668) / count-less-or-equal style
    static int findKthNumber(int m, int n, int k) {
        int lo = 1, hi = m * n;
        while (lo < hi) {
            int mid = lo + (hi - lo) / 2, cnt = 0;
            for (int i = 1; i <= m; i++) cnt += Math.min(mid / i, n);
            if (cnt >= k) hi = mid; else lo = mid + 1;
        }
        return lo;
    }

    // Median of Two Sorted Arrays (LC 4): partition the smaller array, O(log min(m, n))
    static double findMedianSortedArrays(int[] a, int[] b) {
        if (a.length > b.length) return findMedianSortedArrays(b, a);
        int m = a.length, n = b.length, lo = 0, hi = m, half = (m + n + 1) / 2;
        while (lo <= hi) {
            int i = (lo + hi) / 2, j = half - i;
            int aL = i == 0 ? Integer.MIN_VALUE : a[i - 1], aR = i == m ? Integer.MAX_VALUE : a[i];
            int bL = j == 0 ? Integer.MIN_VALUE : b[j - 1], bR = j == n ? Integer.MAX_VALUE : b[j];
            if (aL <= bR && bL <= aR) {
                if ((m + n) % 2 == 1) return Math.max(aL, bL);
                return (Math.max(aL, bL) + (double) Math.min(aR, bR)) / 2;
            }
            if (aL > bR) hi = i - 1; else lo = i + 1;
        }
        throw new IllegalArgumentException();
    }

    // Real-valued search: sqrt with fixed iterations (avoids epsilon traps)
    static double sqrt(double x) {
        double lo = 0, hi = Math.max(1, x);
        for (int it = 0; it < 100; it++) {
            double mid = (lo + hi) / 2;
            if (mid * mid >= x) hi = mid; else lo = mid;
        }
        return hi;
    }

    // Integer sqrt (LC 69): last x with x*x <= n
    static int mySqrt(int n) {
        int lo = 0, hi = 46341; // 46341^2 overflows int, so it is the first "too big"
        while (lo < hi) {
            int mid = lo + (hi - lo) / 2;
            if ((long) mid * mid > n) hi = mid; else lo = mid + 1;
        }
        return lo - 1;
    }

    // Time Based Key-Value Store (LC 981): binary search the latest timestamp <= t
    static class TimeMap {
        private final Map<String, List<int[]>> ts = new HashMap<>(); // {timestamp, valueIndex}
        private final List<String> vals = new ArrayList<>();
        void set(String k, String v, int t) {
            vals.add(v);
            ts.computeIfAbsent(k, x -> new ArrayList<>()).add(new int[]{t, vals.size() - 1});
        }
        String get(String k, int t) {
            List<int[]> l = ts.get(k);
            if (l == null) return "";
            int lo = 0, hi = l.size();
            while (lo < hi) {
                int mid = (lo + hi) >>> 1;
                if (l.get(mid)[0] > t) hi = mid; else lo = mid + 1;
            }
            return lo == 0 ? "" : vals.get(l.get(lo - 1)[1]);
        }
    }

    // Find K Closest Elements (LC 658): binary search the left edge of the window
    static List<Integer> findClosestElements(int[] a, int k, int x) {
        int lo = 0, hi = a.length - k;
        while (lo < hi) {
            int mid = lo + (hi - lo) / 2;
            if (x - a[mid] > a[mid + k] - x) lo = mid + 1; else hi = mid;
        }
        List<Integer> res = new ArrayList<>();
        for (int i = lo; i < lo + k; i++) res.add(a[i]);
        return res;
    }
}

Pitfalls

  1. Compute mid as lo + (hi - lo) / 2 to avoid overflow.
  2. Search-on-answer needs a monotone predicate: if feasible(x) does not imply feasible(x + 1) you cannot binary search.
  3. With duplicates in a rotated array, equal ends force a linear shrink (worst case O(n)).
32 — BACKTRACKING.JAVA

Backtracking

Choose, explore, undo. Subsets and combinations move a START index forward; permutations use a used[] array. For duplicate inputs sort first and skip equal siblings at the same depth. Prune early with a cheap feasibility bound.

PatternUse caseComplexity
Subsets / combinationsStart index moves forwardO(2^n)
Permutationsused[] array, with duplicate skip ruleO(n!)
Constraint searchN-Queens, sudoku with occupancy sets or bitmasksexp
StringsParentheses, partitions, IP addresses, operatorsexp
Backtracking.java
Subsets (+dup), permutations (+unique), combinations, combination sum I/II, parentheses, phone, palindrome partition, word search, N-Queens, sudoku, IP addresses, add operators, gray code
O(2^n) / O(n!) 
import java.util.*;

public class Backtracking {

    // ===== SKELETON =====
    //   void dfs(state) { if (complete) { record; return; } for (choice in choices) { if (!valid) continue; choose; dfs(next); undo; } }
    // Duplicates in input: sort first, then skip `i > start && a[i] == a[i-1]` at the same depth.
    // Subsets vs permutations: subsets move a START index forward; permutations use a USED array.

    // Subsets (LC 78)
    static List<List<Integer>> subsets(int[] a) {
        List<List<Integer>> res = new ArrayList<>();
        subsetsDfs(a, 0, new ArrayList<>(), res);
        return res;
    }

    private static void subsetsDfs(int[] a, int start, List<Integer> cur, List<List<Integer>> res) {
        res.add(new ArrayList<>(cur));
        for (int i = start; i < a.length; i++) {
            cur.add(a[i]);
            subsetsDfs(a, i + 1, cur, res);
            cur.remove(cur.size() - 1);
        }
    }

    // Subsets II with duplicates (LC 90)
    static List<List<Integer>> subsetsWithDup(int[] a) {
        Arrays.sort(a);
        List<List<Integer>> res = new ArrayList<>();
        dupDfs(a, 0, new ArrayList<>(), res);
        return res;
    }

    private static void dupDfs(int[] a, int start, List<Integer> cur, List<List<Integer>> res) {
        res.add(new ArrayList<>(cur));
        for (int i = start; i < a.length; i++) {
            if (i > start && a[i] == a[i - 1]) continue;
            cur.add(a[i]);
            dupDfs(a, i + 1, cur, res);
            cur.remove(cur.size() - 1);
        }
    }

    // Permutations (LC 46)
    static List<List<Integer>> permute(int[] a) {
        List<List<Integer>> res = new ArrayList<>();
        permDfs(a, new boolean[a.length], new ArrayList<>(), res);
        return res;
    }

    private static void permDfs(int[] a, boolean[] used, List<Integer> cur, List<List<Integer>> res) {
        if (cur.size() == a.length) { res.add(new ArrayList<>(cur)); return; }
        for (int i = 0; i < a.length; i++) {
            if (used[i]) continue;
            used[i] = true;
            cur.add(a[i]);
            permDfs(a, used, cur, res);
            cur.remove(cur.size() - 1);
            used[i] = false;
        }
    }

    // Permutations II with duplicates (LC 47): skip a[i] if its equal left twin is not used on this path
    static List<List<Integer>> permuteUnique(int[] a) {
        Arrays.sort(a);
        List<List<Integer>> res = new ArrayList<>();
        uniqDfs(a, new boolean[a.length], new ArrayList<>(), res);
        return res;
    }

    private static void uniqDfs(int[] a, boolean[] used, List<Integer> cur, List<List<Integer>> res) {
        if (cur.size() == a.length) { res.add(new ArrayList<>(cur)); return; }
        for (int i = 0; i < a.length; i++) {
            if (used[i] || (i > 0 && a[i] == a[i - 1] && !used[i - 1])) continue;
            used[i] = true;
            cur.add(a[i]);
            uniqDfs(a, used, cur, res);
            cur.remove(cur.size() - 1);
            used[i] = false;
        }
    }

    // Combinations (LC 77)
    static List<List<Integer>> combine(int n, int k) {
        List<List<Integer>> res = new ArrayList<>();
        combDfs(n, k, 1, new ArrayList<>(), res);
        return res;
    }

    private static void combDfs(int n, int k, int start, List<Integer> cur, List<List<Integer>> res) {
        if (cur.size() == k) { res.add(new ArrayList<>(cur)); return; }
        for (int i = start; i <= n - (k - cur.size()) + 1; i++) { // prune: enough numbers must remain
            cur.add(i);
            combDfs(n, k, i + 1, cur, res);
            cur.remove(cur.size() - 1);
        }
    }

    // Combination Sum (LC 39): reuse allowed -> recurse with the same index
    static List<List<Integer>> combinationSum(int[] c, int target) {
        Arrays.sort(c);
        List<List<Integer>> res = new ArrayList<>();
        csDfs(c, 0, target, new ArrayList<>(), res);
        return res;
    }

    private static void csDfs(int[] c, int start, int remain, List<Integer> cur, List<List<Integer>> res) {
        if (remain == 0) { res.add(new ArrayList<>(cur)); return; }
        for (int i = start; i < c.length && c[i] <= remain; i++) {
            cur.add(c[i]);
            csDfs(c, i, remain - c[i], cur, res);
            cur.remove(cur.size() - 1);
        }
    }

    // Combination Sum II (LC 40): each number once, duplicates present
    static List<List<Integer>> combinationSum2(int[] c, int target) {
        Arrays.sort(c);
        List<List<Integer>> res = new ArrayList<>();
        cs2Dfs(c, 0, target, new ArrayList<>(), res);
        return res;
    }

    private static void cs2Dfs(int[] c, int start, int remain, List<Integer> cur, List<List<Integer>> res) {
        if (remain == 0) { res.add(new ArrayList<>(cur)); return; }
        for (int i = start; i < c.length && c[i] <= remain; i++) {
            if (i > start && c[i] == c[i - 1]) continue;
            cur.add(c[i]);
            cs2Dfs(c, i + 1, remain - c[i], cur, res);
            cur.remove(cur.size() - 1);
        }
    }

    // Generate Parentheses (LC 22): track open / close counts, never let close exceed open
    static List<String> generateParenthesis(int n) {
        List<String> res = new ArrayList<>();
        genDfs(n, 0, 0, new StringBuilder(), res);
        return res;
    }

    private static void genDfs(int n, int open, int close, StringBuilder sb, List<String> res) {
        if (sb.length() == 2 * n) { res.add(sb.toString()); return; }
        if (open < n) { sb.append('('); genDfs(n, open + 1, close, sb, res); sb.setLength(sb.length() - 1); }
        if (close < open) { sb.append(')'); genDfs(n, open, close + 1, sb, res); sb.setLength(sb.length() - 1); }
    }

    // Letter Combinations of a Phone Number (LC 17)
    static List<String> letterCombinations(String digits) {
        List<String> res = new ArrayList<>();
        if (digits.isEmpty()) return res;
        String[] map = {"", "", "abc", "def", "ghi", "jkl", "mno", "pqrs", "tuv", "wxyz"};
        phoneDfs(digits, 0, map, new StringBuilder(), res);
        return res;
    }

    private static void phoneDfs(String d, int i, String[] map, StringBuilder sb, List<String> res) {
        if (i == d.length()) { res.add(sb.toString()); return; }
        for (char c : map[d.charAt(i) - '0'].toCharArray()) {
            sb.append(c);
            phoneDfs(d, i + 1, map, sb, res);
            sb.setLength(sb.length() - 1);
        }
    }

    // Palindrome Partitioning (LC 131)
    static List<List<String>> partition(String s) {
        List<List<String>> res = new ArrayList<>();
        palDfs(s, 0, new ArrayList<>(), res);
        return res;
    }

    private static void palDfs(String s, int start, List<String> cur, List<List<String>> res) {
        if (start == s.length()) { res.add(new ArrayList<>(cur)); return; }
        for (int end = start + 1; end <= s.length(); end++) {
            if (!isPal(s, start, end - 1)) continue;
            cur.add(s.substring(start, end));
            palDfs(s, end, cur, res);
            cur.remove(cur.size() - 1);
        }
    }

    private static boolean isPal(String s, int l, int r) {
        while (l < r) if (s.charAt(l++) != s.charAt(r--)) return false;
        return true;
    }

    // Word Search (LC 79): mark visited in place, restore on the way out
    static boolean exist(char[][] b, String w) {
        for (int i = 0; i < b.length; i++)
            for (int j = 0; j < b[0].length; j++) if (wsDfs(b, w, 0, i, j)) return true;
        return false;
    }

    private static boolean wsDfs(char[][] b, String w, int k, int i, int j) {
        if (k == w.length()) return true;
        if (i < 0 || j < 0 || i >= b.length || j >= b[0].length || b[i][j] != w.charAt(k)) return false;
        char c = b[i][j];
        b[i][j] = '#';
        boolean ok = wsDfs(b, w, k + 1, i + 1, j) || wsDfs(b, w, k + 1, i - 1, j) || wsDfs(b, w, k + 1, i, j + 1) || wsDfs(b, w, k + 1, i, j - 1);
        b[i][j] = c;
        return ok;
    }

    // N-Queens (LC 51): column / diagonal / anti-diagonal occupancy sets -> O(1) validity
    static List<List<String>> solveNQueens(int n) {
        List<List<String>> res = new ArrayList<>();
        queens(n, 0, new int[n], new boolean[n], new boolean[2 * n], new boolean[2 * n], res);
        return res;
    }

    private static void queens(int n, int r, int[] col, boolean[] c, boolean[] d1, boolean[] d2, List<List<String>> res) {
        if (r == n) {
            List<String> board = new ArrayList<>();
            for (int i = 0; i < n; i++) {
                char[] row = new char[n];
                Arrays.fill(row, '.');
                row[col[i]] = 'Q';
                board.add(new String(row));
            }
            res.add(board);
            return;
        }
        for (int j = 0; j < n; j++) {
            if (c[j] || d1[r + j] || d2[r - j + n]) continue;
            c[j] = d1[r + j] = d2[r - j + n] = true;
            col[r] = j;
            queens(n, r + 1, col, c, d1, d2, res);
            c[j] = d1[r + j] = d2[r - j + n] = false;
        }
    }

    // N-Queens count only, bitmask form (fast)
    static int totalNQueens(int n) { return bits(n, 0, 0, 0, 0); }

    private static int bits(int n, int row, int cols, int d1, int d2) {
        if (row == n) return 1;
        int count = 0, avail = ((1 << n) - 1) & ~(cols | d1 | d2);
        while (avail != 0) {
            int bit = avail & -avail;
            avail ^= bit;
            count += bits(n, row + 1, cols | bit, (d1 | bit) << 1, (d2 | bit) >> 1);
        }
        return count;
    }

    // Sudoku Solver (LC 37): fill the first empty cell, try 1..9 with row/col/box bitmasks
    static boolean solveSudoku(char[][] b) {
        int[] row = new int[9], col = new int[9], box = new int[9];
        for (int i = 0; i < 9; i++)
            for (int j = 0; j < 9; j++)
                if (b[i][j] != '.') {
                    int bit = 1 << (b[i][j] - '1');
                    row[i] |= bit; col[j] |= bit; box[i / 3 * 3 + j / 3] |= bit;
                }
        return sudoku(b, 0, row, col, box);
    }

    private static boolean sudoku(char[][] b, int pos, int[] row, int[] col, int[] box) {
        while (pos < 81 && b[pos / 9][pos % 9] != '.') pos++;
        if (pos == 81) return true;
        int i = pos / 9, j = pos % 9, k = i / 3 * 3 + j / 3;
        for (int d = 0; d < 9; d++) {
            int bit = 1 << d;
            if (((row[i] | col[j] | box[k]) & bit) != 0) continue;
            row[i] |= bit; col[j] |= bit; box[k] |= bit;
            b[i][j] = (char) ('1' + d);
            if (sudoku(b, pos + 1, row, col, box)) return true;
            b[i][j] = '.';
            row[i] ^= bit; col[j] ^= bit; box[k] ^= bit;
        }
        return false;
    }

    // Restore IP Addresses (LC 93)
    static List<String> restoreIpAddresses(String s) {
        List<String> res = new ArrayList<>();
        ipDfs(s, 0, new ArrayList<>(), res);
        return res;
    }

    private static void ipDfs(String s, int i, List<String> parts, List<String> res) {
        if (parts.size() == 4) { if (i == s.length()) res.add(String.join(".", parts)); return; }
        for (int len = 1; len <= 3 && i + len <= s.length(); len++) {
            String p = s.substring(i, i + len);
            if ((p.length() > 1 && p.charAt(0) == '0') || Integer.parseInt(p) > 255) continue;
            parts.add(p);
            ipDfs(s, i + len, parts, res);
            parts.remove(parts.size() - 1);
        }
    }

    // Expression Add Operators (LC 282): carry the last multiplied term so '*' can undo the previous add
    static List<String> addOperators(String num, int target) {
        List<String> res = new ArrayList<>();
        opsDfs(num, target, 0, 0, 0, "", res);
        return res;
    }

    private static void opsDfs(String s, int target, int i, long val, long last, String expr, List<String> res) {
        if (i == s.length()) { if (val == target) res.add(expr); return; }
        for (int j = i; j < s.length(); j++) {
            if (j > i && s.charAt(i) == '0') break;
            long cur = Long.parseLong(s.substring(i, j + 1));
            String t = s.substring(i, j + 1);
            if (i == 0) opsDfs(s, target, j + 1, cur, cur, t, res);
            else {
                opsDfs(s, target, j + 1, val + cur, cur, expr + "+" + t, res);
                opsDfs(s, target, j + 1, val - cur, -cur, expr + "-" + t, res);
                opsDfs(s, target, j + 1, val - last + last * cur, last * cur, expr + "*" + t, res);
            }
        }
    }

    // Gray Code (LC 89): i ^ (i >> 1)
    static List<Integer> grayCode(int n) {
        List<Integer> res = new ArrayList<>();
        for (int i = 0; i < 1 << n; i++) res.add(i ^ (i >> 1));
        return res;
    }
}

Pitfalls

  1. Add a COPY of the current path to the result, not the live list.
  2. Duplicates: i > start && a[i] == a[i-1] for combinations; used[i-1] check for permutations.
  3. Prune before recursing (remaining sum < 0, not enough elements left) instead of after.
33 — GREEDY.JAVA

Greedy

Greedy is right when an exchange argument or a "stays ahead" invariant holds. State the argument in one sentence before coding; if you cannot, think DP. Typical shapes: furthest reach, sort by a key, two passes, ratio ordering, heap of options.

PatternUse caseComplexity
Reach / jumpsTrack the furthest frontierO(n)
Two-passCandy, gas station style prefix logicO(n)
Sort then sweepAssign cookies, boats, largest numberO(n log n)
Greedy + heapRefueling stops, scheduling with choicesO(n log n)
Greedy.java
Jump game I/II, gas station, candy, partition labels, queue reconstruction, cookies, largest number, units, valid parenthesis string, boats, refueling, straights, lemonade
O(n log n) 
import java.util.*;

public class Greedy {

    // PROOF HABIT: greedy works when an exchange argument holds (swap the optimal's choice for yours without loss)
    // or when a "stays ahead" invariant holds. If you cannot argue it in one sentence, think DP.

    // Jump Game (LC 55): furthest reachable
    static boolean canJump(int[] a) {
        int far = 0;
        for (int i = 0; i < a.length; i++) {
            if (i > far) return false;
            far = Math.max(far, i + a[i]);
        }
        return true;
    }

    // Jump Game II (LC 45): BFS levels implicitly -- extend the frontier when the current one is exhausted
    static int jump(int[] a) {
        int jumps = 0, curEnd = 0, far = 0;
        for (int i = 0; i < a.length - 1; i++) {
            far = Math.max(far, i + a[i]);
            if (i == curEnd) { jumps++; curEnd = far; }
        }
        return jumps;
    }

    // Gas Station (LC 134): if total >= 0 a start exists; restart after any prefix that goes negative
    static int canCompleteCircuit(int[] gas, int[] cost) {
        int total = 0, tank = 0, start = 0;
        for (int i = 0; i < gas.length; i++) {
            int d = gas[i] - cost[i];
            total += d;
            tank += d;
            if (tank < 0) { start = i + 1; tank = 0; }
        }
        return total >= 0 ? start : -1;
    }

    // Candy (LC 135): left pass, right pass
    static int candy(int[] r) {
        int n = r.length;
        int[] c = new int[n];
        Arrays.fill(c, 1);
        for (int i = 1; i < n; i++) if (r[i] > r[i - 1]) c[i] = c[i - 1] + 1;
        for (int i = n - 2; i >= 0; i--) if (r[i] > r[i + 1]) c[i] = Math.max(c[i], c[i + 1] + 1);
        int sum = 0;
        for (int x : c) sum += x;
        return sum;
    }

    // Partition Labels (LC 763): extend to the last occurrence
    static List<Integer> partitionLabels(String s) {
        int[] last = new int[26];
        for (int i = 0; i < s.length(); i++) last[s.charAt(i) - 'a'] = i;
        List<Integer> res = new ArrayList<>();
        int start = 0, end = 0;
        for (int i = 0; i < s.length(); i++) {
            end = Math.max(end, last[s.charAt(i) - 'a']);
            if (i == end) { res.add(end - start + 1); start = i + 1; }
        }
        return res;
    }

    // Queue Reconstruction by Height (LC 406): tallest first, insert at index k
    static int[][] reconstructQueue(int[][] people) {
        Arrays.sort(people, (a, b) -> a[0] != b[0] ? b[0] - a[0] : a[1] - b[1]);
        List<int[]> out = new ArrayList<>();
        for (int[] p : people) out.add(p[1], p);
        return out.toArray(new int[0][]);
    }

    // Assign Cookies (LC 455): smallest cookie that satisfies the least greedy child
    static int findContentChildren(int[] g, int[] s) {
        Arrays.sort(g);
        Arrays.sort(s);
        int i = 0;
        for (int j = 0; j < s.length && i < g.length; j++) if (s[j] >= g[i]) i++;
        return i;
    }

    // Largest Number (LC 179): comparator a+b vs b+a
    static String largestNumber(int[] a) {
        String[] s = new String[a.length];
        for (int i = 0; i < a.length; i++) s[i] = String.valueOf(a[i]);
        Arrays.sort(s, (x, y) -> (y + x).compareTo(x + y));
        if (s[0].equals("0")) return "0";
        return String.join("", s);
    }

    // Task order minimizing lateness / weighted completion: sort by ratio (exchange argument)
    // Example: Minimum Cost to Hire Workers style ratios live in HeapsTopK; here: Maximum units on a truck (LC 1710)
    static int maximumUnits(int[][] boxes, int truckSize) {
        Arrays.sort(boxes, (a, b) -> b[1] - a[1]);
        int units = 0;
        for (int[] b : boxes) {
            int take = Math.min(b[0], truckSize);
            units += take * b[1];
            truckSize -= take;
            if (truckSize == 0) break;
        }
        return units;
    }

    // Valid Parenthesis String (LC 678): track the range [lo, hi] of possible open counts
    static boolean checkValidString(String s) {
        int lo = 0, hi = 0;
        for (char c : s.toCharArray()) {
            lo += c == '(' ? 1 : -1;
            hi += c == ')' ? -1 : 1;
            if (hi < 0) return false;
            lo = Math.max(lo, 0);
        }
        return lo == 0;
    }

    // Boats to Save People (LC 881): pair heaviest with lightest
    static int numRescueBoats(int[] p, int limit) {
        Arrays.sort(p);
        int i = 0, j = p.length - 1, boats = 0;
        while (i <= j) { if (p[i] + p[j] <= limit) i++; j--; boats++; }
        return boats;
    }

    // Minimum Number of Refueling Stops (LC 871): greedy with a max-heap of passed stations
    static int minRefuelStops(int target, int startFuel, int[][] stations) {
        PriorityQueue<Integer> pq = new PriorityQueue<>(Collections.reverseOrder());
        long fuel = startFuel;
        int stops = 0, i = 0;
        while (fuel < target) {
            while (i < stations.length && stations[i][0] <= fuel) pq.add(stations[i++][1]);
            if (pq.isEmpty()) return -1;
            fuel += pq.poll();
            stops++;
        }
        return stops;
    }

    // Hand of Straights (LC 846): always start from the smallest remaining card
    static boolean isNStraightHand(int[] hand, int w) {
        if (hand.length % w != 0) return false;
        TreeMap<Integer, Integer> cnt = new TreeMap<>();
        for (int h : hand) cnt.merge(h, 1, Integer::sum);
        while (!cnt.isEmpty()) {
            int first = cnt.firstKey();
            for (int c = first; c < first + w; c++) {
                Integer v = cnt.get(c);
                if (v == null) return false;
                if (v == 1) cnt.remove(c); else cnt.put(c, v - 1);
            }
        }
        return true;
    }

    // Lemonade Change (LC 860)
    static boolean lemonadeChange(int[] bills) {
        int five = 0, ten = 0;
        for (int b : bills) {
            if (b == 5) five++;
            else if (b == 10) { if (five == 0) return false; five--; ten++; }
            else {
                if (ten > 0 && five > 0) { ten--; five--; }
                else if (five >= 3) five -= 3;
                else return false;
            }
        }
        return true;
    }
}

Pitfalls

  1. Without a proof sketch, test the greedy against brute force on small inputs.
  2. Largest Number: compare a+b with b+a, and special-case an all-zero result.
  3. Valid Parenthesis String: carry a range [lo, hi] of possible open counts, not a single count.
34 — LINKEDLISTS.JAVA

Linked Lists

Use a dummy head whenever the head can change. Slow/fast pointers find the middle and the cycle; reversing the second half turns many problems (palindrome, reorder) into a merge of two lists. Draw the pointers before coding.

PatternUse caseComplexity
ReverseWhole list, a range, k-groupsO(n)
Slow / fastMiddle, cycle entry, nth from endO(n)
MergeTwo lists, K lists by divide and conquer, sort listO(n log n)
Pointer surgeryReorder, partition, rotate, copy with random pointerO(n)
LinkedLists.java
Reverse (range, k-group), swap pairs, middle, cycle, remove nth, merge, merge K, sort, reorder, palindrome, add numbers, intersection, dedupe II, partition, rotate, odd-even, copy random
O(n) 
import java.util.*;

public class LinkedLists {

    static class ListNode {
        int val;
        ListNode next;
        ListNode(int v) { val = v; }
    }

    // Test helpers
    static ListNode of(int... a) {
        ListNode dummy = new ListNode(0), t = dummy;
        for (int x : a) { t.next = new ListNode(x); t = t.next; }
        return dummy.next;
    }

    static String str(ListNode n) {
        StringBuilder sb = new StringBuilder("[");
        for (; n != null; n = n.next) sb.append(n.val).append(n.next != null ? "," : "");
        return sb.append("]").toString();
    }

    // DUMMY HEAD: use it whenever the head itself might change (delete, merge, partition, reverse range).

    // Reverse (LC 206): iterative
    static ListNode reverse(ListNode head) {
        ListNode prev = null;
        while (head != null) { ListNode nx = head.next; head.next = prev; prev = head; head = nx; }
        return prev;
    }

    // Reverse Between (LC 92)
    static ListNode reverseBetween(ListNode head, int left, int right) {
        ListNode dummy = new ListNode(0);
        dummy.next = head;
        ListNode pre = dummy;
        for (int i = 1; i < left; i++) pre = pre.next;
        ListNode cur = pre.next;
        for (int i = 0; i < right - left; i++) { // pull the next node to the front of the segment
            ListNode nx = cur.next;
            cur.next = nx.next;
            nx.next = pre.next;
            pre.next = nx;
        }
        return dummy.next;
    }

    // Reverse Nodes in k-Group (LC 25)
    static ListNode reverseKGroup(ListNode head, int k) {
        ListNode probe = head;
        for (int i = 0; i < k; i++) { if (probe == null) return head; probe = probe.next; }
        ListNode prev = reverseKGroup(probe, k), cur = head;
        for (int i = 0; i < k; i++) { ListNode nx = cur.next; cur.next = prev; prev = cur; cur = nx; }
        return prev;
    }

    // Swap Nodes in Pairs (LC 24)
    static ListNode swapPairs(ListNode head) {
        ListNode dummy = new ListNode(0), prev = dummy;
        dummy.next = head;
        while (prev.next != null && prev.next.next != null) {
            ListNode a = prev.next, b = a.next;
            a.next = b.next;
            b.next = a;
            prev.next = b;
            prev = a;
        }
        return dummy.next;
    }

    // Middle (LC 876): slow / fast; for even length returns the second middle
    static ListNode middle(ListNode head) {
        ListNode s = head, f = head;
        while (f != null && f.next != null) { s = s.next; f = f.next.next; }
        return s;
    }

    // Cycle detection + entry (LC 141 / 142): Floyd
    static ListNode detectCycle(ListNode head) {
        ListNode s = head, f = head;
        while (f != null && f.next != null) {
            s = s.next;
            f = f.next.next;
            if (s == f) {
                s = head;
                while (s != f) { s = s.next; f = f.next; }
                return s;
            }
        }
        return null;
    }

    // Remove Nth From End (LC 19): gap of n between two pointers
    static ListNode removeNthFromEnd(ListNode head, int n) {
        ListNode dummy = new ListNode(0), fast = dummy, slow = dummy;
        dummy.next = head;
        for (int i = 0; i <= n; i++) fast = fast.next;
        while (fast != null) { fast = fast.next; slow = slow.next; }
        slow.next = slow.next.next;
        return dummy.next;
    }

    // Merge Two Sorted Lists (LC 21)
    static ListNode mergeTwo(ListNode a, ListNode b) {
        ListNode dummy = new ListNode(0), t = dummy;
        while (a != null && b != null) {
            if (a.val <= b.val) { t.next = a; a = a.next; } else { t.next = b; b = b.next; }
            t = t.next;
        }
        t.next = a != null ? a : b;
        return dummy.next;
    }

    // Merge K Lists (LC 23): divide and conquer, O(N log k), no heap needed
    static ListNode mergeKLists(ListNode[] lists) {
        if (lists.length == 0) return null;
        for (int step = 1; step < lists.length; step *= 2)
            for (int i = 0; i + step < lists.length; i += 2 * step) lists[i] = mergeTwo(lists[i], lists[i + step]);
        return lists[0];
    }

    // Sort List (LC 148): merge sort, split with slow/fast
    static ListNode sortList(ListNode head) {
        if (head == null || head.next == null) return head;
        ListNode slow = head, fast = head.next;
        while (fast != null && fast.next != null) { slow = slow.next; fast = fast.next.next; }
        ListNode right = slow.next;
        slow.next = null;
        return mergeTwo(sortList(head), sortList(right));
    }

    // Reorder List (LC 143): middle -> reverse second half -> interleave
    static void reorderList(ListNode head) {
        if (head == null || head.next == null) return;
        ListNode mid = middle(head), second = reverse(mid.next);
        mid.next = null;
        ListNode first = head;
        while (second != null) {
            ListNode a = first.next, b = second.next;
            first.next = second;
            second.next = a;
            first = a;
            second = b;
        }
    }

    // Palindrome List (LC 234)
    static boolean isPalindrome(ListNode head) {
        ListNode mid = middle(head), second = reverse(mid), first = head;
        while (second != null) {
            if (first.val != second.val) return false;
            first = first.next;
            second = second.next;
        }
        return true;
    }

    // Add Two Numbers (LC 2): digits in reverse order
    static ListNode addTwoNumbers(ListNode a, ListNode b) {
        ListNode dummy = new ListNode(0), t = dummy;
        int carry = 0;
        while (a != null || b != null || carry > 0) {
            int s = carry + (a != null ? a.val : 0) + (b != null ? b.val : 0);
            t.next = new ListNode(s % 10);
            carry = s / 10;
            t = t.next;
            if (a != null) a = a.next;
            if (b != null) b = b.next;
        }
        return dummy.next;
    }

    // Intersection of Two Lists (LC 160): switch heads at the end, meet at the intersection (or both null)
    static ListNode getIntersectionNode(ListNode a, ListNode b) {
        ListNode p = a, q = b;
        while (p != q) {
            p = p == null ? b : p.next;
            q = q == null ? a : q.next;
        }
        return p;
    }

    // Remove Duplicates from Sorted List II (LC 82): drop every value that repeats
    static ListNode deleteDuplicates(ListNode head) {
        ListNode dummy = new ListNode(0), prev = dummy;
        dummy.next = head;
        while (head != null) {
            if (head.next != null && head.val == head.next.val) {
                while (head.next != null && head.val == head.next.val) head = head.next;
                prev.next = head.next;
            } else prev = prev.next;
            head = head.next;
        }
        return dummy.next;
    }

    // Partition List (LC 86): two dummy lists
    static ListNode partition(ListNode head, int x) {
        ListNode lo = new ListNode(0), hi = new ListNode(0), a = lo, b = hi;
        for (; head != null; head = head.next) {
            if (head.val < x) { a.next = head; a = a.next; } else { b.next = head; b = b.next; }
        }
        b.next = null;
        a.next = hi.next;
        return lo.next;
    }

    // Rotate List (LC 61)
    static ListNode rotateRight(ListNode head, int k) {
        if (head == null || head.next == null || k == 0) return head;
        int n = 1;
        ListNode tail = head;
        while (tail.next != null) { tail = tail.next; n++; }
        k %= n;
        if (k == 0) return head;
        ListNode newTail = head;
        for (int i = 0; i < n - k - 1; i++) newTail = newTail.next;
        ListNode newHead = newTail.next;
        newTail.next = null;
        tail.next = head;
        return newHead;
    }

    // Odd Even Linked List (LC 328)
    static ListNode oddEvenList(ListNode head) {
        if (head == null) return null;
        ListNode odd = head, even = head.next, evenHead = even;
        while (even != null && even.next != null) {
            odd.next = even.next;
            odd = odd.next;
            even.next = odd.next;
            even = even.next;
        }
        odd.next = evenHead;
        return head;
    }

    // Copy List with Random Pointer (LC 138): interleave copies, set randoms, split. O(1) extra space.
    static class RNode {
        int val;
        RNode next, random;
        RNode(int v) { val = v; }
    }

    static RNode copyRandomList(RNode head) {
        if (head == null) return null;
        for (RNode c = head; c != null; c = c.next.next) {
            RNode cp = new RNode(c.val);
            cp.next = c.next;
            c.next = cp;
        }
        for (RNode c = head; c != null; c = c.next.next) if (c.random != null) c.next.random = c.random.next;
        RNode newHead = head.next;
        for (RNode c = head; c != null; c = c.next) {
            RNode cp = c.next;
            c.next = cp.next;
            if (cp.next != null) cp.next = cp.next.next;
        }
        return newHead;
    }
}

Pitfalls

  1. Dummy head removes every "is it the head?" special case.
  2. Save next BEFORE overwriting it when reversing.
  3. Cycle entry: after slow meets fast, restart one pointer at the head and move both one step.
35 — STRINGALGORITHMS.JAVA

String Algorithms

KMP and the Z-function give linear pattern matching and expose periodic structure; Rabin-Karp hashes windows (use two moduli and verify); Manacher finds the longest palindrome in O(n). The rest are careful big-number and parsing routines.

PatternUse caseComplexity
KMPPattern search, repeated pattern, shortest palindromeO(n + m)
Z-functionAll occurrences, prefix matchesO(n + m)
Rabin-KarpRolling hash, duplicate substring with binary searchO(n) expected
ManacherLongest palindromic substringO(n)
StringAlgorithms.java
Prefix function, KMP, repeated pattern, shortest palindrome, Z, Rabin-Karp, Manacher, anagram, isomorphic, compress, reverse words, multiply, add, atoi, roman
O(n + m) 
import java.util.*;

public class StringAlgorithms {

    // ---------- KMP: failure function = longest proper prefix that is also a suffix ----------
    // RECOGNIZE: find a pattern in text in O(n + m); periodic structure; shortest repeating unit.
    static int[] prefixFunction(String p) {
        int[] pi = new int[p.length()];
        for (int i = 1, k = 0; i < p.length(); i++) {
            while (k > 0 && p.charAt(i) != p.charAt(k)) k = pi[k - 1];
            if (p.charAt(i) == p.charAt(k)) k++;
            pi[i] = k;
        }
        return pi;
    }

    // first index of p in s, or -1 (LC 28)
    static int kmpSearch(String s, String p) {
        if (p.isEmpty()) return 0;
        int[] pi = prefixFunction(p);
        for (int i = 0, k = 0; i < s.length(); i++) {
            while (k > 0 && s.charAt(i) != p.charAt(k)) k = pi[k - 1];
            if (s.charAt(i) == p.charAt(k)) k++;
            if (k == p.length()) return i - k + 1;
        }
        return -1;
    }

    // Repeated Substring Pattern (LC 459): smallest period divides n
    static boolean repeatedSubstringPattern(String s) {
        int n = s.length(), p = prefixFunction(s)[n - 1];
        return p > 0 && n % (n - p) == 0;
    }

    // Shortest Palindrome (LC 214): longest palindromic prefix via KMP on s + '#' + reverse(s)
    static String shortestPalindrome(String s) {
        String t = s + "#" + new StringBuilder(s).reverse();
        int keep = prefixFunction(t)[t.length() - 1];
        return new StringBuilder(s.substring(keep)).reverse() + s;
    }

    // ---------- Z-function: z[i] = longest common prefix of s and s[i..] ----------
    static int[] zFunction(String s) {
        int n = s.length();
        int[] z = new int[n];
        for (int i = 1, l = 0, r = 0; i < n; i++) {
            if (i < r) z[i] = Math.min(r - i, z[i - l]);
            while (i + z[i] < n && s.charAt(z[i]) == s.charAt(i + z[i])) z[i]++;
            if (i + z[i] > r) { l = i; r = i + z[i]; }
        }
        return z;
    }

    // All occurrences of p in s using Z on p + '#' + s
    static List<Integer> findAllZ(String s, String p) {
        String t = p + "\u0001" + s;
        int[] z = zFunction(t);
        List<Integer> res = new ArrayList<>();
        for (int i = p.length() + 1; i < t.length(); i++) if (z[i] >= p.length()) res.add(i - p.length() - 1);
        return res;
    }

    // ---------- Rabin-Karp rolling hash (double mod to dodge anti-hash tests) ----------
    static List<Integer> rabinKarp(String s, String p) {
        List<Integer> res = new ArrayList<>();
        int n = s.length(), m = p.length();
        if (m > n) return res;
        final long B = 131, M1 = 1_000_000_007L, M2 = 998_244_353L;
        long h1 = 0, h2 = 0, t1 = 0, t2 = 0, p1 = 1, p2 = 1;
        for (int i = 0; i < m; i++) {
            h1 = (h1 * B + p.charAt(i)) % M1; h2 = (h2 * B + p.charAt(i)) % M2;
            t1 = (t1 * B + s.charAt(i)) % M1; t2 = (t2 * B + s.charAt(i)) % M2;
            if (i > 0) { p1 = p1 * B % M1; p2 = p2 * B % M2; }
        }
        for (int i = 0; ; i++) {
            if (h1 == t1 && h2 == t2 && s.startsWith(p, i)) res.add(i); // verify to be safe
            if (i + m >= n) break;
            t1 = ((t1 - s.charAt(i) * p1 % M1 + M1) * B + s.charAt(i + m)) % M1;
            t2 = ((t2 - s.charAt(i) * p2 % M2 + M2) * B + s.charAt(i + m)) % M2;
        }
        return res;
    }

    // ---------- Manacher: longest palindromic substring in O(n) ----------
    static String manacher(String s) {
        int n = s.length();
        if (n == 0) return "";
        StringBuilder sb = new StringBuilder("^");
        for (char c : s.toCharArray()) sb.append('#').append(c);
        String t = sb.append("#$").toString();
        int[] p = new int[t.length()];
        int c = 0, r = 0, bestLen = 0, bestCenter = 0;
        for (int i = 1; i < t.length() - 1; i++) {
            if (i < r) p[i] = Math.min(r - i, p[2 * c - i]);
            while (t.charAt(i + 1 + p[i]) == t.charAt(i - 1 - p[i])) p[i]++;
            if (i + p[i] > r) { c = i; r = i + p[i]; }
            if (p[i] > bestLen) { bestLen = p[i]; bestCenter = i; }
        }
        int start = (bestCenter - bestLen) / 2;
        return s.substring(start, start + bestLen);
    }

    // ---------- Anagram / counting utilities ----------
    static boolean isAnagram(String a, String b) {
        if (a.length() != b.length()) return false;
        int[] c = new int[26];
        for (int i = 0; i < a.length(); i++) { c[a.charAt(i) - 'a']++; c[b.charAt(i) - 'a']--; }
        for (int x : c) if (x != 0) return false;
        return true;
    }

    // Isomorphic Strings (LC 205): bidirectional mapping
    static boolean isIsomorphic(String s, String t) {
        int[] a = new int[256], b = new int[256];
        for (int i = 0; i < s.length(); i++) {
            if (a[s.charAt(i)] != b[t.charAt(i)]) return false;
            a[s.charAt(i)] = b[t.charAt(i)] = i + 1;
        }
        return true;
    }

    // Longest Common Prefix (LC 14)
    static String longestCommonPrefix(String[] strs) {
        String p = strs[0];
        for (String s : strs) while (!s.startsWith(p)) p = p.substring(0, p.length() - 1);
        return p;
    }

    // String Compression (LC 443): two pointers in place
    static int compress(char[] chars) {
        int w = 0, i = 0;
        while (i < chars.length) {
            int j = i;
            while (j < chars.length && chars[j] == chars[i]) j++;
            chars[w++] = chars[i];
            if (j - i > 1) for (char d : String.valueOf(j - i).toCharArray()) chars[w++] = d;
            i = j;
        }
        return w;
    }

    // Reverse Words (LC 151)
    static String reverseWords(String s) {
        String[] w = s.trim().split("\\s+");
        Collections.reverse(Arrays.asList(w));
        return String.join(" ", w);
    }

    // Multiply Strings (LC 43)
    static String multiply(String a, String b) {
        if (a.equals("0") || b.equals("0")) return "0";
        int[] r = new int[a.length() + b.length()];
        for (int i = a.length() - 1; i >= 0; i--)
            for (int j = b.length() - 1; j >= 0; j--) {
                int p = (a.charAt(i) - '0') * (b.charAt(j) - '0') + r[i + j + 1];
                r[i + j + 1] = p % 10;
                r[i + j] += p / 10;
            }
        StringBuilder sb = new StringBuilder();
        for (int d : r) if (!(sb.length() == 0 && d == 0)) sb.append(d);
        return sb.toString();
    }

    // Add Strings (LC 415)
    static String addStrings(String a, String b) {
        StringBuilder sb = new StringBuilder();
        int i = a.length() - 1, j = b.length() - 1, carry = 0;
        while (i >= 0 || j >= 0 || carry > 0) {
            int s = carry + (i >= 0 ? a.charAt(i--) - '0' : 0) + (j >= 0 ? b.charAt(j--) - '0' : 0);
            sb.append(s % 10);
            carry = s / 10;
        }
        return sb.reverse().toString();
    }

    // String to Integer / atoi (LC 8)
    static int myAtoi(String s) {
        int i = 0, n = s.length(), sign = 1;
        long v = 0;
        while (i < n && s.charAt(i) == ' ') i++;
        if (i < n && (s.charAt(i) == '+' || s.charAt(i) == '-')) sign = s.charAt(i++) == '-' ? -1 : 1;
        while (i < n && Character.isDigit(s.charAt(i))) {
            v = v * 10 + (s.charAt(i++) - '0');
            if (v * sign > Integer.MAX_VALUE) return Integer.MAX_VALUE;
            if (v * sign < Integer.MIN_VALUE) return Integer.MIN_VALUE;
        }
        return (int) (v * sign);
    }

    // Integer to Roman (LC 12) / Roman to Integer (LC 13)
    static int romanToInt(String s) {
        Map<Character, Integer> m = Map.of('I', 1, 'V', 5, 'X', 10, 'L', 50, 'C', 100, 'D', 500, 'M', 1000);
        int res = 0;
        for (int i = 0; i < s.length(); i++) {
            int v = m.get(s.charAt(i));
            res += i + 1 < s.length() && v < m.get(s.charAt(i + 1)) ? -v : v;
        }
        return res;
    }

    // Longest Duplicate Substring (LC 1044) idea: binary search on length + rolling hash is the standard answer;
    // see rabinKarp above for the hash and BinarySearch template for the outer loop.

    // Smallest rotation / Lyndon: minimal string rotation via doubling (simple O(n^2) baseline kept short)
    static String minRotation(String s) {
        String best = s;
        for (int i = 1; i < s.length(); i++) {
            String r = s.substring(i) + s.substring(0, i);
            if (r.compareTo(best) < 0) best = r;
        }
        return best;
    }

    // Longest Palindromic Substring is in DPSequences (expand around center) -- Manacher above is the O(n) version.
}

Pitfalls

  1. KMP failure function: on mismatch fall back with k = pi[k - 1], never reset to 0.
  2. Rabin-Karp: single mod hashes collide on adversarial inputs; use two moduli and verify with startsWith.
  3. atoi: clamp as you accumulate, using long, before overflow.
36 — BITSMATH.JAVA

Bits & Number Theory

Know the five bit identities cold (clear lowest set bit, isolate it, read, set, toggle). Number theory in interviews is gcd, fast power, sieve, factorization, modular inverse and nCr mod a prime, plus overflow discipline.

PatternUse caseComplexity
XOR tricksSingle number I/II/III, missing numberO(n)
Bit countingpopcount via n & (n - 1), counting bits dpO(bits)
Number theorygcd/lcm, powMod, sieve, factorizationO(log) / O(n log log n)
CombinatoricsnCr mod prime with factorial tablesO(1) per query
BitsMath.java
Single number family, hamming weight, count bits, powers of two/four, reverse bits, missing number, add without +, range AND, subsets by mask, gcd/lcm, powMod, sieve, factorize, nCr, overflow-safe reverse, fraction to decimal
O(log n) 
import java.util.*;

public class BitsMath {

    // ===== BIT CHEAT SHEET =====
    //   x & (x - 1)   clears the lowest set bit        x & -x   isolates the lowest set bit
    //   x ^ x = 0, x ^ 0 = x                           (x >> k) & 1   reads bit k
    //   x | (1 << k) sets   x & ~(1 << k) clears   x ^ (1 << k) toggles
    //   use >>> for logical shift of negatives; use long literals 1L << k above bit 30

    // Single Number (LC 136): xor cancels pairs
    static int singleNumber(int[] a) { int x = 0; for (int v : a) x ^= v; return x; }

    // Single Number II (LC 137): count each bit mod 3
    static int singleNumberII(int[] a) {
        int ones = 0, twos = 0;
        for (int x : a) { ones = (ones ^ x) & ~twos; twos = (twos ^ x) & ~ones; }
        return ones;
    }

    // Single Number III (LC 260): split by the lowest set bit of xor of the two uniques
    static int[] singleNumberIII(int[] a) {
        int x = 0;
        for (int v : a) x ^= v;
        int low = x & -x, p = 0;
        for (int v : a) if ((v & low) != 0) p ^= v;
        return new int[]{p, x ^ p};
    }

    // Number of 1 Bits (LC 191)
    static int hammingWeight(int n) { int c = 0; while (n != 0) { n &= n - 1; c++; } return c; }

    // Counting Bits (LC 338): dp[i] = dp[i >> 1] + (i & 1)
    static int[] countBits(int n) {
        int[] d = new int[n + 1];
        for (int i = 1; i <= n; i++) d[i] = d[i >> 1] + (i & 1);
        return d;
    }

    // Power of Two / Four
    static boolean isPowerOfTwo(int n) { return n > 0 && (n & (n - 1)) == 0; }
    static boolean isPowerOfFour(int n) { return isPowerOfTwo(n) && (n & 0x55555555) != 0; }

    // Reverse Bits (LC 190)
    static int reverseBits(int n) {
        int r = 0;
        for (int i = 0; i < 32; i++) { r = (r << 1) | (n & 1); n >>>= 1; }
        return r;
    }

    // Missing Number (LC 268)
    static int missingNumber(int[] a) {
        int x = a.length;
        for (int i = 0; i < a.length; i++) x ^= i ^ a[i];
        return x;
    }

    // Sum of Two Integers without + (LC 371)
    static int getSum(int a, int b) {
        while (b != 0) { int carry = (a & b) << 1; a ^= b; b = carry; }
        return a;
    }

    // Bitwise AND of Range (LC 201): common prefix of m and n
    static int rangeBitwiseAnd(int m, int n) {
        int shift = 0;
        while (m != n) { m >>= 1; n >>= 1; shift++; }
        return m << shift;
    }

    // Maximum Product of Word Lengths (LC 318): letters as bitmask
    static int maxProduct(String[] words) {
        int n = words.length, best = 0;
        int[] mask = new int[n];
        for (int i = 0; i < n; i++) for (char c : words[i].toCharArray()) mask[i] |= 1 << (c - 'a');
        for (int i = 0; i < n; i++)
            for (int j = i + 1; j < n; j++)
                if ((mask[i] & mask[j]) == 0) best = Math.max(best, words[i].length() * words[j].length());
        return best;
    }

    // Subsets via bitmask enumeration
    static List<List<Integer>> subsetsMask(int[] a) {
        List<List<Integer>> res = new ArrayList<>();
        for (int m = 0; m < 1 << a.length; m++) {
            List<Integer> s = new ArrayList<>();
            for (int i = 0; i < a.length; i++) if ((m >> i & 1) != 0) s.add(a[i]);
            res.add(s);
        }
        return res;
    }

    // ===== NUMBER THEORY =====
    static long gcd(long a, long b) { return b == 0 ? a : gcd(b, a % b); }
    static long lcm(long a, long b) { return a / gcd(a, b) * b; }

    // Fast power mod (LC 50 for doubles, here modular)
    static long powMod(long b, long e, long m) {
        long r = 1;
        b %= m;
        while (e > 0) { if ((e & 1) == 1) r = r * b % m; b = b * b % m; e >>= 1; }
        return r;
    }

    static double myPow(double x, long n) {
        if (n < 0) { x = 1 / x; n = -n; }
        double r = 1;
        while (n > 0) { if ((n & 1) == 1) r *= x; x *= x; n >>= 1; }
        return r;
    }

    // Sieve of Eratosthenes (LC 204): count primes < n
    static int countPrimes(int n) {
        if (n < 3) return 0;
        boolean[] comp = new boolean[n];
        int count = 0;
        for (int i = 2; i < n; i++) {
            if (comp[i]) continue;
            count++;
            for (long j = (long) i * i; j < n; j += i) comp[(int) j] = true;
        }
        return count;
    }

    // Prime factorization by trial division, O(sqrt n)
    static List<Long> factorize(long n) {
        List<Long> f = new ArrayList<>();
        for (long p = 2; p * p <= n; p++) while (n % p == 0) { f.add(p); n /= p; }
        if (n > 1) f.add(n);
        return f;
    }

    // Modular inverse via Fermat (m prime) and nCr mod prime with factorial tables
    static long modInverse(long a, long p) { return powMod(a, p - 2, p); }

    static class Combinatorics {
        final long MOD;
        final long[] fact, inv;
        Combinatorics(int n, long mod) {
            MOD = mod;
            fact = new long[n + 1];
            inv = new long[n + 1];
            fact[0] = 1;
            for (int i = 1; i <= n; i++) fact[i] = fact[i - 1] * i % mod;
            inv[n] = modInverse(fact[n], mod);
            for (int i = n; i > 0; i--) inv[i - 1] = inv[i] * i % mod;
        }
        long nCr(int n, int r) { return r < 0 || r > n ? 0 : fact[n] * inv[r] % MOD * inv[n - r] % MOD; }
    }

    // Integer overflow guards: use long for products, Math.multiplyExact to detect, and compare via division.
    // Reverse Integer (LC 7)
    static int reverseInteger(int x) {
        int r = 0;
        while (x != 0) {
            int d = x % 10;
            if (r > Integer.MAX_VALUE / 10 || (r == Integer.MAX_VALUE / 10 && d > 7)) return 0;
            if (r < Integer.MIN_VALUE / 10 || (r == Integer.MIN_VALUE / 10 && d < -8)) return 0;
            r = r * 10 + d;
            x /= 10;
        }
        return r;
    }

    // Excel Sheet Column Number / Title (LC 168): bijective base 26
    static String columnTitle(int n) {
        StringBuilder sb = new StringBuilder();
        while (n > 0) { n--; sb.append((char) ('A' + n % 26)); n /= 26; }
        return sb.reverse().toString();
    }

    // Fraction to Recurring Decimal (LC 166): remainder -> position map detects the cycle
    static String fractionToDecimal(long num, long den) {
        if (num == 0) return "0";
        StringBuilder sb = new StringBuilder();
        if ((num < 0) ^ (den < 0)) sb.append('-');
        num = Math.abs(num);
        den = Math.abs(den);
        sb.append(num / den);
        long rem = num % den;
        if (rem == 0) return sb.toString();
        sb.append('.');
        Map<Long, Integer> pos = new HashMap<>();
        while (rem != 0) {
            if (pos.containsKey(rem)) { sb.insert(pos.get(rem), "(").append(')'); break; }
            pos.put(rem, sb.length());
            rem *= 10;
            sb.append(rem / den);
            rem %= den;
        }
        return sb.toString();
    }

    // Happy Number (LC 202): Floyd on digit-square-sum
    static boolean isHappy(int n) {
        int slow = n, fast = next(n);
        while (fast != 1 && slow != fast) { slow = next(slow); fast = next(next(fast)); }
        return fast == 1;
    }

    private static int next(int n) { int s = 0; while (n > 0) { int d = n % 10; s += d * d; n /= 10; } return s; }

    // Pow(x, n) with BigInteger-free big arithmetic is in StringAlgorithms (multiply / addStrings).
}

Pitfalls

  1. Use >>> for logical shifts of negatives and 1L << k once k reaches 31.
  2. Check overflow BEFORE multiplying or adding (Reverse Integer), or widen to long.
  3. Fermat inverse needs a PRIME modulus; otherwise use extended Euclid.
37 — DESIGNSTRUCTURES.JAVA

Data-Structure Design

The pattern is almost always a hash map for lookup plus an ordering structure (linked list, TreeMap, heap, array with swap-delete) that makes the second operation cheap. State the complexity of every operation before coding.

PatternUse caseComplexity
LRU / LFUMap + doubly linked list; frequency buckets with minFreqO(1)
Randomized setArray + index map, swap with last on deleteO(1)
FeedsPer-user lists merged with a heapO(k log u)
Limiters / countersDeque of timestamps, token bucket, circular bucketsO(1) amortized
DesignStructures.java
LRU (hand-rolled and LinkedHashMap), LFU, randomized set, max stack, Twitter feed, rate limiter, token bucket, snapshot array, hit counter, peeking iterator, consistent hashing
O(1) 
import java.util.*;

public class DesignStructures {

    // RECOGNIZE: "design a data structure supporting X, Y, Z in O(1) / O(log n)". Pair a hash map with an ordering structure.

    // ---------- LRU Cache (LC 146): hash map + doubly linked list; move to front on touch, evict tail ----------
    static class LRUCache {
        private static class Node { int k, v; Node prev, next; Node(int k, int v) { this.k = k; this.v = v; } }
        private final int cap;
        private final Map<Integer, Node> map = new HashMap<>();
        private final Node head = new Node(0, 0), tail = new Node(0, 0); // sentinels

        LRUCache(int cap) { this.cap = cap; head.next = tail; tail.prev = head; }

        int get(int key) {
            Node n = map.get(key);
            if (n == null) return -1;
            unlink(n);
            addFront(n);
            return n.v;
        }

        void put(int key, int val) {
            Node n = map.get(key);
            if (n != null) { n.v = val; unlink(n); addFront(n); return; }
            if (map.size() == cap) { Node lru = tail.prev; unlink(lru); map.remove(lru.k); }
            n = new Node(key, val);
            map.put(key, n);
            addFront(n);
        }

        private void unlink(Node n) { n.prev.next = n.next; n.next.prev = n.prev; }
        private void addFront(Node n) { n.next = head.next; n.prev = head; head.next.prev = n; head.next = n; }
    }

    // Shortcut: LinkedHashMap with accessOrder = true (state this in an interview, then offer to hand-roll)
    static class LRUCacheLHM extends LinkedHashMap<Integer, Integer> {
        private final int cap;
        LRUCacheLHM(int cap) { super(16, 0.75f, true); this.cap = cap; }
        @Override protected boolean removeEldestEntry(Map.Entry<Integer, Integer> e) { return size() > cap; }
    }

    // ---------- LFU Cache (LC 460): key -> (value, freq), freq -> LinkedHashSet of keys, track minFreq ----------
    static class LFUCache {
        private final int cap;
        private int minFreq;
        private final Map<Integer, Integer> val = new HashMap<>(), freq = new HashMap<>();
        private final Map<Integer, LinkedHashSet<Integer>> buckets = new HashMap<>();

        LFUCache(int cap) { this.cap = cap; }

        int get(int key) {
            if (!val.containsKey(key)) return -1;
            touch(key);
            return val.get(key);
        }

        void put(int key, int v) {
            if (cap == 0) return;
            if (val.containsKey(key)) { val.put(key, v); touch(key); return; }
            if (val.size() == cap) {
                LinkedHashSet<Integer> s = buckets.get(minFreq);
                int evict = s.iterator().next();
                s.remove(evict);
                val.remove(evict);
                freq.remove(evict);
            }
            val.put(key, v);
            freq.put(key, 1);
            buckets.computeIfAbsent(1, f -> new LinkedHashSet<>()).add(key);
            minFreq = 1;
        }

        private void touch(int key) {
            int f = freq.get(key);
            freq.put(key, f + 1);
            buckets.get(f).remove(key);
            if (buckets.get(f).isEmpty() && minFreq == f) minFreq++;
            buckets.computeIfAbsent(f + 1, x -> new LinkedHashSet<>()).add(key);
        }
    }

    // ---------- Insert Delete GetRandom O(1) (LC 380): array + index map, swap-with-last on delete ----------
    static class RandomizedSet {
        private final List<Integer> list = new ArrayList<>();
        private final Map<Integer, Integer> idx = new HashMap<>();
        private final Random rnd = new Random();

        boolean insert(int v) {
            if (idx.containsKey(v)) return false;
            idx.put(v, list.size());
            list.add(v);
            return true;
        }

        boolean remove(int v) {
            Integer i = idx.get(v);
            if (i == null) return false;
            int last = list.get(list.size() - 1);
            list.set(i, last);
            idx.put(last, i);
            list.remove(list.size() - 1);
            idx.remove(v);
            return true;
        }

        int getRandom() { return list.get(rnd.nextInt(list.size())); }
    }

    // ---------- Min Stack is in MonotonicStructures. Max Stack (LC 716): TreeMap of value -> stack of ids ----------
    static class MaxStack {
        private final TreeMap<Integer, Deque<Integer>> byVal = new TreeMap<>();
        private final Map<Integer, Integer> idVal = new HashMap<>();
        private final TreeSet<Integer> ids = new TreeSet<>(); // ids as stack order
        private int next = 0;

        void push(int x) {
            int id = next++;
            idVal.put(id, x);
            ids.add(id);
            byVal.computeIfAbsent(x, k -> new ArrayDeque<>()).push(id);
        }
        int pop() { int id = ids.last(); return remove(id); }
        int top() { return idVal.get(ids.last()); }
        int peekMax() { return byVal.lastKey(); }
        int popMax() { return remove(byVal.lastEntry().getValue().peek()); }

        private int remove(int id) {
            int x = idVal.remove(id);
            ids.remove(id);
            Deque<Integer> d = byVal.get(x);
            d.remove(id);
            if (d.isEmpty()) byVal.remove(x);
            return x;
        }
    }

    // ---------- Design Twitter / news feed (LC 355): per-user tweet lists + k-way merge heap ----------
    static class Twitter {
        private int time = 0;
        private final Map<Integer, List<int[]>> tweets = new HashMap<>(); // {time, id}
        private final Map<Integer, Set<Integer>> follows = new HashMap<>();

        void postTweet(int u, int id) { tweets.computeIfAbsent(u, k -> new ArrayList<>()).add(new int[]{time++, id}); }

        List<Integer> getNewsFeed(int u) {
            PriorityQueue<int[]> pq = new PriorityQueue<>((a, b) -> b[0] - a[0]); // {time, id, user, idxInUserList}
            Set<Integer> users = new HashSet<>(follows.getOrDefault(u, Set.of()));
            users.add(u);
            for (int v : users) {
                List<int[]> l = tweets.get(v);
                if (l != null && !l.isEmpty()) pq.add(new int[]{l.get(l.size() - 1)[0], l.get(l.size() - 1)[1], v, l.size() - 1});
            }
            List<Integer> feed = new ArrayList<>();
            while (!pq.isEmpty() && feed.size() < 10) {
                int[] t = pq.poll();
                feed.add(t[1]);
                if (t[3] > 0) {
                    int[] prev = tweets.get(t[2]).get(t[3] - 1);
                    pq.add(new int[]{prev[0], prev[1], t[2], t[3] - 1});
                }
            }
            return feed;
        }

        void follow(int a, int b) { follows.computeIfAbsent(a, k -> new HashSet<>()).add(b); }
        void unfollow(int a, int b) { Set<Integer> s = follows.get(a); if (s != null) s.remove(b); }
    }

    // ---------- Sliding-window rate limiter (log-based): O(1) amortized with a deque of timestamps ----------
    static class RateLimiter {
        private final int limit;
        private final long windowMs;
        private final Deque<Long> times = new ArrayDeque<>();
        RateLimiter(int limit, long windowMs) { this.limit = limit; this.windowMs = windowMs; }
        boolean allow(long now) {
            while (!times.isEmpty() && times.peekFirst() <= now - windowMs) times.pollFirst();
            if (times.size() >= limit) return false;
            times.addLast(now);
            return true;
        }
    }

    // Token bucket variant: refill continuously, O(1) state
    static class TokenBucket {
        private final double capacity, perMs;
        private double tokens;
        private long last;
        TokenBucket(double capacity, double perSecond, long now) { this.capacity = capacity; this.perMs = perSecond / 1000.0; this.tokens = capacity; this.last = now; }
        boolean allow(long now) {
            tokens = Math.min(capacity, tokens + (now - last) * perMs);
            last = now;
            if (tokens < 1) return false;
            tokens -= 1;
            return true;
        }
    }

    // ---------- Snapshot Array (LC 1146): per-index history of (snapId, value), binary search on read ----------
    static class SnapshotArray {
        private final List<int[]>[] hist;
        private int snap = 0;
        @SuppressWarnings("unchecked")
        SnapshotArray(int n) {
            hist = new List[n];
            for (int i = 0; i < n; i++) { hist[i] = new ArrayList<>(); hist[i].add(new int[]{0, 0}); }
        }
        void set(int i, int v) {
            List<int[]> h = hist[i];
            if (h.get(h.size() - 1)[0] == snap) h.get(h.size() - 1)[1] = v; else h.add(new int[]{snap, v});
        }
        int snap() { return snap++; }
        int get(int i, int snapId) {
            List<int[]> h = hist[i];
            int lo = 0, hi = h.size();
            while (lo < hi) { int mid = (lo + hi) >>> 1; if (h.get(mid)[0] > snapId) hi = mid; else lo = mid + 1; }
            return h.get(lo - 1)[1];
        }
    }

    // ---------- Design Hit Counter (LC 362) with circular buckets of 300 seconds ----------
    static class HitCounter {
        private final int[] times = new int[300], hits = new int[300];
        void hit(int t) {
            int i = t % 300;
            if (times[i] != t) { times[i] = t; hits[i] = 1; } else hits[i]++;
        }
        int getHits(int t) {
            int total = 0;
            for (int i = 0; i < 300; i++) if (t - times[i] < 300) total += hits[i];
            return total;
        }
    }

    // ---------- Iterator designs: Flatten Nested List (LC 341) via stack of iterators; Peeking Iterator (LC 284) ----------
    static class PeekingIterator implements Iterator<Integer> {
        private final Iterator<Integer> it;
        private Integer buffered;
        PeekingIterator(Iterator<Integer> it) { this.it = it; if (it.hasNext()) buffered = it.next(); }
        Integer peek() { return buffered; }
        @Override public Integer next() { Integer r = buffered; buffered = it.hasNext() ? it.next() : null; return r; }
        @Override public boolean hasNext() { return buffered != null; }
    }

    // ---------- Autocomplete / Trie-backed designs live in the trees topic (Trie.java). ----------
    // ---------- Consistent hashing ring (interview favorite): TreeMap of hash -> node, clockwise successor ----------
    static class ConsistentHash {
        private final TreeMap<Integer, String> ring = new TreeMap<>();
        private final int replicas;
        ConsistentHash(int replicas) { this.replicas = replicas; }
        void addNode(String node) { for (int i = 0; i < replicas; i++) ring.put((node + "#" + i).hashCode() & 0x7fffffff, node); }
        void removeNode(String node) { for (int i = 0; i < replicas; i++) ring.remove((node + "#" + i).hashCode() & 0x7fffffff); }
        String route(String key) {
            if (ring.isEmpty()) return null;
            Map.Entry<Integer, String> e = ring.ceilingEntry(key.hashCode() & 0x7fffffff);
            return (e != null ? e : ring.firstEntry()).getValue();
        }
    }
}

Pitfalls

  1. LRU: use sentinel head and tail nodes so no operation needs null checks.
  2. LFU: when the min-frequency bucket empties during a touch, increment minFreq; on insert, reset it to 1.
  3. Say aloud which operations are O(1) and which are O(log n) and why.
